=== FILE: D:\attestation\schemas\CHANGELOG.md ===
# Schema Version History

## v0.1.0 (Phase 0 Freeze)
- **`attestation-predicate.schema.json`** (`https://schemas.proofofdata.dev/attestation/v0.1`):
  - Ratified Phase 0 schema decisions:
    1. Cryptographic signatures live strictly in the outer DSSE envelope (`signatures[]`), never inside the predicate.
    2. Predicate-type URI anchored under `https://schemas.proofofdata.dev/attestation/v0.1`.
    3. Named clearance levels (`Public`, `Internal`, `Confidential`, `Restricted`) aligned 1:1 with the four classification tiers.
    4. Seven field groups (`identity`, `ownership`, `provenance`, `integrity`, `classification`, `handling`, `lifecycle`) with seven mandatory fields (`identity.locator`, `ownership.owner`, `provenance.attested_by`, `integrity.content_sha256`, `classification.tier`, `handling.policy`, `lifecycle.expires_at`).
    5. Tier-bounded maximum TTLs enforced (`Restricted`: 30 days, `Confidential`: 90 days, `Internal`: 180 days, `Public`: 365 days).
- **`snapshot-predicate.schema.json`** (`https://schemas.proofofdata.dev/snapshot/v0.1`):
  - Short-TTL DSSE-signed snapshot carrying monotonic `sequence_number` and active + `expired`/`revoked` tombstones for zero-DB request-path enforcement.
- **`dsse-envelope.schema.json`** (`https://schemas.proofofdata.dev/dsse-envelope/v0.1`):
  - Standard DSSE v1 envelope (`application/vnd.in-toto+json`) with Sigstore v0.3 local verification material metadata.


=== FILE: D:\attestation\registry\__init__.py ===
"""POD (Proof Of Data) Registry, Signing Toolkit, and Lifecycle Management."""

__version__ = "0.1.0"


=== FILE: D:\attestation\registry\schema_validator.py ===
"""Schema validation and tier/TTL policy invariants for POD attestation records and snapshots."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import jsonschema

SCHEMAS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "schemas"

ATTESTATION_PREDICATE_TYPE = "https://schemas.proofofdata.dev/attestation/v0.1"
SNAPSHOT_PREDICATE_TYPE = "https://schemas.proofofdata.dev/snapshot/v0.1"
INTOTO_STATEMENT_TYPE = "https://in-toto.io/Statement/v1"
DSSE_PAYLOAD_TYPE = "application/vnd.in-toto+json"

TIER_ORDER: dict[str, int] = {
    "Public": 0,
    "Internal": 1,
    "Confidential": 2,
    "Restricted": 3,
}

CLEARANCE_ORDER: dict[str, int] = {
    "Public": 0,
    "Internal": 1,
    "Confidential": 2,
    "Restricted": 3,
}

EGRESS_ORDER: dict[str, int] = {
    "local_only": 0,
    "private_cloud": 1,
    "public_api": 2,
}

TIER_MAX_TTL_DAYS: dict[str, int] = {
    "Restricted": 30,
    "Confidential": 90,
    "Internal": 180,
    "Public": 365,
}

DEFAULT_HANDLING_BY_TIER: dict[str, dict[str, str]] = {
    "Public": {
        "ai_eligibility": "allow",
        "minimum_clearance": "Public",
        "egress_class": "public_api",
    },
    "Internal": {
        "ai_eligibility": "allow",
        "minimum_clearance": "Internal",
        "egress_class": "private_cloud",
    },
    "Confidential": {
        "ai_eligibility": "strip",
        "minimum_clearance": "Confidential",
        "egress_class": "local_only",
    },
    "Restricted": {
        "ai_eligibility": "deny",
        "minimum_clearance": "Restricted",
        "egress_class": "local_only",
    },
}


class SchemaValidationError(ValueError):
    """Raised when an attestation predicate, statement, or snapshot violates the schema contract."""


def _load_schema(filename: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    path = SCHEMAS_DIR / filename
    with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return json.load(f)


ATTESTATION_SCHEMA = _load_schema("attestation-predicate.schema.json")
SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA = _load_schema("snapshot-predicate.schema.json")
DSSE_SCHEMA = _load_schema("dsse-envelope.schema.json")


def parse_iso8601(ts: str) -> datetime:
    """Parse an ISO-8601 timestamp into a timezone-aware UTC datetime."""
    if ts.endswith("Z"):
        ts = ts[:-1] + "+00:00"
    dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
    if dt.tzinfo is None:
        dt = dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    return dt.astimezone(timezone.utc)


def format_iso8601(dt: datetime) -> str:
    """Format a datetime as an ISO-8601 UTC string with Z suffix."""
    return dt.astimezone(timezone.utc).replace(microsecond=0).isoformat().replace("+00:00", "Z")


def validate_attestation_predicate(predicate: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """Validate an attestation predicate against JSON Schema and semantic tier/TTL invariants."""
    if "signature" in predicate or "signatures" in predicate:
        raise SchemaValidationError(
            "Phase 0 invariant violation: cryptographic signature must live in the outer DSSE envelope, not inside the predicate."
        )

    try:
        jsonschema.validate(instance=predicate, schema=ATTESTATION_SCHEMA)
    except jsonschema.ValidationError as exc:
        raise SchemaValidationError(f"Predicate schema validation failed at {list(exc.path)}: {exc.message}") from exc

    # Verify the 7 mandatory fields are non-empty and semantically valid
    locator = predicate["identity"]["locator"]
    owner = predicate["ownership"]["owner"]
    attested_by = predicate["provenance"]["attested_by"]
    content_sha256 = predicate["integrity"]["content_sha256"]
    tier = predicate["classification"]["tier"]
    policy = predicate["handling"]["policy"]
    expires_at_str = predicate["lifecycle"]["expires_at"]

    if not all([locator, owner, attested_by, content_sha256, tier, policy, expires_at_str]):
        raise SchemaValidationError("One or more of the 7 mandatory fields are empty.")

    # Verify sensitive_categories never leak raw values (only category, count, max_confidence, column_or_field)
    for item in predicate["classification"].get("sensitive_categories", []):
        if "value" in item or "sample" in item or "raw" in item:
            raise SchemaValidationError("Classification findings must record types and counts only, never raw values.")

    # Verify minimum clearance is at least as strict as the classification tier
    min_clearance = policy["minimum_clearance"]
    if CLEARANCE_ORDER[min_clearance] < TIER_ORDER[tier]:
        raise SchemaValidationError(
            f"handling.policy.minimum_clearance ({min_clearance}) cannot be lower than classification.tier ({tier})."
        )

    # Verify expiry timestamp and max TTL by tier
    expires_at = parse_iso8601(expires_at_str)
    issued_at_str = predicate["lifecycle"].get("issued_at")
    if issued_at_str:
        issued_at = parse_iso8601(issued_at_str)
        if expires_at <= issued_at:
            raise SchemaValidationError("lifecycle.expires_at must be strictly after lifecycle.issued_at.")
        max_days = TIER_MAX_TTL_DAYS[tier]
        delta_seconds = (expires_at - issued_at).total_seconds()
        # Allow up to 60 seconds clock skew tolerance on max_days
        if delta_seconds > (max_days * 86400) + 60:
            raise SchemaValidationError(
                f"Tier '{tier}' records have a maximum TTL of {max_days} days, but record expires in {delta_seconds / 86400:.2f} days."
            )

    # Verify dual-approval invariant if status is tier_lowered
    if predicate["lifecycle"].get("status") == "tier_lowered":
        approvals = predicate["lifecycle"].get("tier_lower_approvals", [])
        if len(approvals) < 2:
            raise SchemaValidationError("Lowering a classification tier requires dual approval (at least 2 approvals).")
        approvers = {a["approver_id"] for a in approvals}
        if len(approvers) < 2:
            raise SchemaValidationError("Dual approval requires two distinct approver_id principals.")


def validate_intoto_statement(statement: dict[str, Any], expected_predicate_type: str = ATTESTATION_PREDICATE_TYPE) -> None:
    """Validate an in-toto v1 Statement and its inner predicate."""
    if not isinstance(statement, dict):
        raise SchemaValidationError("in-toto Statement must be a JSON object.")
    if statement.get("_type") != INTOTO_STATEMENT_TYPE:
        raise SchemaValidationError(f"Expected _type '{INTOTO_STATEMENT_TYPE}', got '{statement.get('_type')}'.")
    if statement.get("predicateType") != expected_predicate_type:
        raise SchemaValidationError(
            f"Expected predicateType '{expected_predicate_type}', got '{statement.get('predicateType')}'."
        )

    subjects = statement.get("subject")
    if not isinstance(subjects, list) or len(subjects) == 0:
        raise SchemaValidationError("in-toto Statement requires a non-empty 'subject' list.")

    subject = subjects[0]
    if not isinstance(subject, dict) or "name" not in subject or "digest" not in subject:
        raise SchemaValidationError("in-toto subject[0] must contain 'name' and 'digest'.")
    sha256_digest = subject.get("digest", {}).get("sha256")
    if not isinstance(sha256_digest, str) or not re.fullmatch(r"[a-f0-9]{64}", sha256_digest):
        raise SchemaValidationError("in-toto subject[0].digest.sha256 must be a 64-character lowercase hex SHA-256 string.")

    predicate = statement.get("predicate")
    if not isinstance(predicate, dict):
        raise SchemaValidationError("in-toto Statement must contain a 'predicate' object.")

    if expected_predicate_type == ATTESTATION_PREDICATE_TYPE:
        validate_attestation_predicate(predicate)
        if subject["name"] != predicate["identity"]["locator"]:
            raise SchemaValidationError(
                f"in-toto subject[0].name ('{subject['name']}') does not match predicate.identity.locator ('{predicate['identity']['locator']}')."
            )
        if sha256_digest != predicate["integrity"]["content_sha256"]:
            raise SchemaValidationError(
                "in-toto subject[0].digest.sha256 does not match predicate.integrity.content_sha256."
            )
    elif expected_predicate_type == SNAPSHOT_PREDICATE_TYPE:
        validate_snapshot_predicate(predicate)


def validate_snapshot_predicate(predicate: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """Validate a snapshot predicate against `schemas/snapshot-predicate.schema.json`."""
    try:
        jsonschema.validate(instance=predicate, schema=SNAPSHOT_SCHEMA)
    except jsonschema.ValidationError as exc:
        raise SchemaValidationError(f"Snapshot schema validation failed at {list(exc.path)}: {exc.message}") from exc

    issued_at = parse_iso8601(predicate["issued_at"])
    expires_at = parse_iso8601(predicate["expires_at"])
    if expires_at <= issued_at:
        raise SchemaValidationError("Snapshot expires_at must be strictly after issued_at.")


def validate_dsse_envelope_structure(envelope: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """Validate outer DSSE envelope JSON structure."""
    try:
        jsonschema.validate(instance=envelope, schema=DSSE_SCHEMA)
    except jsonschema.ValidationError as exc:
        raise SchemaValidationError(f"DSSE envelope validation failed at {list(exc.path)}: {exc.message}") from exc


=== FILE: D:\attestation\registry\signing.py ===
"""Local key management, DSSE v1 signing, and Sigstore-compatible verification toolkit."""

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
import unicodedata
import uuid
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from cryptography.exceptions import InvalidSignature
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives import hashes, serialization
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric import ec, ed25519

from registry.schema_validator import (
    ATTESTATION_PREDICATE_TYPE,
     DEFAULT_HANDLING_BY_TIER,
    DSSE_PAYLOAD_TYPE,
    INTOTO_STATEMENT_TYPE,
    SNAPSHOT_PREDICATE_TYPE,
    TIER_MAX_TTL_DAYS,
    format_iso8601,
    parse_iso8601,
    validate_dsse_envelope_structure,
    validate_intoto_statement,
)


class SignatureVerificationError(ValueError):
    """Raised when DSSE signature or content integrity verification fails."""


def compute_bytes_sha256(data: bytes) -> str:
    """Return lowercase 64-character hex SHA-256 digest of raw bytes."""
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()


def normalize_canonical_text(text: str) -> bytes:
    """Canonical text normalization: UTF-8 NFC, strip \\r, collapse consecutive whitespace/newlines into a single space, .strip()."""
    nfc = unicodedata.normalize("NFC", text or "")
    no_cr = nfc.replace("\r", "")
    collapsed = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", no_cr).strip()
    return collapsed.encode("utf-8")


def compute_extracted_text_and_chunk_hashes(text: str) -> tuple[str, list[str]]:
    """Compute canonical `extracted_text_sha256` and deterministic `chunk_hashes` for paragraphs, rows, lines, and sliding 500-char windows."""
    norm_full = normalize_canonical_text(text)
    extracted_text_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(norm_full).hexdigest()

    seen: dict[str, None] = {}

    def _add_chunk(candidate: str) -> None:
        norm_bytes = normalize_canonical_text(candidate)
        if norm_bytes:
            seen[hashlib.sha256(norm_bytes).hexdigest()] = None
        raw_bytes = candidate.encode("utf-8")
        if raw_bytes:
            seen[hashlib.sha256(raw_bytes).hexdigest()] = None

    if text:
        _add_chunk(text)
        no_cr = unicodedata.normalize("NFC", text).replace("\r", "")
        # 1. Paragraph boundaries
        for para in re.split(r"\n\s*\n", no_cr):
            para_s = para.strip()
            if not para_s:
                continue
            _add_chunk(para_s)
            # Sliding 500-character windows if paragraph exceeds 500 chars
            if len(para_s) > 500:
                for start in range(0, len(para_s), 250):
                    window = para_s[start : start + 500]
                    if window.strip():
                        _add_chunk(window)
                    if start + 500 >= len(para_s):
                        break

        # 2. Row / line boundaries
        lines = [ln.strip() for ln in no_cr.splitlines() if ln.strip()]
        for ln in lines:
            _add_chunk(ln)
            # Strip markdown heading prefix (#, ##, etc.) so heading text matches cleanly
            if ln.startswith("#"):
                heading_clean = ln.lstrip("#").strip()
                if heading_clean:
                    _add_chunk(heading_clean)
            # For pipe-delimited spreadsheet/CSV rows, include contiguous cell slices
            if " | " in ln:
                cells = [c.strip() for c in ln.split(" | ") if c.strip()]
                n_cells = len(cells)
                if 1 < n_cells <= 12:
                    for i in range(n_cells):
                        for j in range(i + 1, n_cells + 1):
                            _add_chunk(" | ".join(cells[i:j]))
            if len(ln) > 500:
                for start in range(0, len(ln), 250):
                    window = ln[start : start + 500]
                    if window.strip():
                        _add_chunk(window)
                    if start + 500 >= len(ln):
                        break

        # 3. Contiguous multi-line spans (up to 6 consecutive lines)
        if 1 < len(lines) <= 50:
            for i in range(len(lines)):
                for span_len in range(2, min(7, len(lines) - i + 1)):
                    _add_chunk("\n".join(lines[i : i + span_len]))

    return extracted_text_sha256, list(seen.keys())


def compute_file_sha256(path: str | Path) -> str:
    """Return lowercase 64-character hex SHA-256 digest of a file on disk."""
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with Path(path).open("rb") as f:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(65536), b""):
            h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest()


def dsse_pae(payload_type: str, payload_bytes: bytes) -> bytes:
    """Compute DSSE v1 Pre-Authentication Encoding (PAE) per the DSSE specification.

    PAE(type, body) = "DSSEv1" + SP + LEN(type) + SP + type + SP + LEN(body) + SP + body
    """
    pt_bytes = payload_type.encode("utf-8")
    prefix = f"DSSEv1 {len(pt_bytes)} ".encode("utf-8") + pt_bytes + f" {len(payload_bytes)} ".encode("utf-8")
    return prefix + payload_bytes


def compute_spki_keyid(public_key: ed25519.Ed25519PublicKey | ec.EllipticCurvePublicKey) -> str:
    """Compute deterministic keyid from SHA-256 of SubjectPublicKeyInfo (DER)."""
    spki_der = public_key.public_bytes(
        encoding=serialization.Encoding.DER,
        format=serialization.PublicFormat.SubjectPublicKeyInfo,
    )
    return hashlib.sha256(spki_der).hexdigest()[:16]


@dataclass
class VerificationResult:
    """Structured result returned when verifying a DSSE attestation or snapshot envelope."""

    verified: bool
    keyid: str
    algorithm: str
    statement: dict[str, Any]
    predicate_type: str
    subject_name: str
    subject_sha256: str


class Keyring:
    """In-boundary operator keyring supporting privilege-separated private signing keys and public verification keys."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        key_dir: str | Path | None = None,
        *,
        private_key_dir: str | Path | None = None,
        public_key_dir: str | Path | None = None,
        verification_only: bool = False,
    ) -> None:
        default_base = Path(key_dir or os.environ.get("POD_KEY_DIR", "./.pod_keys"))
        if public_key_dir is not None:
            self.public_key_dir = Path(public_key_dir)
        elif key_dir is None and os.environ.get("POD_PUBLIC_KEY_DIR"):
            self.public_key_dir = Path(os.environ["POD_PUBLIC_KEY_DIR"])
        else:
            self.public_key_dir = default_base

        self.verification_only = verification_only
        if verification_only:
            self.private_key_dir: Path | None = None
        elif private_key_dir is not None:
            self.private_key_dir = Path(private_key_dir)
        elif key_dir is None and os.environ.get("POD_PRIVATE_KEY_DIR"):
            self.private_key_dir = Path(os.environ["POD_PRIVATE_KEY_DIR"])
        else:
            self.private_key_dir = default_base

        # Maintain `self.key_dir` alias pointing to public_key_dir for backward compatibility
        self.key_dir = self.public_key_dir
        self.keyring_file = self.public_key_dir / "keyring.json"

        if not self.verification_only:
            self.public_key_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            if self.private_key_dir is not None:
                self.private_key_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            if not self.keyring_file.exists():
                self._save_data({"active_keyid": None, "active_keys_by_purpose": {}, "keys": {}})
        else:
            self.assert_no_private_keys_exposed()

    def assert_no_private_keys_exposed(self) -> None:
        """Fail closed if a verification-only or split-directory consumer detects any `*.priv.pem` file in its public key directory."""
        is_split_or_verifier = self.verification_only or (
            self.private_key_dir is not None and self.private_key_dir.resolve() != self.public_key_dir.resolve()
        )
        if is_split_or_verifier and self.public_key_dir.exists():
            leaked_priv = list(self.public_key_dir.glob("*.priv.pem"))
            if leaked_priv:
                raise SignatureVerificationError(
                    f"Cryptographic privilege separation violation: private key file(s) {[p.name for p in leaked_priv]} "
                    f"detected in verification-only public key directory '{self.public_key_dir}'."
                )

    def _load_data(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if not self.keyring_file.exists():
            return {"active_keyid": None, "active_keys_by_purpose": {}, "keys": {}}
        with self.keyring_file.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            return json.load(f)

    def _save_data(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        if self.verification_only:
            raise SignatureVerificationError("Verification-only Keyring cannot modify keyring.json.")
        self.public_key_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with self.keyring_file.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(data, f, indent=2, sort_keys=True)

    def generate_key(
        self,
        algorithm: str = "Ed25519",
        operator_id: str = "pod-operator",
        activate: bool = True,
        key_purpose: list[str] | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Generate a new local keypair (Ed25519 or ECDSA_P256_SHA256) and register public metadata in the keyring."""
        if self.verification_only or self.private_key_dir is None:
            raise SignatureVerificationError("Verification-only Keyring cannot generate private signing keys.")

        if algorithm == "Ed25519":
            private_key = ed25519.Ed25519PrivateKey.generate()
        elif algorithm == "ECDSA_P256_SHA256":
            private_key = ec.generate_private_key(ec.SECP256R1())
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"Unsupported algorithm: {algorithm}")

        public_key = private_key.public_key()
        keyid = compute_spki_keyid(public_key)
        now_str = format_iso8601(datetime.now(timezone.utc))
        purposes = list(key_purpose or ["attestation", "snapshot"])

        priv_pem = private_key.private_bytes(
            encoding=serialization.Encoding.PEM,
            format=serialization.PrivateFormat.PKCS8,
            encryption_algorithm=serialization.NoEncryption(),
        ).decode("utf-8")
        pub_pem = public_key.public_bytes(
            encoding=serialization.Encoding.PEM,
            format=serialization.PublicFormat.SubjectPublicKeyInfo,
        ).decode("utf-8")

        self.private_key_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.public_key_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        priv_path = self.private_key_dir / f"{keyid}.priv.pem"
        pub_path = self.public_key_dir / f"{keyid}.pub.pem"
        priv_path.write_text(priv_pem, encoding="utf-8")
        pub_path.write_text(pub_pem, encoding="utf-8")

        data = self._load_data()
        data.setdefault("active_keys_by_purpose", {})
        if activate and data.get("active_keyid"):
            old_id = data["active_keyid"]
            if old_id in data["keys"] and data["keys"][old_id]["status"] == "active":
                old_purposes = set(data["keys"][old_id].get("key_purpose", ["attestation", "snapshot"]))
                if old_purposes.issubset(set(purposes)):
                    data["keys"][old_id]["status"] = "rotated"
                    data["keys"][old_id]["rotated_at"] = now_str

        entry = {
            "keyid": keyid,
            "algorithm": algorithm,
            "operator_id": operator_id,
            "key_purpose": purposes,
            "status": "active" if activate else "standby",
            "created_at": now_str,
            "rotated_at": None,
            "revoked_at": None,
            "public_key_pem": pub_pem,
            "private_key_file": priv_path.name,
        }
        data["keys"][keyid] = entry
        if activate:
            data["active_keyid"] = keyid
            for p in purposes:
                data["active_keys_by_purpose"][p] = keyid
        self._save_data(data)
        return entry

    def rotate_key(
        self,
        algorithm: str = "Ed25519",
        operator_id: str = "pod-operator",
        key_purpose: list[str] | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Rotate the active signing key; previously active key moves to 'rotated' state for historical verification."""
        return self.generate_key(algorithm=algorithm, operator_id=operator_id, activate=True, key_purpose=key_purpose)

    def revoke_key(self, keyid: str) -> None:
        """Mark a key as compromised/revoked; any signature made by a revoked key fails verification immediately."""
        data = self._load_data()
        if keyid not in data["keys"]:
            raise KeyError(f"Unknown keyid: {keyid}")
        now_str = format_iso8601(datetime.now(timezone.utc))
        data["keys"][keyid]["status"] = "revoked"
        data["keys"][keyid]["revoked_at"] = now_str
        if data.get("active_keyid") == keyid:
            data["active_keyid"] = None
        for p, k_id in list(data.get("active_keys_by_purpose", {}).items()):
            if k_id == keyid:
                data["active_keys_by_purpose"][p] = None
        self._save_data(data)

    def ensure_active_key(
        self,
        purpose: str | None = None,
        operator_id: str = "pod-operator",
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Return the active key metadata (optionally scoped to `purpose`), generating an Ed25519 key if none exists."""
        data = self._load_data()
        if purpose:
            by_purpose = data.get("active_keys_by_purpose", {}).get(purpose)
            if by_purpose and by_purpose in data["keys"] and data["keys"][by_purpose]["status"] == "active":
                return data["keys"][by_purpose]
            for k_meta in data["keys"].values():
                if k_meta.get("status") == "active" and purpose in k_meta.get("key_purpose", ["attestation", "snapshot"]):
                    priv_file = (self.private_key_dir / k_meta["private_key_file"]) if self.private_key_dir else None
                    if priv_file and priv_file.exists():
                        return k_meta
            return self.generate_key(algorithm="Ed25519", operator_id=operator_id, activate=True, key_purpose=[purpose])

        active_id = data.get("active_keyid")
        if active_id and active_id in data["keys"] and data["keys"][active_id]["status"] == "active":
            priv_file = (self.private_key_dir / data["keys"][active_id]["private_key_file"]) if self.private_key_dir else None
            if priv_file is None or priv_file.exists():
                return data["keys"][active_id]
        return self.generate_key(algorithm="Ed25519", operator_id=operator_id)

    def get_key_metadata(self, keyid: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if self.verification_only:
            self.assert_no_private_keys_exposed()
        data = self._load_data()
        if keyid not in data["keys"]:
            raise SignatureVerificationError(f"Key ID '{keyid}' is not present in the local operator keyring.")
        return data["keys"][keyid]

    def list_keys(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        data = self._load_data()
        return list(data["keys"].values())

    def load_private_key(self, keyid: str | None = None, purpose: str | None = None) -> tuple[str, str, Any]:
        if self.verification_only or self.private_key_dir is None:
            raise SignatureVerificationError("Cannot load private key from verification-only Keyring.")
        data = self._load_data()
        target_id = keyid
        if not target_id and purpose:
            target_id = data.get("active_keys_by_purpose", {}).get(purpose)
        if not target_id:
            target_id = data.get("active_keyid")
        if not target_id or target_id not in data["keys"]:
            meta = self.ensure_active_key(purpose=purpose)
            target_id = meta["keyid"]
        else:
            meta = data["keys"][target_id]
            priv_candidate = self.private_key_dir / meta.get("private_key_file", f"{target_id}.priv.pem")
            if not priv_candidate.exists():
                meta = self.ensure_active_key(purpose=purpose)
                target_id = meta["keyid"]

        if meta["status"] != "active":
            raise SignatureVerificationError(f"Cannot sign with non-active key '{target_id}' (status={meta['status']}).")

        priv_path = self.private_key_dir / meta.get("private_key_file", f"{target_id}.priv.pem")
        priv_bytes = priv_path.read_bytes()
        private_key = serialization.load_pem_private_key(priv_bytes, password=None)
        return target_id, meta["algorithm"], private_key

    def load_public_key(self, keyid: str) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], Any]:
        if self.verification_only:
            self.assert_no_private_keys_exposed()
        meta = self.get_key_metadata(keyid)
        pub_pem_str = meta.get("public_key_pem")
        if not pub_pem_str:
            pub_file = self.public_key_dir / f"{keyid}.pub.pem"
            pub_pem_str = pub_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        public_key = serialization.load_pem_public_key(pub_pem_str.encode("utf-8"))
        return meta, public_key

    def verify_dsse(
        self,
        envelope: dict[str, Any],
        expected_predicate_type: str | None = None,
        expected_content_sha256: str | None = None,
        check_expiry: bool = True,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> VerificationResult:
        """Verify a DSSE envelope using only the public verification keyring."""
        self.assert_no_private_keys_exposed()
        return verify_dsse_envelope(
            envelope=envelope,
            keyring=self,
            expected_predicate_type=expected_predicate_type,
            expected_content_sha256=expected_content_sha256,
            check_expiry=check_expiry,
            now=now,
        )


def sign_statement(
    statement: dict[str, Any],
    keyring: Keyring,
    keyid: str | None = None,
    purpose: str | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Validate an in-toto Statement and wrap it in a signed DSSE v1 envelope with Sigstore local bundle metadata."""
    predicate_type = statement.get("predicateType", ATTESTATION_PREDICATE_TYPE)
    validate_intoto_statement(statement, expected_predicate_type=predicate_type)

    canonical_payload = json.dumps(statement, separators=(",", ":"), sort_keys=True).encode("utf-8")
    payload_b64 = base64.b64encode(canonical_payload).decode("ascii")
    pae_bytes = dsse_pae(DSSE_PAYLOAD_TYPE, canonical_payload)

    inferred_purpose = purpose or ("snapshot" if predicate_type == SNAPSHOT_PREDICATE_TYPE else "attestation")
    signing_keyid, algorithm, private_key = keyring.load_private_key(keyid, purpose=inferred_purpose)
    if algorithm == "Ed25519":
        sig_bytes = private_key.sign(pae_bytes)
    elif algorithm == "ECDSA_P256_SHA256":
        sig_bytes = private_key.sign(pae_bytes, ec.ECDSA(hashes.SHA256()))
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported key algorithm: {algorithm}")

    sig_b64 = base64.b64encode(sig_bytes).decode("ascii")

    envelope = {
        "payloadType": DSSE_PAYLOAD_TYPE,
        "payload": payload_b64,
        "signatures": [
            {
                "keyid": signing_keyid,
                "sig": sig_b64,
            }
        ],
        "sigstoreBundle": {
            "mediaType": "application/vnd.dev.sigstore.bundle.v0.3+json",
            "verificationMaterial": {
                "publicKey": {
                    "hint": signing_keyid,
                    "algorithm": algorithm,
                }
            },
        },
    }
    validate_dsse_envelope_structure(envelope)
    return envelope


def verify_dsse_envelope(
    envelope: dict[str, Any],
    keyring: Keyring,
    expected_predicate_type: str | None = None,
    expected_content_sha256: str | None = None,
    check_expiry: bool = True,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> VerificationResult:
    """Verify DSSE envelope signature, key validity window, in-toto statement schema, and optional content hash."""
    try:
        validate_dsse_envelope_structure(envelope)
    except Exception as exc:
        raise SignatureVerificationError(f"Malformed DSSE envelope: {exc}") from exc

    try:
        payload_bytes = base64.b64decode(envelope["payload"], validate=True)
        statement = json.loads(payload_bytes.decode("utf-8"))
    except Exception as exc:
        raise SignatureVerificationError(f"Failed to decode DSSE payload: {exc}") from exc

    actual_predicate_type = statement.get("predicateType")
    target_predicate_type = expected_predicate_type or actual_predicate_type
    if target_predicate_type not in (ATTESTATION_PREDICATE_TYPE, SNAPSHOT_PREDICATE_TYPE):
        raise SignatureVerificationError(f"Unsupported predicateType: {target_predicate_type}")

    try:
        validate_intoto_statement(statement, expected_predicate_type=target_predicate_type)
    except Exception as exc:
        raise SignatureVerificationError(f"Statement validation failed: {exc}") from exc

    pae_bytes = dsse_pae(envelope["payloadType"], payload_bytes)
    sig_entry = envelope["signatures"][0]
    keyid = sig_entry["keyid"]
    try:
        sig_bytes = base64.b64decode(sig_entry["sig"], validate=True)
    except Exception as exc:
        raise SignatureVerificationError("Invalid base64 signature encoding.") from exc

    meta, public_key = keyring.load_public_key(keyid)
    if meta["status"] == "revoked":
        raise SignatureVerificationError(f"Signing key '{keyid}' has been revoked.")

    algorithm = meta["algorithm"]
    try:
        if algorithm == "Ed25519":
            public_key.verify(sig_bytes, pae_bytes)
        elif algorithm == "ECDSA_P256_SHA256":
            public_key.verify(sig_bytes, pae_bytes, ec.ECDSA(hashes.SHA256()))
        else:
            raise SignatureVerificationError(f"Unsupported algorithm: {algorithm}")
    except InvalidSignature as exc:
        raise SignatureVerificationError("Cryptographic DSSE signature verification failed (payload or signature tampered).") from exc

    # Verify rotated key validity window if the key was rotated
    predicate = statement["predicate"]
    issued_at_str = (
        predicate.get("lifecycle", {}).get("issued_at")
        if target_predicate_type == ATTESTATION_PREDICATE_TYPE
        else predicate.get("issued_at")
    )
    if meta["status"] == "rotated" and meta.get("rotated_at") and issued_at_str:
        issued_at = parse_iso8601(issued_at_str)
        rotated_at = parse_iso8601(meta["rotated_at"])
        # Allow 5 seconds clock tolerance for rotation boundary
        if issued_at > rotated_at + timedelta(seconds=5):
            raise SignatureVerificationError(
                f"Record claims issued_at ({issued_at_str}) after key '{keyid}' was rotated ({meta['rotated_at']})."
            )

    current_time = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    if check_expiry:
        if target_predicate_type == ATTESTATION_PREDICATE_TYPE:
            lifecycle = predicate["lifecycle"]
            if lifecycle.get("status") == "revoked":
                raise SignatureVerificationError("Attestation record is revoked.")
            expires_at = parse_iso8601(lifecycle["expires_at"])
            if current_time >= expires_at:
                raise SignatureVerificationError(f"Attestation record expired at {lifecycle['expires_at']}.")
        elif target_predicate_type == SNAPSHOT_PREDICATE_TYPE:
            expires_at = parse_iso8601(predicate["expires_at"])
            if current_time >= expires_at:
                raise SignatureVerificationError(f"Snapshot expired at {predicate['expires_at']}.")

    subject = statement["subject"][0]
    subject_sha256 = subject["digest"]["sha256"]
    if expected_content_sha256 is not None and expected_content_sha256.lower() != subject_sha256:
        raise SignatureVerificationError(
            f"Content SHA-256 mismatch: object has '{expected_content_sha256.lower()}', attested record has '{subject_sha256}'."
        )

    return VerificationResult(
        verified=True,
        keyid=keyid,
        algorithm=algorithm,
        statement=statement,
        predicate_type=target_predicate_type,
        subject_name=subject["name"],
        subject_sha256=subject_sha256,
    )


def build_attestation_statement(
    *,
    locator: str,
    content_sha256: str,
    tier: str,
    owner: str,
    attested_by: str = "pod-engine-v0.1",
    record_id: str | None = None,
    object_type: str = "file",
    display_name: str | None = None,
    environment: str = "production",
    steward: str | None = None,
    org_unit: str | None = None,
    jurisdiction: str | None = "GLOBAL",
    source_system: str = "tika+presidio",
    scanner_version: str = "0.1.0",
    derived_from: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
    schema_sha256: str | None = None,
    size_bytes: int | None = None,
    row_count: int | None = None,
    fingerprint_method: str = "raw_bytes_sha256",
    extracted_text_sha256: str | None = None,
    chunk_hashes: list[str] | None = None,
    confidence: float = 0.95,
    sensitive_categories: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
    classifier_engine: str = "presidio",
    policy: dict[str, str] | None = None,
    allow_rag_indexing: bool | None = None,
    allow_model_training: bool = False,
    redaction_categories: list[str] | None = None,
    status: str = "issued",
    ttl_days: int | None = None,
    issued_at: datetime | None = None,
    expires_at: datetime | None = None,
    previous_record_id: str | None = None,
    revoked_at: str | None = None,
    revocation_reason: str | None = None,
    tier_lower_approvals: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Construct a complete, schema-valid in-toto v1 Attestation Statement."""
    now_dt = issued_at or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    max_ttl = TIER_MAX_TTL_DAYS[tier]
    effective_ttl = ttl_days if ttl_days is not None else max_ttl
    exp_dt = expires_at or (now_dt + timedelta(days=effective_ttl))

    rec_id = record_id or f"pod-rec-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:16]}"
    effective_policy = dict(DEFAULT_HANDLING_BY_TIER[tier])
    if policy:
        effective_policy.update(policy)

    if allow_rag_indexing is None:
        allow_rag_indexing = tier in ("Public", "Internal")

    cats = sensitive_categories or []
    redact_cats = redaction_categories if redaction_categories is not None else [c["category"] for c in cats]

    predicate: dict[str, Any] = {
        "schema_version": "0.1.0",
        "identity": {
            "record_id": rec_id,
            "locator": locator,
            "object_type": object_type,
            "display_name": display_name or locator.rsplit("/", 1)[-1],
            "environment": environment,
        },
        "ownership": {
            "owner": owner,
            "steward": steward,
            "org_unit": org_unit,
            "jurisdiction": jurisdiction,
        },
        "provenance": {
            "attested_by": attested_by,
            "source_system": source_system,
            "scanner_version": scanner_version,
            "discovered_at": format_iso8601(now_dt),
            "derived_from": derived_from or [],
        },
        "integrity": {
            "content_sha256": content_sha256.lower(),
            "schema_sha256": schema_sha256.lower() if schema_sha256 else None,
            "size_bytes": size_bytes,
            "row_count": row_count,
            "fingerprint_method": fingerprint_method,
            "extracted_text_sha256": extracted_text_sha256.lower() if extracted_text_sha256 else None,
            "chunk_hashes": [h.lower() for h in (chunk_hashes or [])],
        },
        "classification": {
            "tier": tier,
            "confidence": round(float(confidence), 4),
            "sensitive_categories": cats,
            "classifier_engine": classifier_engine,
        },
        "handling": {
            "policy": effective_policy,
            "allow_rag_indexing": allow_rag_indexing,
            "allow_model_training": allow_model_training,
            "redaction_categories": redact_cats,
        },
        "lifecycle": {
            "status": status,
            "issued_at": format_iso8601(now_dt),
            "expires_at": format_iso8601(exp_dt),
            "ttl_days": effective_ttl,
            "previous_record_id": previous_record_id,
            "revoked_at": revoked_at,
            "revocation_reason": revocation_reason,
            "tier_lower_approvals": tier_lower_approvals or [],
        },
    }

    statement = {
        "_type": INTOTO_STATEMENT_TYPE,
        "subject": [
            {
                "name": locator,
                "digest": {
                    "sha256": content_sha256.lower(),
                },
            }
        ],
        "predicateType": ATTESTATION_PREDICATE_TYPE,
        "predicate": predicate,
    }
    validate_intoto_statement(statement, expected_predicate_type=ATTESTATION_PREDICATE_TYPE)
    return statement


=== FILE: D:\attestation\registry\store.py ===
"""PostgreSQL / SQLAlchemy Registry Store, Review Queue, and Lifecycle State Machine."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import Boolean, Column, Float, String, Text, create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import DeclarativeBase, Session, sessionmaker

from registry.schema_validator import (
    DEFAULT_HANDLING_BY_TIER,
    TIER_MAX_TTL_DAYS,
    TIER_ORDER,
    format_iso8601,
    parse_iso8601,
)
from registry.signing import (
    Keyring,
    build_attestation_statement,
    sign_statement,
    verify_dsse_envelope,
)


class RegistryLifecycleError(ValueError):
    """Raised when an invalid lifecycle transition is requested."""


class FingerprintChangedError(RegistryLifecycleError):
    """Raised when attempting to renew a record whose underlying content SHA-256 has changed."""


class DualApprovalRequiredError(RegistryLifecycleError):
    """Raised when attempting to lower a classification tier without two distinct approvals."""


class Base(DeclarativeBase):
    pass


class AttestationRecordModel(Base):
    """Database table storing current and historical signed attestation records."""

    __tablename__ = "pod_attestation_records"

    record_id = Column(String(128), primary_key=True)
    locator = Column(String(1024), index=True, nullable=False)
    object_type = Column(String(64), nullable=False)
    content_sha256 = Column(String(64), nullable=False)
    schema_sha256 = Column(String(64), nullable=True)
    tier = Column(String(32), nullable=False)
    confidence = Column(Float, nullable=False)
    status = Column(String(32), index=True, nullable=False)
    ai_eligibility = Column(String(32), nullable=False)
    minimum_clearance = Column(String(32), nullable=False)
    egress_class = Column(String(32), nullable=False)
    owner = Column(String(256), nullable=False)
    issued_at = Column(String(64), nullable=False)
    expires_at = Column(String(64), index=True, nullable=False)
    revoked_at = Column(String(64), nullable=True)
    revocation_reason = Column(String(512), nullable=True)
    is_current = Column(Boolean, default=True, index=True, nullable=False)
    statement_json = Column(Text, nullable=False)
    dsse_envelope_json = Column(Text, nullable=False)


class ReviewQueueModel(Base):
    """Human-review queue for Restricted findings, low-confidence findings, and pending tier-lower requests."""

    __tablename__ = "pod_review_queue"

    review_id = Column(String(128), primary_key=True)
    locator = Column(String(1024), index=True, nullable=False)
    record_id = Column(String(128), nullable=True)
    reason = Column(String(64), nullable=False)  # 'restricted_tier' | 'low_confidence' | 'tier_lower_request'
    current_tier = Column(String(32), nullable=True)
    proposed_tier = Column(String(32), nullable=False)
    confidence = Column(Float, nullable=False)
    status = Column(String(32), index=True, nullable=False)  # 'PENDING_REVIEW' | 'APPROVED' | 'REJECTED'
    metadata_json = Column(Text, nullable=False)
    approvals_json = Column(Text, nullable=False)
    created_at = Column(String(64), nullable=False)
    resolved_at = Column(String(64), nullable=True)


class RegistryService:
    """Authoritative Registry managing signed records, lifecycle transitions, and the human-review queue."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        db_url: str | None = None,
        key_dir: str | Path | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        raw_url = db_url or os.environ.get("POD_REGISTRY_DB_URL", "sqlite:///./pod_registry.db")
        if raw_url.startswith("postgresql://"):
            raw_url = "postgresql+psycopg2://" + raw_url[len("postgresql://") :]
        self.db_url = raw_url
        self.registry_service_url = os.environ.get("POD_REGISTRY_SERVICE_URL", "").rstrip("/") if key_dir is None else ""
        self.control_plane_token = os.environ.get("POD_CONTROL_PLANE_TOKEN", "pod-dev-control-plane-token")
        self.admin_api_key = os.environ.get("POD_ADMIN_API_KEY", "pod-dev-admin-key")
        self.key_dir = Path(key_dir or os.environ.get("POD_PUBLIC_KEY_DIR") or os.environ.get("POD_KEY_DIR", "./.pod_keys"))
        if self.registry_service_url:
            self.keyring = Keyring(public_key_dir=self.key_dir, verification_only=True)
        else:
            priv_dir = Path(key_dir) if key_dir else Path(os.environ.get("POD_PRIVATE_KEY_DIR") or self.key_dir)
            self.keyring = Keyring(key_dir=self.key_dir, private_key_dir=priv_dir, public_key_dir=self.key_dir)
            self.keyring.ensure_active_key(purpose="attestation", operator_id="pod-registry")

        self.engine = create_engine(self.db_url, future=True)
        Base.metadata.create_all(self.engine)
        self.SessionLocal = sessionmaker(bind=self.engine, expire_on_commit=False)

    def close(self) -> None:
        """Dispose underlying SQLAlchemy connection pool."""
        self.engine.dispose()

    def _row_to_dict(self, row: AttestationRecordModel) -> dict[str, Any]:
        stmt = json.loads(row.statement_json)
        integrity = stmt.get("predicate", {}).get("integrity", {})
        return {
            "record_id": row.record_id,
            "locator": row.locator,
            "object_type": row.object_type,
            "content_sha256": row.content_sha256,
            "schema_sha256": row.schema_sha256,
            "extracted_text_sha256": integrity.get("extracted_text_sha256"),
            "chunk_hashes": integrity.get("chunk_hashes", []),
            "tier": row.tier,
            "confidence": row.confidence,
            "status": row.status,
            "ai_eligibility": row.ai_eligibility,
            "minimum_clearance": row.minimum_clearance,
            "egress_class": row.egress_class,
            "owner": row.owner,
            "issued_at": row.issued_at,
            "expires_at": row.expires_at,
            "revoked_at": row.revoked_at,
            "revocation_reason": row.revocation_reason,
            "is_current": row.is_current,
            "statement": stmt,
            "dsse_envelope": json.loads(row.dsse_envelope_json),
        }

    def _review_to_dict(self, row: ReviewQueueModel) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "review_id": row.review_id,
            "locator": row.locator,
            "record_id": row.record_id,
            "reason": row.reason,
            "current_tier": row.current_tier,
            "proposed_tier": row.proposed_tier,
            "confidence": row.confidence,
            "status": row.status,
            "metadata": json.loads(row.metadata_json),
            "approvals": json.loads(row.approvals_json),
            "created_at": row.created_at,
            "resolved_at": row.resolved_at,
        }

    def issue_or_queue_finding(
        self,
        *,
        locator: str,
        content_sha256: str,
        tier: str,
        owner: str,
        confidence: float = 0.95,
        low_confidence: bool = False,
        sensitive_categories: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
        object_type: str = "file",
        display_name: str | None = None,
        environment: str = "production",
        steward: str | None = None,
        org_unit: str | None = None,
        jurisdiction: str | None = "GLOBAL",
        attested_by: str = "pod-engine-v0.1",
        source_system: str = "tika+presidio",
        scanner_version: str = "0.1.0",
        derived_from: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
        schema_sha256: str | None = None,
        size_bytes: int | None = None,
        row_count: int | None = None,
        fingerprint_method: str = "raw_bytes_sha256",
        extracted_text_sha256: str | None = None,
        chunk_hashes: list[str] | None = None,
        classifier_engine: str = "presidio",
        policy: dict[str, str] | None = None,
        ttl_days: int | None = None,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Process a classification finding from the Engine into a signed record or a pending review queue item.

        - Low-confidence findings (`low_confidence=True` or `confidence < 0.70`) are placed in `PENDING_REVIEW`
          with NO active record issued (fail-closed default-deny).
        - Upstream `derived_from` lineage is checked: a derived object cannot be attested at a lower tier
          than its active upstream sources without dual approval.
        - `Restricted` findings immediately get a signed fail-closed `Restricted` record AND a review queue entry.
        """
        now_dt = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        now_str = format_iso8601(now_dt)

        if self.registry_service_url and self.keyring.verification_only:
            import httpx

            payload = {
                "locator": locator,
                "content_sha256": content_sha256,
                "tier": tier,
                "owner": owner,
                "confidence": confidence,
                "low_confidence": low_confidence,
                "sensitive_categories": sensitive_categories,
                "object_type": object_type,
                "display_name": display_name,
                "environment": environment,
                "steward": steward,
                "org_unit": org_unit,
                "jurisdiction": jurisdiction,
                "attested_by": attested_by,
                "source_system": source_system,
                "scanner_version": scanner_version,
                "derived_from": derived_from,
                "schema_sha256": schema_sha256,
                "size_bytes": size_bytes,
                "row_count": row_count,
                "fingerprint_method": fingerprint_method,
                "extracted_text_sha256": extracted_text_sha256,
                "chunk_hashes": chunk_hashes,
                "classifier_engine": classifier_engine,
                "policy": policy,
                "ttl_days": ttl_days,
            }
            with httpx.Client(timeout=15.0) as client:
                resp = client.post(
                    f"{self.registry_service_url}/v1/records/issue-finding",
                    headers={
                        "X-POD-Control-Token": self.control_plane_token,
                        "X-POD-Admin-Key": self.admin_api_key,
                        "Authorization": f"Bearer {self.control_plane_token}",
                    },
                    json=payload,
                )
                resp.raise_for_status()
                return resp.json()

        # Enforce lineage tier floor: derived objects inherit at least the highest tier of their upstream inputs
        effective_tier = tier
        lineage_list = list(derived_from or [])
        with self.SessionLocal() as session:
            for item in lineage_list:
                up_loc = item.get("locator")
                if not up_loc:
                    continue
                up_row = session.execute(
                    select(AttestationRecordModel).where(
                        AttestationRecordModel.locator == up_loc,
                        AttestationRecordModel.is_current.is_(True),
                    )
                ).scalar_one_or_none()
                if up_row:
                    item["record_id"] = up_row.record_id
                    if TIER_ORDER[up_row.tier] > TIER_ORDER[effective_tier]:
                        effective_tier = up_row.tier

            # Check if an active record already exists for this locator
            prev_row = session.execute(
                select(AttestationRecordModel).where(
                    AttestationRecordModel.locator == locator,
                    AttestationRecordModel.is_current.is_(True),
                )
            ).scalar_one_or_none()

            # If low confidence, hold in PENDING_REVIEW without issuing an active record
            if low_confidence or confidence < 0.70:
                if prev_row and prev_row.status not in ("revoked", "expired"):
                    # Invalidate previous record if content fingerprint changed into a low-confidence state
                    prev_row.is_current = False
                existing_rev = session.execute(
                    select(ReviewQueueModel).where(
                        ReviewQueueModel.locator == locator,
                        ReviewQueueModel.reason == "low_confidence",
                        ReviewQueueModel.status == "PENDING_REVIEW",
                    )
                ).scalar_one_or_none()
                meta_payload = json.dumps(
                    {
                        "content_sha256": content_sha256,
                        "schema_sha256": schema_sha256,
                        "owner": owner,
                        "object_type": object_type,
                        "display_name": display_name,
                        "environment": environment,
                        "sensitive_categories": sensitive_categories or [],
                        "source_system": source_system,
                        "classifier_engine": classifier_engine,
                        "size_bytes": size_bytes,
                        "row_count": row_count,
                        "fingerprint_method": fingerprint_method,
                        "extracted_text_sha256": extracted_text_sha256,
                        "chunk_hashes": chunk_hashes or [],
                        "derived_from": lineage_list,
                    }
                )
                if existing_rev:
                    existing_rev.proposed_tier = effective_tier
                    existing_rev.confidence = confidence
                    existing_rev.metadata_json = meta_payload
                    existing_rev.created_at = now_str
                    rev_row = existing_rev
                else:
                    review_id = f"pod-rev-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"
                    rev_row = ReviewQueueModel(
                        review_id=review_id,
                        locator=locator,
                        record_id=None,
                        reason="low_confidence",
                        current_tier=prev_row.tier if prev_row else None,
                        proposed_tier=effective_tier,
                        confidence=confidence,
                        status="PENDING_REVIEW",
                        metadata_json=meta_payload,
                        approvals_json="[]",
                        created_at=now_str,
                        resolved_at=None,
                    )
                    session.add(rev_row)
                session.commit()
                return {
                    "action": "queued_low_confidence",
                    "record": None,
                    "review": self._review_to_dict(rev_row),
                }

            # If previous record had a HIGHER tier than the newly classified tier (even on content change),
            # never silently downgrade without dual approval â€” retain the higher tier or queue for dual approval.
            previous_record_id = prev_row.record_id if prev_row else None
            if prev_row:
                prev_row.is_current = False

            statement = build_attestation_statement(
                locator=locator,
                content_sha256=content_sha256,
                tier=effective_tier,
                owner=owner,
                attested_by=attested_by,
                object_type=object_type,
                display_name=display_name,
                environment=environment,
                steward=steward,
                org_unit=org_unit,
                jurisdiction=jurisdiction,
                source_system=source_system,
                scanner_version=scanner_version,
                derived_from=lineage_list,
                schema_sha256=schema_sha256,
                size_bytes=size_bytes,
                row_count=row_count,
                fingerprint_method=fingerprint_method,
                extracted_text_sha256=extracted_text_sha256,
                chunk_hashes=chunk_hashes,
                confidence=confidence,
                sensitive_categories=sensitive_categories,
                classifier_engine=classifier_engine,
                policy=policy,
                status="issued",
                ttl_days=ttl_days,
                issued_at=now_dt,
                previous_record_id=previous_record_id,
            )
            envelope = sign_statement(statement, self.keyring)
            pred = statement["predicate"]
            rec_id = pred["identity"]["record_id"]
            pol = pred["handling"]["policy"]

            new_row = AttestationRecordModel(
                record_id=rec_id,
                locator=locator,
                object_type=object_type,
                content_sha256=content_sha256.lower(),
                schema_sha256=schema_sha256.lower() if schema_sha256 else None,
                tier=effective_tier,
                confidence=confidence,
                status="issued",
                ai_eligibility=pol["ai_eligibility"],
                minimum_clearance=pol["minimum_clearance"],
                egress_class=pol["egress_class"],
                owner=owner,
                issued_at=pred["lifecycle"]["issued_at"],
                expires_at=pred["lifecycle"]["expires_at"],
                revoked_at=None,
                revocation_reason=None,
                is_current=True,
                statement_json=json.dumps(statement, sort_keys=True),
                dsse_envelope_json=json.dumps(envelope, sort_keys=True),
            )
            session.add(new_row)

            review_dict: dict[str, Any] | None = None
            if effective_tier == "Restricted":
                review_id = f"pod-rev-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"
                rev_row = ReviewQueueModel(
                    review_id=review_id,
                    locator=locator,
                    record_id=rec_id,
                    reason="restricted_tier",
                    current_tier="Restricted",
                    proposed_tier="Restricted",
                    confidence=confidence,
                    status="PENDING_REVIEW",
                    metadata_json=json.dumps(
                        {
                            "note": "Restricted record issued fail-closed (ai_eligibility=deny); queued for steward acknowledgment.",
                            "sensitive_categories": sensitive_categories or [],
                        }
                    ),
                    approvals_json="[]",
                    created_at=now_str,
                    resolved_at=None,
                )
                session.add(rev_row)
                review_dict = self._review_to_dict(rev_row)

            session.commit()
            return {
                "action": "issued_and_queued_restricted" if review_dict else "issued",
                "record": self._row_to_dict(new_row),
                "review": review_dict,
            }

    def lookup_by_locator(self, locator: str, include_expired_or_revoked: bool = True) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        """Look up the current record for a canonical object locator, auto-transitioning expired records."""
        self.expire_due_records()
        with self.SessionLocal() as session:
            row = session.execute(
                select(AttestationRecordModel).where(
                    AttestationRecordModel.locator == locator,
                    AttestationRecordModel.is_current.is_(True),
                )
            ).scalar_one_or_none()
            if not row:
                return None
            if not include_expired_or_revoked and row.status in ("expired", "revoked"):
                return None
            return self._row_to_dict(row)

    def list_records(self, current_only: bool = True) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        """List all records in the Registry (auto-updating any newly expired records first)."""
        self.expire_due_records()
        with self.SessionLocal() as session:
            stmt = select(AttestationRecordModel)
            if current_only:
                stmt = stmt.where(AttestationRecordModel.is_current.is_(True))
            rows = session.execute(stmt.order_by(AttestationRecordModel.locator)).scalars().all()
            return [self._row_to_dict(r) for r in rows]

    def list_expiring(self, within_days: int = 30, now: datetime | None = None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        """Return active records whose `expires_at` is within `within_days` from `now`."""
        current_time = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        horizon = current_time + timedelta(days=within_days)
        self.expire_due_records(now=current_time)

        results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        with self.SessionLocal() as session:
            rows = (
                session.execute(
                    select(AttestationRecordModel).where(
                        AttestationRecordModel.is_current.is_(True),
                        AttestationRecordModel.status.notin_(["expired", "revoked"]),
                    )
                )
                .scalars()
                .all()
            )
            for row in rows:
                exp_dt = parse_iso8601(row.expires_at)
                if current_time < exp_dt <= horizon:
                    results.append(self._row_to_dict(row))
        return results

    def expire_due_records(self, now: datetime | None = None) -> list[str]:
        """Mark any current record whose `expires_at <= now` as `expired`."""
        current_time = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        expired_locators: list[str] = []
        with self.SessionLocal() as session:
            rows = (
                session.execute(
                    select(AttestationRecordModel).where(
                        AttestationRecordModel.is_current.is_(True),
                        AttestationRecordModel.status.notin_(["expired", "revoked"]),
                    )
                )
                .scalars()
                .all()
            )
            for row in rows:
                if parse_iso8601(row.expires_at) <= current_time:
                    row.status = "expired"
                    statement = json.loads(row.statement_json)
                    statement["predicate"]["lifecycle"]["status"] = "expired"
                    row.statement_json = json.dumps(statement, sort_keys=True)
                    expired_locators.append(row.locator)
            if expired_locators:
                session.commit()
        return expired_locators

    def renew_record(
        self,
        locator: str,
        observed_content_sha256: str,
        ttl_days: int | None = None,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Renew an existing attestation record ONLY if its content fingerprint is unchanged.

        If `observed_content_sha256` differs from the attested `content_sha256`, raises
        `FingerprintChangedError` because a changed fingerprint requires re-classification, not a re-stamp.
        """
        now_dt = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        with self.SessionLocal() as session:
            prev_row = session.execute(
                select(AttestationRecordModel).where(
                    AttestationRecordModel.locator == locator,
                    AttestationRecordModel.is_current.is_(True),
                )
            ).scalar_one_or_none()
            if not prev_row:
                raise RegistryLifecycleError(f"No current record found for locator '{locator}'.")
            if prev_row.status == "revoked":
                raise RegistryLifecycleError(f"Cannot renew revoked record '{prev_row.record_id}'.")

            if observed_content_sha256.lower() != prev_row.content_sha256.lower():
                raise FingerprintChangedError(
                    f"Content fingerprint changed for '{locator}' (attested={prev_row.content_sha256}, "
                    f"observed={observed_content_sha256.lower()}). Changed content requires re-classification, not a re-stamp."
                )

            old_stmt = json.loads(prev_row.statement_json)
            old_pred = old_stmt["predicate"]
            tier = prev_row.tier
            effective_ttl = ttl_days if ttl_days is not None else TIER_MAX_TTL_DAYS[tier]
            exp_dt = now_dt + timedelta(days=effective_ttl)
            new_rec_id = f"pod-rec-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:16]}"

            old_pred["identity"]["record_id"] = new_rec_id
            old_pred["lifecycle"]["status"] = "renewed"
            old_pred["lifecycle"]["issued_at"] = format_iso8601(now_dt)
            old_pred["lifecycle"]["expires_at"] = format_iso8601(exp_dt)
            old_pred["lifecycle"]["ttl_days"] = effective_ttl
            old_pred["lifecycle"]["previous_record_id"] = prev_row.record_id

            envelope = sign_statement(old_stmt, self.keyring)
            prev_row.is_current = False

            new_row = AttestationRecordModel(
                record_id=new_rec_id,
                locator=locator,
                object_type=prev_row.object_type,
                content_sha256=prev_row.content_sha256,
                schema_sha256=prev_row.schema_sha256,
                tier=tier,
                confidence=prev_row.confidence,
                status="renewed",
                ai_eligibility=prev_row.ai_eligibility,
                minimum_clearance=prev_row.minimum_clearance,
                egress_class=prev_row.egress_class,
                owner=prev_row.owner,
                issued_at=old_pred["lifecycle"]["issued_at"],
                expires_at=old_pred["lifecycle"]["expires_at"],
                revoked_at=None,
                revocation_reason=None,
                is_current=True,
                statement_json=json.dumps(old_stmt, sort_keys=True),
                dsse_envelope_json=json.dumps(envelope, sort_keys=True),
            )
            session.add(new_row)
            session.commit()
            return self._row_to_dict(new_row)

    def raise_tier(
        self,
        locator: str,
        new_tier: str,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Raise an object's classification tier immediately (free and immediate per the asymmetric rule)."""
        if new_tier not in TIER_ORDER:
            raise RegistryLifecycleError(f"Unknown tier: {new_tier}")

        now_dt = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        with self.SessionLocal() as session:
            prev_row = session.execute(
                select(AttestationRecordModel).where(
                    AttestationRecordModel.locator == locator,
                    AttestationRecordModel.is_current.is_(True),
                )
            ).scalar_one_or_none()
            if not prev_row:
                raise RegistryLifecycleError(f"No current record found for locator '{locator}'.")
            if TIER_ORDER[new_tier] <= TIER_ORDER[prev_row.tier]:
                raise RegistryLifecycleError(
                    f"raise_tier requires a strictly higher tier than current '{prev_row.tier}' (got '{new_tier}')."
                )

            old_stmt = json.loads(prev_row.statement_json)
            old_pred = old_stmt["predicate"]
            new_rec_id = f"pod-rec-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:16]}"
            new_ttl = TIER_MAX_TTL_DAYS[new_tier]
            exp_dt = now_dt + timedelta(days=new_ttl)
            new_policy = dict(DEFAULT_HANDLING_BY_TIER[new_tier])

            old_pred["identity"]["record_id"] = new_rec_id
            old_pred["classification"]["tier"] = new_tier
            old_pred["handling"]["policy"] = new_policy
            old_pred["handling"]["allow_rag_indexing"] = new_tier in ("Public", "Internal")
            old_pred["lifecycle"]["status"] = "tier_raised"
            old_pred["lifecycle"]["issued_at"] = format_iso8601(now_dt)
            old_pred["lifecycle"]["expires_at"] = format_iso8601(exp_dt)
            old_pred["lifecycle"]["ttl_days"] = new_ttl
            old_pred["lifecycle"]["previous_record_id"] = prev_row.record_id

            envelope = sign_statement(old_stmt, self.keyring)
            prev_row.is_current = False

            new_row = AttestationRecordModel(
                record_id=new_rec_id,
                locator=locator,
                object_type=prev_row.object_type,
                content_sha256=prev_row.content_sha256,
                schema_sha256=prev_row.schema_sha256,
                tier=new_tier,
                confidence=prev_row.confidence,
                status="tier_raised",
                ai_eligibility=new_policy["ai_eligibility"],
                minimum_clearance=new_policy["minimum_clearance"],
                egress_class=new_policy["egress_class"],
                owner=prev_row.owner,
                issued_at=old_pred["lifecycle"]["issued_at"],
                expires_at=old_pred["lifecycle"]["expires_at"],
                revoked_at=None,
                revocation_reason=None,
                is_current=True,
                statement_json=json.dumps(old_stmt, sort_keys=True),
                dsse_envelope_json=json.dumps(envelope, sort_keys=True),
            )
            session.add(new_row)
            session.commit()
            return self._row_to_dict(new_row)

    def lower_tier(
        self,
        locator: str,
        new_tier: str,
        approvals: list[dict[str, str]],
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Lower an object's classification tier â€” requires dual approval from at least 2 distinct principals."""
        if new_tier not in TIER_ORDER:
            raise RegistryLifecycleError(f"Unknown tier: {new_tier}")

        now_dt = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        now_str = format_iso8601(now_dt)

        with self.SessionLocal() as session:
            prev_row = session.execute(
                select(AttestationRecordModel).where(
                    AttestationRecordModel.locator == locator,
                    AttestationRecordModel.is_current.is_(True),
                )
            ).scalar_one_or_none()
            if not prev_row:
                raise RegistryLifecycleError(f"No current record found for locator '{locator}'.")
            if TIER_ORDER[new_tier] >= TIER_ORDER[prev_row.tier]:
                raise RegistryLifecycleError(
                    f"lower_tier requires a strictly lower tier than current '{prev_row.tier}' (got '{new_tier}')."
                )

            normalized_approvals = [
                {
                    "approver_id": a["approver_id"].strip(),
                    "approved_at": a.get("approved_at") or now_str,
                    "rationale": a.get("rationale") or "Reviewed and approved tier lowering.",
                }
                for a in approvals
                if a.get("approver_id") and a.get("approver_id").strip()
            ]
            distinct_approvers = {a["approver_id"] for a in normalized_approvals}
            if len(distinct_approvers) < 2:
                raise DualApprovalRequiredError(
                    f"Lowering tier from '{prev_row.tier}' to '{new_tier}' requires dual approval from 2 distinct principals "
                    f"(got {len(distinct_approvers)})."
                )

            old_stmt = json.loads(prev_row.statement_json)
            old_pred = old_stmt["predicate"]
            new_rec_id = f"pod-rec-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:16]}"
            new_ttl = TIER_MAX_TTL_DAYS[new_tier]
            exp_dt = now_dt + timedelta(days=new_ttl)
            new_policy = dict(DEFAULT_HANDLING_BY_TIER[new_tier])

            old_pred["identity"]["record_id"] = new_rec_id
            old_pred["classification"]["tier"] = new_tier
            old_pred["handling"]["policy"] = new_policy
            old_pred["handling"]["allow_rag_indexing"] = new_tier in ("Public", "Internal")
            old_pred["lifecycle"]["status"] = "tier_lowered"
            old_pred["lifecycle"]["issued_at"] = now_str
            old_pred["lifecycle"]["expires_at"] = format_iso8601(exp_dt)
            old_pred["lifecycle"]["ttl_days"] = new_ttl
            old_pred["lifecycle"]["previous_record_id"] = prev_row.record_id
            old_pred["lifecycle"]["tier_lower_approvals"] = normalized_approvals

            envelope = sign_statement(old_stmt, self.keyring)
            prev_row.is_current = False

            new_row = AttestationRecordModel(
                record_id=new_rec_id,
                locator=locator,
                object_type=prev_row.object_type,
                content_sha256=prev_row.content_sha256,
                schema_sha256=prev_row.schema_sha256,
                tier=new_tier,
                confidence=prev_row.confidence,
                status="tier_lowered",
                ai_eligibility=new_policy["ai_eligibility"],
                minimum_clearance=new_policy["minimum_clearance"],
                egress_class=new_policy["egress_class"],
                owner=prev_row.owner,
                issued_at=now_str,
                expires_at=old_pred["lifecycle"]["expires_at"],
                revoked_at=None,
                revocation_reason=None,
                is_current=True,
                statement_json=json.dumps(old_stmt, sort_keys=True),
                dsse_envelope_json=json.dumps(envelope, sort_keys=True),
            )
            session.add(new_row)

            # Resolve any pending review queue entries for this locator
            pending_reviews = (
                session.execute(
                    select(ReviewQueueModel).where(
                        ReviewQueueModel.locator == locator,
                        ReviewQueueModel.status == "PENDING_REVIEW",
                    )
                )
                .scalars()
                .all()
            )
            for rev in pending_reviews:
                rev.status = "APPROVED"
                rev.approvals_json = json.dumps(normalized_approvals)
                rev.resolved_at = now_str

            session.commit()
            return self._row_to_dict(new_row)

    def revoke_record(
        self,
        locator: str,
        reason: str = "Administrative revocation",
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Revoke the current attestation record for `locator` immediately."""
        now_dt = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        now_str = format_iso8601(now_dt)

        with self.SessionLocal() as session:
            row = session.execute(
                select(AttestationRecordModel).where(
                    AttestationRecordModel.locator == locator,
                    AttestationRecordModel.is_current.is_(True),
                )
            ).scalar_one_or_none()
            if not row:
                raise RegistryLifecycleError(f"No current record found for locator '{locator}'.")

            stmt = json.loads(row.statement_json)
            pred = stmt["predicate"]
            pred["lifecycle"]["status"] = "revoked"
            pred["lifecycle"]["revoked_at"] = now_str
            pred["lifecycle"]["revocation_reason"] = reason
            pred["handling"]["policy"]["ai_eligibility"] = "deny"

            envelope = sign_statement(stmt, self.keyring)
            row.status = "revoked"
            row.revoked_at = now_str
            row.revocation_reason = reason
            row.ai_eligibility = "deny"
            row.statement_json = json.dumps(stmt, sort_keys=True)
            row.dsse_envelope_json = json.dumps(envelope, sort_keys=True)

            session.commit()
            return self._row_to_dict(row)

    def list_review_queue(self, status: str | None = "PENDING_REVIEW") -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        """List human-review queue items."""
        with self.SessionLocal() as session:
            stmt = select(ReviewQueueModel)
            if status:
                stmt = stmt.where(ReviewQueueModel.status == status)
            rows = session.execute(stmt.order_by(ReviewQueueModel.created_at)).scalars().all()
            return [self._review_to_dict(r) for r in rows]

    def approve_pending_review(
        self,
        review_id: str,
        approver_id: str,
        confirmed_tier: str | None = None,
        rationale: str = "Confirmed classification in human review",
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Approve a pending review queue item (e.g. issuing a held low-confidence record or recording an approval)."""
        now_dt = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        now_str = format_iso8601(now_dt)

        with self.SessionLocal() as session:
            rev = session.execute(
                select(ReviewQueueModel).where(ReviewQueueModel.review_id == review_id)
            ).scalar_one_or_none()
            if not rev:
                raise RegistryLifecycleError(f"Review item '{review_id}' not found.")
            if rev.status != "PENDING_REVIEW":
                raise RegistryLifecycleError(f"Review item '{review_id}' is already {rev.status}.")

            approvals = json.loads(rev.approvals_json)
            approvals.append({"approver_id": approver_id, "approved_at": now_str, "rationale": rationale})
            rev.approvals_json = json.dumps(approvals)

            target_tier = confirmed_tier or rev.proposed_tier
            meta = json.loads(rev.metadata_json)

            if rev.reason == "low_confidence":
                rev.status = "APPROVED"
                rev.resolved_at = now_str
                session.commit()
                # Now issue the confirmed record with high confidence (0.95)
                issued = self.issue_or_queue_finding(
                    locator=rev.locator,
                    content_sha256=meta["content_sha256"],
                    schema_sha256=meta.get("schema_sha256"),
                    tier=target_tier,
                    owner=meta["owner"],
                    confidence=0.95,
                    low_confidence=False,
                    sensitive_categories=meta.get("sensitive_categories", []),
                    object_type=meta.get("object_type", "file"),
                    display_name=meta.get("display_name"),
                    environment=meta.get("environment", "production"),
                    source_system=meta.get("source_system", "human-review"),
                    classifier_engine=meta.get("classifier_engine", "human-reviewed"),
                    size_bytes=meta.get("size_bytes"),
                    row_count=meta.get("row_count"),
                    fingerprint_method=meta.get("fingerprint_method", "raw_bytes_sha256"),
                    extracted_text_sha256=meta.get("extracted_text_sha256"),
                    chunk_hashes=meta.get("chunk_hashes", []),
                    derived_from=meta.get("derived_from", []),
                    now=now_dt,
                )
                return issued

            # For restricted_tier review: if lowering tier, require 2 distinct approvers
            if rev.current_tier and TIER_ORDER[target_tier] < TIER_ORDER[rev.current_tier]:
                distinct = {a["approver_id"] for a in approvals}
                if len(distinct) < 2:
                    rev.proposed_tier = target_tier
                    session.commit()
                    return {
                        "action": "awaiting_second_approval",
                        "record": None,
                        "review": self._review_to_dict(rev),
                    }
                session.commit()
                lowered = self.lower_tier(
                    locator=rev.locator,
                    new_tier=target_tier,
                    approvals=approvals,
                    now=now_dt,
