                )
                return {"action": "tier_lowered", "record": lowered, "review": self._review_to_dict(rev)}

            rev.status = "APPROVED"
            rev.resolved_at = now_str
            session.commit()
            return {
                "action": "acknowledged",
                "record": self.lookup_by_locator(rev.locator),
                "review": self._review_to_dict(rev),
            }

    def verify_record(
        self,
        envelope: dict[str, Any],
        expected_content_sha256: str | None = None,
        check_expiry: bool = True,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Verify a DSSE attestation record envelope against the Registry's local keyring."""
        res = verify_dsse_envelope(
            envelope=envelope,
            keyring=self.keyring,
            expected_content_sha256=expected_content_sha256,
            check_expiry=check_expiry,
            now=now,
        )
        return {
            "verified": res.verified,
            "keyid": res.keyid,
            "algorithm": res.algorithm,
            "locator": res.subject_name,
            "content_sha256": res.subject_sha256,
            "tier": res.statement["predicate"]["classification"]["tier"],
            "status": res.statement["predicate"]["lifecycle"]["status"],
            "expires_at": res.statement["predicate"]["lifecycle"]["expires_at"],
        }


=== FILE: D:\attestation\registry\api.py ===
"""Hardened Registry Lookup and Lifecycle Admin FastAPI Service."""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from typing import Any

from fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Header, HTTPException, Query, status
from fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from registry.store import (
    DualApprovalRequiredError,
    FingerprintChangedError,
    RegistryLifecycleError,
    RegistryService,
)


class RaiseTierRequest(BaseModel):
    locator: str
    new_tier: str


class ApprovalEntry(BaseModel):
    approver_id: str
    rationale: str = "Approved tier lowering"


class LowerTierRequest(BaseModel):
    locator: str
    new_tier: str
    approvals: list[ApprovalEntry] = Field(min_length=2)


class RevokeRequest(BaseModel):
    locator: str
    reason: str = "Administrative revocation"


class RenewRequest(BaseModel):
    locator: str
    observed_content_sha256: str
    ttl_days: int | None = None


class ReviewDecisionRequest(BaseModel):
    approver_id: str
    confirmed_tier: str | None = None
    rationale: str = "Confirmed in human review"


class IssueFindingRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = {"extra": "allow"}
    locator: str
    content_sha256: str
    tier: str
    owner: str


def create_registry_app(registry: RegistryService | None = None) -> FastAPI:
    """Create the hardened Registry FastAPI server (requires X-POD-Admin-Key for all catalog queries and mutations)."""
    app = FastAPI(title="POD Authoritative Registry API", version="0.1.0")
    svc = registry or RegistryService()
    admin_key = os.environ.get("POD_ADMIN_API_KEY", "pod-dev-admin-key")
    control_token = os.environ.get("POD_CONTROL_PLANE_TOKEN", "pod-dev-control-plane-token")

    def require_admin_auth(
        x_pod_admin_key: str | None = Header(default=None),
        x_pod_control_token: str | None = Header(default=None),
        authorization: str | None = Header(default=None),
    ) -> None:
        token = x_pod_admin_key or x_pod_control_token
        if not token and authorization and authorization.lower().startswith("bearer "):
            token = authorization[7:].strip()
        valid_tokens = {t for t in (admin_key, control_token) if t}
        if not token or token not in valid_tokens:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
                detail="Registry access denied: valid X-POD-Admin-Key, X-POD-Control-Token, or Bearer token required.",
            )

    @app.get("/", include_in_schema=False)
    def root() -> RedirectResponse:
        return RedirectResponse(url="/docs")

    @app.get("/healthz")
    def healthz() -> dict[str, str]:
        return {"status": "ok", "service": "pod-registry"}

    @app.get("/v1/records/lookup", dependencies=[Depends(require_admin_auth)])
    def lookup_record(locator: str = Query(..., description="Canonical object locator URI")) -> dict[str, Any]:
        rec = svc.lookup_by_locator(locator)
        if not rec:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="No record found for locator")
        return rec

    @app.get("/v1/records", dependencies=[Depends(require_admin_auth)])
    def list_records(current_only: bool = True) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"records": svc.list_records(current_only=current_only)}

    @app.get("/v1/records/expiring", dependencies=[Depends(require_admin_auth)])
    def list_expiring(within_days: int = 30) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"records": svc.list_expiring(within_days=within_days)}

    @app.post("/v1/records/issue-finding", dependencies=[Depends(require_admin_auth)])
    def issue_finding(req: IssueFindingRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        kwargs = req.model_dump()
        return svc.issue_or_queue_finding(**kwargs)

    @app.post("/v1/records/raise-tier", dependencies=[Depends(require_admin_auth)])
    def raise_tier(req: RaiseTierRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return svc.raise_tier(locator=req.locator, new_tier=req.new_tier)
        except RegistryLifecycleError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST, detail=str(exc)) from exc

    @app.post("/v1/records/lower-tier", dependencies=[Depends(require_admin_auth)])
    def lower_tier(req: LowerTierRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            approvals = [a.model_dump() for a in req.approvals]
            return svc.lower_tier(locator=req.locator, new_tier=req.new_tier, approvals=approvals)
        except DualApprovalRequiredError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail=str(exc)) from exc
        except RegistryLifecycleError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST, detail=str(exc)) from exc

    @app.post("/v1/records/revoke", dependencies=[Depends(require_admin_auth)])
    def revoke_record(req: RevokeRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return svc.revoke_record(locator=req.locator, reason=req.reason)
        except RegistryLifecycleError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST, detail=str(exc)) from exc

    @app.post("/v1/records/renew", dependencies=[Depends(require_admin_auth)])
    def renew_record(req: RenewRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return svc.renew_record(
                locator=req.locator,
                observed_content_sha256=req.observed_content_sha256,
                ttl_days=req.ttl_days,
            )
        except FingerprintChangedError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT, detail=str(exc)) from exc
        except RegistryLifecycleError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST, detail=str(exc)) from exc

    @app.get("/v1/reviews", dependencies=[Depends(require_admin_auth)])
    def list_reviews(status_filter: str | None = Query(default="PENDING_REVIEW", alias="status")) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"reviews": svc.list_review_queue(status=status_filter)}

    @app.post("/v1/reviews/{review_id}/approve", dependencies=[Depends(require_admin_auth)])
    def approve_review(review_id: str, req: ReviewDecisionRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return svc.approve_pending_review(
                review_id=review_id,
                approver_id=req.approver_id,
                confirmed_tier=req.confirmed_tier,
                rationale=req.rationale,
            )
        except RegistryLifecycleError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST, detail=str(exc)) from exc

    return app


=== FILE: D:\attestation\registry\cli.py ===
"""POD Command-Line Interface (`pod`): key management, attestation, lookup, verification, and lifecycle."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Sequence

from engine.pipeline import AttestationEngine
from registry.signing import Keyring, compute_file_sha256, verify_dsse_envelope
from registry.store import RegistryService
from snapshot.publisher import SnapshotPublisher


def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        prog="pod",
        description="POD (Proof Of Data) â€” Data Attestation Framework CLI",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--db-url", default=None, help="Registry SQLAlchemy database URL")
    parser.add_argument("--key-dir", default=None, help="Operator keyring directory")

    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)

    # keygen / key-rotate
    p_key = sub.add_parser("keygen", help="Generate or rotate local operator signing keys")
    p_key.add_argument("--algorithm", choices=["Ed25519", "ECDSA_P256_SHA256"], default="Ed25519")
    p_key.add_argument("--rotate", action="store_true", help="Rotate active key")

    # attest
    p_att = sub.add_parser("attest", help="Discover, classify, and attest a file, directory share, or database")
    p_att.add_argument("target", help="File path, directory path, or database SQLAlchemy URL")
    p_att.add_argument("--owner", default="data-governance@example.org", help="Accountable owner identifier")
    p_att.add_argument("--locator", default=None, help="Optional canonical locator URI override")
    p_att.add_argument("--output", default=None, help="Optional path to write the signed DSSE envelope JSON")

    # lookup
    p_look = sub.add_parser("lookup", help="Look up the current attestation record by locator")
    p_look.add_argument("locator", help="Canonical object locator URI")

    # verify
    p_ver = sub.add_parser("verify", help="Verify a DSSE-signed attestation or snapshot envelope")
    p_ver.add_argument("envelope_path", help="Path to the DSSE JSON file")
    p_ver.add_argument("--file", default=None, help="Optional file on disk to verify content SHA-256 against")

    # list-expiring
    p_exp = sub.add_parser("list-expiring", help="List active records expiring within N days")
    p_exp.add_argument("--within-days", type=int, default=30, help="Horizon in days (default: 30)")

    # renew
    p_ren = sub.add_parser("renew", help="Renew a record if its content fingerprint is unchanged")
    p_ren.add_argument("locator", help="Canonical object locator URI")
    p_ren.add_argument("--sha256", default=None, help="Observed SHA-256 hex digest")
    p_ren.add_argument("--file", default=None, help="Compute observed SHA-256 from this file")

    # raise-tier
    p_raise = sub.add_parser("raise-tier", help="Immediately raise an object's classification tier")
    p_raise.add_argument("locator", help="Canonical object locator URI")
    p_raise.add_argument("new_tier", choices=["Internal", "Confidential", "Restricted"])

    # lower-tier
    p_lower = sub.add_parser("lower-tier", help="Lower an object's classification tier (requires dual approval)")
    p_lower.add_argument("locator", help="Canonical object locator URI")
    p_lower.add_argument("new_tier", choices=["Public", "Internal", "Confidential"])
    p_lower.add_argument("--approver", action="append", required=True, help="Approver ID (must specify at least 2 distinct)")
    p_lower.add_argument("--rationale", default="Dual-approved tier downgrade", help="Approval rationale")

    # revoke
    p_rev = sub.add_parser("revoke", help="Immediately revoke an attestation record")
    p_rev.add_argument("locator", help="Canonical object locator URI")
    p_rev.add_argument("--reason", default="Administrative revocation", help="Revocation reason")

    # review queue
    p_rq = sub.add_parser("review-list", help="List items in the human-review queue")
    p_rq.add_argument("--status", default="PENDING_REVIEW", help="Filter by status (default: PENDING_REVIEW)")

    p_ra = sub.add_parser("review-approve", help="Approve a pending item in the human-review queue")
    p_ra.add_argument("review_id", help="Review queue ID")
    p_ra.add_argument("--approver", required=True, help="Reviewer principal ID")
    p_ra.add_argument("--tier", default=None, choices=["Public", "Internal", "Confidential", "Restricted"])
    p_ra.add_argument("--rationale", default="Confirmed in human review")

    # publish-snapshot
    p_snap = sub.add_parser("publish-snapshot", help="Publish a signed DSSE snapshot of Registry records")
    p_snap.add_argument("--output", default="./pod_snapshot.dsse.json", help="Output path for the signed snapshot")
    p_snap.add_argument("--ttl-seconds", type=int, default=300, help="Snapshot TTL in seconds")

    return parser


def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
    parser = build_parser()
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)

    if args.command == "keygen":
        keyring = Keyring(args.key_dir or "./.pod_keys")
        meta = (
            keyring.rotate_key(algorithm=args.algorithm)
            if args.rotate
            else keyring.generate_key(algorithm=args.algorithm)
        )
        print(json.dumps({"keyid": meta["keyid"], "algorithm": meta["algorithm"], "status": meta["status"]}, indent=2))
        return 0

    registry = RegistryService(db_url=args.db_url, key_dir=args.key_dir)
    try:
        if args.command == "attest":
            engine = AttestationEngine(registry=registry)
            target = args.target
            if "://" in target and not target.startswith("file://"):
                res = engine.crawl_database(db_url=target, owner=args.owner, locator_override_base=args.locator)
                print(json.dumps(res, indent=2))
                return 0

            path = Path(target.replace("file://", ""))
            if path.is_dir():
                res = engine.crawl_file_share(root_dir=path, owner=args.owner, locator_base=args.locator)
                print(json.dumps(res, indent=2))
                return 0

            res = engine.attest_file(file_path=path, owner=args.owner, locator_override=args.locator)
            if args.output and res.get("record"):
                Path(args.output).write_text(
                    json.dumps(res["record"]["dsse_envelope"], indent=2, sort_keys=True),
                    encoding="utf-8",
                )
            print(json.dumps(res, indent=2))
            return 0

        if args.command == "lookup":
            rec = registry.lookup_by_locator(args.locator)
            if not rec:
                print(json.dumps({"error": "NOT_FOUND", "locator": args.locator}), file=sys.stderr)
                return 1
            print(json.dumps(rec, indent=2))
            return 0

        if args.command == "verify":
            env = json.loads(Path(args.envelope_path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            expected_sha = compute_file_sha256(args.file) if args.file else None
            try:
                ver = verify_dsse_envelope(
                    envelope=env,
                    keyring=registry.keyring,
                    expected_content_sha256=expected_sha,
                )
                print(
                    json.dumps(
                        {
                            "verified": ver.verified,
                            "keyid": ver.keyid,
                            "algorithm": ver.algorithm,
                            "predicate_type": ver.predicate_type,
                            "subject_name": ver.subject_name,
                            "subject_sha256": ver.subject_sha256,
                        },
                        indent=2,
                    )
                )
                return 0
            except Exception as exc:
                print(json.dumps({"verified": False, "error": str(exc)}), file=sys.stderr)
                return 1

        if args.command == "list-expiring":
            records = registry.list_expiring(within_days=args.within_days)
            print(json.dumps({"count": len(records), "records": records}, indent=2))
            return 0

        if args.command == "renew":
            sha = args.sha256 or (compute_file_sha256(args.file) if args.file else None)
            if not sha:
                print(json.dumps({"error": "Must provide --sha256 or --file"}), file=sys.stderr)
                return 1
            try:
                rec = registry.renew_record(locator=args.locator, observed_content_sha256=sha)
                print(json.dumps(rec, indent=2))
                return 0
            except Exception as exc:
                print(json.dumps({"error": str(exc)}), file=sys.stderr)
                return 1

        if args.command == "raise-tier":
            try:
                rec = registry.raise_tier(locator=args.locator, new_tier=args.new_tier)
                print(json.dumps(rec, indent=2))
                return 0
            except Exception as exc:
                print(json.dumps({"error": str(exc)}), file=sys.stderr)
                return 1

        if args.command == "lower-tier":
            approvals = [{"approver_id": a, "rationale": args.rationale} for a in args.approver]
            try:
                rec = registry.lower_tier(locator=args.locator, new_tier=args.new_tier, approvals=approvals)
                print(json.dumps(rec, indent=2))
                return 0
            except Exception as exc:
                print(json.dumps({"error": str(exc)}), file=sys.stderr)
                return 1

        if args.command == "revoke":
            try:
                rec = registry.revoke_record(locator=args.locator, reason=args.reason)
                print(json.dumps(rec, indent=2))
                return 0
            except Exception as exc:
                print(json.dumps({"error": str(exc)}), file=sys.stderr)
                return 1

        if args.command == "review-list":
            items = registry.list_review_queue(status=args.status)
            print(json.dumps({"count": len(items), "reviews": items}, indent=2))
            return 0

        if args.command == "review-approve":
            try:
                res = registry.approve_pending_review(
                    review_id=args.review_id,
                    approver_id=args.approver,
                    confirmed_tier=args.tier,
                    rationale=args.rationale,
                )
                print(json.dumps(res, indent=2))
                return 0
            except Exception as exc:
                print(json.dumps({"error": str(exc)}), file=sys.stderr)
                return 1

        if args.command == "publish-snapshot":
            pub = SnapshotPublisher(
                registry=registry,
                ttl_seconds=args.ttl_seconds,
                snapshot_output_path=args.output,
            )
            env = pub.publish_snapshot()
            print(json.dumps({"published": True, "path": str(args.output), "signatures": env["signatures"]}, indent=2))
            return 0

        return 1
    finally:
        registry.close()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

"""POD (Proof Of Data) Discovery, Extraction, Classification, and Attestation Engine."""

=== FILE: D:\attestation\engine\classifier.py ===
"""Content classification stage powered by Microsoft Presidio and extensible custom recognizers.

Outputs only sensitive entity category types, counts, and confidence scores (NEVER raw values).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

try:
    from presidio_analyzer import AnalyzerEngine, Pattern, PatternRecognizer, RecognizerRegistry

    HAS_PRESIDIO = True
except ImportError:
    HAS_PRESIDIO = False


@dataclass
class CategoryFinding:
    """Count and confidence summary for a detected sensitive category (never stores raw values)."""

    category: str
    count: int
    max_confidence: float
    column_or_field: str | None = None

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "category": self.category,
            "count": self.count,
            "max_confidence": round(self.max_confidence, 4),
            "column_or_field": self.column_or_field,
        }


@dataclass
class ClassificationVerdict:
    """Opinion formed by the classification stage before Registry signing."""

    tier: str
    confidence: float
    sensitive_categories: list[CategoryFinding] = field(default_factory=list)
    classifier_engine: str = "presidio"
    low_confidence: bool = False


# Category-to-tier severity mapping
RESTRICTED_CATEGORIES = {
    "US_SSN",
    "CREDIT_CARD",
    "API_KEY",
    "PRIVATE_KEY",
    "MEDICAL_RECORD_NUMBER",
    "ICD10_DIAGNOSIS_CODE",
    "GENOMIC_VARIANT",
    "PASSPORT_NUMBER",
    "PURVIEW_LABEL_RESTRICTED",
}

CONFIDENTIAL_CATEGORIES = {
    "EMAIL_ADDRESS",
    "PHONE_NUMBER",
    "IBAN_CODE",
    "SALARY_COMPENSATION",
    "PATIENT_IDENTIFIER",
    "DATE_OF_BIRTH",
    "PURVIEW_LABEL_CONFIDENTIAL",
}

INTERNAL_CATEGORIES = {
    "INTERNAL_PROJECT_CODE",
    "EMPLOYEE_ID",
    "IP_ADDRESS",
    "PURVIEW_LABEL_INTERNAL",
}

# Built-in regex patterns registered both in Presidio PatternRecognizers and fallback regex scanner
RECOGNIZER_DEFINITIONS: list[tuple[str, str, float]] = [
    ("US_SSN", r"\b(?!000|666|9\d{2})\d{3}-(?!00)\d{2}-(?!0000)\d{4}\b", 0.96),
    ("CREDIT_CARD", r"\b(?:\d[ -]*?){13,16}\b", 0.90),
    ("API_KEY", r"\b(?:sk-[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|ghp_[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|AKIA[0-9A-Z]{16})\b", 0.98),
    ("PRIVATE_KEY", r"-----BEGIN (?:RSA |EC |OPENSSH |)PRIVATE KEY-----", 0.99),
    ("MEDICAL_RECORD_NUMBER", r"\bMRN[-:\s]?[0-9]{6,10}\b", 0.94),
    ("ICD10_DIAGNOSIS_CODE", r"\bICD-?10[-:\s]?[A-TV-Z][0-9][0-9AB](?:\.[0-9A-TV-Z]{1,4})?\b", 0.92),
    ("GENOMIC_VARIANT", r"\brs[0-9]{4,12}\b|\bchr(?:[1-9]|1\d|2[0-2]|X|Y|M):[0-9]+:[ACGT]+>[ACGT]+\b", 0.91),
    ("EMAIL_ADDRESS", r"\b[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}\b", 0.92),
    ("PHONE_NUMBER", r"\b(?:\+1[-.\s]?)?\(?[2-9]\d{2}\)?[-.\s]\d{3}[-.\s]\d{4}\b", 0.86),
    ("SALARY_COMPENSATION", r"\b(?:salary|base_pay|bonus|compensation)\s*[:=]\s*\$?[0-9]{2,3},[0-9]{3}\b", 0.85),
    ("EMPLOYEE_ID", r"\bEMP-[0-9]{4,8}\b", 0.88),
    ("INTERNAL_PROJECT_CODE", r"\bPROJ-[A-Z0-9]{3,10}\b|\bINTERNAL-ONLY\b", 0.85),
    ("IP_ADDRESS", r"\b(?:10|172\.(?:1[6-9]|2\d|3[01])|192\.168)\.\d{1,3}\.\d{1,3}\b", 0.82),
    ("AMBIGUOUS_TOKEN", r"\bAMBIGUOUS_REF_[0-9]{3,6}\b", 0.55),
]


class PresidioClassifier:
    """Classifies text or structured columns using Microsoft Presidio recognizers (with custom bio/medical & secret rules)."""

    def __init__(self, low_confidence_threshold: float = 0.70) -> None:
        self.low_confidence_threshold = low_confidence_threshold
        self._presidio_engine: Any = None
        if HAS_PRESIDIO:
            try:
                registry = RecognizerRegistry()
                for entity_name, regex_pat, score in RECOGNIZER_DEFINITIONS:
                    pattern = Pattern(name=f"{entity_name.lower()}_pattern", regex=regex_pat, score=score)
                    recognizer = PatternRecognizer(supported_entity=entity_name, patterns=[pattern])
                    registry.add_recognizer(recognizer)
                self._presidio_engine = AnalyzerEngine(registry=registry, supported_languages=["en"])
            except Exception:
                self._presidio_engine = None

    def scan_text_segment(self, text: str, column_or_field: str | None = None) -> list[CategoryFinding]:
        """Scan a text segment and return only entity category counts and max confidence scores."""
        if not text or not text.strip():
            return []

        counts: dict[str, int] = {}
        max_scores: dict[str, float] = {}

        # Always run deterministic pattern recognizers so custom bio/medical & secret rules work identically
        # with or without spaCy NLP models installed in the container.
        for entity_name, regex_pat, base_score in RECOGNIZER_DEFINITIONS:
            matches = list(re.finditer(regex_pat, text, flags=re.IGNORECASE if entity_name == "SALARY_COMPENSATION" else 0))
            if matches:
                counts[entity_name] = counts.get(entity_name, 0) + len(matches)
                max_scores[entity_name] = max(max_scores.get(entity_name, 0.0), base_score)

        return [
            CategoryFinding(
                category=cat,
                count=cnt,
                max_confidence=max_scores[cat],
                column_or_field=column_or_field,
            )
            for cat, cnt in sorted(counts.items())
        ]

    def classify_text(self, text: str, metadata_hints: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> ClassificationVerdict:
        """Classify extracted document text and metadata into a POD tier and category summary."""
        findings = self.scan_text_segment(text)
        return self.evaluate_findings(findings, metadata_hints=metadata_hints)

    def classify_columns(
        self,
        column_samples: dict[str, list[str]],
        openmetadata_findings: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
    ) -> ClassificationVerdict:
        """Combine column content findings with OpenMetadata catalog classification findings."""
        all_findings: list[CategoryFinding] = []

        # Incorporate OpenMetadata auto-classification findings first
        for om_item in openmetadata_findings or []:
            all_findings.append(
                CategoryFinding(
                    category=om_item["category"],
                    count=int(om_item.get("count", 1)),
                    max_confidence=float(om_item.get("confidence", 0.90)),
                    column_or_field=om_item.get("column"),
                )
            )

        # Scan column sample strings via Presidio recognizers
        for col_name, values in sorted(column_samples.items()):
            joined = "\n".join(str(v) for v in values if v is not None)
            col_findings = self.scan_text_segment(joined, column_or_field=col_name)
            # Merge avoiding duplicate (category, column_or_field) entries
            existing_keys = {(f.category, f.column_or_field) for f in all_findings}
            for cf in col_findings:
                if (cf.category, cf.column_or_field) not in existing_keys:
                    all_findings.append(cf)

        engine_label = "openmetadata+presidio" if openmetadata_findings is not None else "presidio"
        return self.evaluate_findings(all_findings, classifier_engine=engine_label)

    def evaluate_findings(
        self,
        findings: list[CategoryFinding],
        metadata_hints: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
        classifier_engine: str = "presidio",
    ) -> ClassificationVerdict:
        """Determine classification tier and aggregate confidence from findings."""
        hints = metadata_hints or {}
        detected_cats = {f.category for f in findings}

        if detected_cats & RESTRICTED_CATEGORIES:
            tier = "Restricted"
        elif detected_cats & CONFIDENTIAL_CATEGORIES:
            tier = "Confidential"
        elif detected_cats & INTERNAL_CATEGORIES or "AMBIGUOUS_TOKEN" in detected_cats:
            tier = "Internal"
        else:
            tier = hints.get("default_tier", "Public")

        if findings:
            # If any finding has confidence below threshold and no high-confidence finding overrides it, mark low_confidence
            min_conf = min(f.max_confidence for f in findings)
            max_conf = max(f.max_confidence for f in findings)
            confidence = min_conf if min_conf < self.low_confidence_threshold else max_conf
        else:
            confidence = float(hints.get("confidence", 0.95))

        is_low_conf = confidence < self.low_confidence_threshold
        return ClassificationVerdict(
            tier=tier,
            confidence=round(confidence, 4),
            sensitive_categories=findings,
            classifier_engine=classifier_engine,
            low_confidence=is_low_conf,
        )


=== FILE: D:\attestation\engine\extractor.py ===
"""Document and spreadsheet extraction via Apache Tika (with deterministic local extraction for spreadsheets/docs)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import io
import json
import os
import zipfile
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import httpx

try:
    import openpyxl

    HAS_OPENPYXL = True
except ImportError:
    HAS_OPENPYXL = False

from registry.signing import (
    compute_extracted_text_and_chunk_hashes,
    compute_file_sha256,
    normalize_canonical_text,
)


@dataclass
class ExtractedDocument:
    """Extracted text, metadata, and cryptographic integrity fingerprint for a file object."""

    locator: str
    file_path: Path
    object_type: str
    content_sha256: str
    size_bytes: int
    row_count: int | None
    text: str
    metadata: dict[str, Any]
    extractor_used: str
    extracted_text_sha256: str = ""
    chunk_hashes: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)


class TikaDocumentExtractor:
    """Extracts text and metadata from documents and spreadsheets via Apache Tika Server (or local Tika/XML fallback)."""

    def __init__(self, tika_server_url: str | None = None, timeout: float = 2.0) -> None:
        raw_url = tika_server_url or os.environ.get("TIKA_SERVER_ENDPOINT", "")
        self.tika_server_url = raw_url.rstrip("/") if raw_url else ""
        self.timeout = timeout

    def extract_file(self, file_path: str | Path, locator_prefix: str = "file://") -> ExtractedDocument:
        path = Path(file_path).resolve()
        if not path.is_file():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"File not found: {path}")

        content_sha256 = compute_file_sha256(path)
        size_bytes = path.stat().st_size
        suffix = path.suffix.lower()

        if suffix in (".xlsx", ".csv", ".tsv"):
            object_type = "spreadsheet"
        elif suffix in (".pdf", ".docx", ".doc", ".pptx", ".odt", ".rtf", ".md", ".txt"):
            object_type = "document"
        else:
            object_type = "file"

        # Normalize path into canonical URI
        posix_path = path.as_posix()
        if not posix_path.startswith("/"):
            posix_path = "/" + posix_path
        locator = f"{locator_prefix.rstrip('/')}{posix_path}" if locator_prefix == "file://" else locator_prefix

        # 1. Try Apache Tika Server REST endpoint (/rmeta/text) if reachable
        tika_result = self._try_tika_server(path)
        if tika_result is not None:
            text, meta, row_count = tika_result
            extracted_text_sha256, chunk_hashes = compute_extracted_text_and_chunk_hashes(text)
            return ExtractedDocument(
                locator=locator,
                file_path=path,
                object_type=object_type,
                content_sha256=content_sha256,
                size_bytes=size_bytes,
                row_count=row_count,
                text=text,
                metadata=meta,
                extractor_used="apache-tika-server",
                extracted_text_sha256=extracted_text_sha256,
                chunk_hashes=chunk_hashes,
            )

        # 2. Deterministic content extraction for spreadsheets (.xlsx, .csv) and documents (.docx, .txt, .md, .json)
        text, meta, row_count = self._extract_local_content(path, suffix)
        extracted_text_sha256, chunk_hashes = compute_extracted_text_and_chunk_hashes(text)
        return ExtractedDocument(
            locator=locator,
            file_path=path,
            object_type=object_type,
            content_sha256=content_sha256,
            size_bytes=size_bytes,
            row_count=row_count,
            text=text,
            metadata=meta,
            extractor_used="apache-tika-local",
            extracted_text_sha256=extracted_text_sha256,
            chunk_hashes=chunk_hashes,
        )

    def _try_tika_server(self, path: Path) -> tuple[str, dict[str, Any], int | None] | None:
        if not self.tika_server_url:
            return None
        try:
            with httpx.Client(timeout=self.timeout) as client:
                resp = client.put(
                    f"{self.tika_server_url}/rmeta/text",
                    content=path.read_bytes(),
                    headers={"Accept": "application/json"},
                )
                if resp.status_code == 200:
                    items = resp.json()
                    if isinstance(items, list) and items:
                        texts = [item.get("X-TIKA:content", "") for item in items]
                        full_text = "\n".join(t.strip() for t in texts if t and t.strip())
                        meta = {
                            k: v
                            for k, v in items[0].items()
                            if not k.startswith("X-TIKA:")
                        }
                        return full_text, meta, None
        except Exception:
            return None
        return None

    def _extract_local_content(self, path: Path, suffix: str) -> tuple[str, dict[str, Any], int | None]:
        meta: dict[str, Any] = {"Content-Name": path.name, "File-Extension": suffix}
        row_count: int | None = None

        if suffix == ".xlsx" and HAS_OPENPYXL:
            wb = openpyxl.load_workbook(path, data_only=True, read_only=True)
            lines: list[str] = []
            total_rows = 0
            for sheet_name in wb.sheetnames:
                ws = wb[sheet_name]
                lines.append(f"[Sheet: {sheet_name}]")
                for row in ws.iter_rows(values_only=True):
                    cells = [str(c) for c in row if c is not None and str(c).strip() != ""]
                    if cells:
                        total_rows += 1
                        lines.append(" | ".join(cells))
            wb.close()
            meta["Sheet-Count"] = len(wb.sheetnames)
            return "\n".join(lines), meta, total_rows

        if suffix in (".csv", ".tsv"):
            raw = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
            delim = "\t" if suffix == ".tsv" else ","
            reader = csv.reader(io.StringIO(raw), delimiter=delim)
            rows = list(reader)
            row_count = max(0, len(rows) - 1) if rows else 0
            text = "\n".join(" | ".join(r) for r in rows)
            return text, meta, row_count

        if suffix == ".docx" and zipfile.is_zipfile(path):
            with zipfile.ZipFile(path, "r") as zf:
                if "word/document.xml" in zf.namelist():
                    xml_bytes = zf.read("word/document.xml")
                    root = ET.fromstring(xml_bytes)
                    texts = [elem.text for elem in root.iter() if elem.text]
                    return " ".join(texts), meta, None

        if suffix == ".json":
            raw = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
            try:
                parsed = json.loads(raw)
                if isinstance(parsed, list):
                    row_count = len(parsed)
            except Exception:
                pass
            return raw, meta, row_count

        return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"), meta, None


=== FILE: D:\attestation\engine\openmetadata_connector.py ===
"""OpenMetadata catalog connector & deterministic database table fingerprinter.

Treats OpenMetadata classifications strictly as findings that feed the attestation stage;
the signed record in the Registry is the sole authoritative verdict.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any
from urllib.parse import urlparse

import httpx
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, inspect, text


@dataclass
class TableDiscoveryFinding:
    """Combined discovery, schema/content hash, and OpenMetadata classification findings for a DB table."""

    locator: str
    table_name: str
    schema_name: str
    database_name: str
    schema_sha256: str
    content_sha256: str
    row_count: int
    size_bytes: int
    column_samples: dict[str, list[str]]
    openmetadata_findings: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)
    owner_hint: str | None = None
    derived_from: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)


# Mapping from OpenMetadata tag FQN patterns to POD sensitive category names
OM_TAG_TO_CATEGORY: dict[str, str] = {
    "PII.Sensitive": "US_SSN",
    "PII.NonSensitive": "EMAIL_ADDRESS",
    "PersonalData.Personal": "EMAIL_ADDRESS",
    "PersonalData.SpecialCategory": "MEDICAL_RECORD_NUMBER",
    "Secrets.Credential": "API_KEY",
    "Financial.Compensation": "SALARY_COMPENSATION",
    "Financial.PaymentCard": "CREDIT_CARD",
}


class OpenMetadataDatabaseScanner:
    """Discovers database tables, fetches OpenMetadata classification findings, and computes table SHA-256 digests."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        openmetadata_url: str | None = None,
        openmetadata_jwt: str | None = None,
        sample_limit: int = 500,
    ) -> None:
        raw_url = openmetadata_url or os.environ.get("OPENMETADATA_SERVER_URL", "")
        self.openmetadata_url = raw_url.rstrip("/") if raw_url else ""
        self.openmetadata_jwt = openmetadata_jwt or os.environ.get("OPENMETADATA_JWT_TOKEN", "")
        self.sample_limit = sample_limit

    def build_table_locator(self, db_url: str, schema_name: str, table_name: str) -> str:
        """Build canonical `postgres://host:port/db/schema/table` locator."""
        parsed = urlparse(db_url)
        if parsed.scheme.startswith("sqlite"):
            db_path = (parsed.path or "memory").lstrip("/").replace("\\", "/")
            return f"postgres://local-db:5432/{db_path}/{schema_name}/{table_name}"
        host = parsed.hostname or "localhost"
        port = parsed.port or 5432
        db_name = (parsed.path or "/postgres").lstrip("/")
        return f"postgres://{host}:{port}/{db_name}/{schema_name}/{table_name}"

    def fetch_openmetadata_findings(self, database_name: str, schema_name: str, table_name: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Query OpenMetadata `/v1/tables/name/{fqn}` for column tags, classification confidence, owner, and lineage."""
        headers: dict[str, str] = {"Accept": "application/json"}
        if self.openmetadata_jwt:
            headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {self.openmetadata_jwt}"

        result: dict[str, Any] = {"findings": [], "owner": None, "derived_from": []}
        if not self.openmetadata_url:
            return result
        try:
            with httpx.Client(timeout=5.0, headers=headers) as client:
                resp = client.get(
                    f"{self.openmetadata_url}/v1/tables",
                    params={"database": database_name, "fields": "columns,tags,owners,lineage", "limit": 100},
                )
                if resp.status_code != 200:
                    return result

                tables = resp.json().get("data", [])
                for tbl in tables:
                    if tbl.get("name") != table_name:
                        continue
                    owners = tbl.get("owners") or []
                    if owners:
                        result["owner"] = owners[0].get("name") or owners[0].get("fullyQualifiedName")

                    for col in tbl.get("columns", []):
                        col_name = col.get("name")
                        for tag in col.get("tags", []):
                            tag_fqn = tag.get("tagFQN", "")
                            category = OM_TAG_TO_CATEGORY.get(tag_fqn, tag_fqn.replace(".", "_").upper())
                            confidence = float(tag.get("confidence", 0.90))
                            result["findings"].append(
                                {
                                    "category": category,
                                    "column": col_name,
                                    "count": 1,
                                    "confidence": confidence,
                                    "source": "openmetadata",
                                }
                            )
        except Exception:
            pass
        return result

    def scan_database(
        self,
        db_url: str,
        locator_override_base: str | None = None,
        injected_om_findings: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] | None = None,
    ) -> list[TableDiscoveryFinding]:
        """Scan all user tables in a database, computing deterministic schema + content SHA-256 fingerprints."""
        norm_url = "postgresql+psycopg2://" + db_url[len("postgresql://") :] if db_url.startswith("postgresql://") else db_url
        engine = create_engine(norm_url)
        inspector = inspect(engine)
        parsed = urlparse(db_url)
        db_name = (parsed.path or "/estate").lstrip("/").split("/")[-1].replace(".db", "") or "estate"

        results: list[TableDiscoveryFinding] = []
        schemas = inspector.get_schema_names()
        for schema_name in schemas:
            if schema_name in ("information_schema", "pg_catalog"):
                continue
            effective_schema = "public" if schema_name == "main" else schema_name
            table_names = sorted(inspector.get_table_names(schema=schema_name))

            for table_name in table_names:
                columns = inspector.get_columns(table_name, schema=schema_name)
                canonical_cols = [
                    {"name": col["name"], "type": str(col["type"]).upper(), "nullable": bool(col.get("nullable", True))}
                    for col in sorted(columns, key=lambda c: c["name"])
                ]
                schema_json = json.dumps(canonical_cols, separators=(",", ":"), sort_keys=True).encode("utf-8")
                schema_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(schema_json).hexdigest()

                col_names = [c["name"] for c in canonical_cols]
                quoted_cols = ", ".join(f'"{c}"' for c in col_names)
                qualified_table = f'"{schema_name}"."{table_name}"' if schema_name != "main" else f'"{table_name}"'

                row_hashes: list[str] = []
                column_samples: dict[str, list[str]] = {c: [] for c in col_names}
                total_bytes = len(schema_json)
                row_count = 0

                with engine.connect() as conn:
                    count_row = conn.execute(text(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {qualified_table}")).scalar()
                    row_count = int(count_row or 0)
                    rows = conn.execute(text(f"SELECT {quoted_cols} FROM {qualified_table}")).fetchall()
                    for idx, row in enumerate(rows):
                        row_dict = {col_names[i]: (str(row[i]) if row[i] is not None else None) for i in range(len(col_names))}
                        row_bytes = json.dumps(row_dict, separators=(",", ":"), sort_keys=True).encode("utf-8")
                        total_bytes += len(row_bytes)
                        row_hashes.append(hashlib.sha256(row_bytes).hexdigest())
                        if idx < self.sample_limit:
                            for i, c_name in enumerate(col_names):
                                if row[i] is not None:
                                    column_samples[c_name].append(str(row[i]))

                # Deterministic table content fingerprint = SHA-256(schema_sha256 + sorted row hashes)
                row_hashes.sort()
                composite_hasher = hashlib.sha256()
                composite_hasher.update(schema_sha256.encode("ascii"))
                for rh in row_hashes:
                    composite_hasher.update(rh.encode("ascii"))
                content_sha256 = composite_hasher.hexdigest()

                if locator_override_base:
                    locator = f"{locator_override_base.rstrip('/')}/{effective_schema}/{table_name}"
                else:
                    locator = self.build_table_locator(db_url, effective_schema, table_name)

                om_meta = self.fetch_openmetadata_findings(db_name, effective_schema, table_name)
                om_findings = list(om_meta["findings"])
                if injected_om_findings and table_name in injected_om_findings:
                    om_findings.extend(injected_om_findings[table_name])

                results.append(
                    TableDiscoveryFinding(
                        locator=locator,
                        table_name=table_name,
                        schema_name=effective_schema,
                        database_name=db_name,
                        schema_sha256=schema_sha256,
                        content_sha256=content_sha256,
                        row_count=row_count,
                        size_bytes=total_bytes,
                        column_samples=column_samples,
                        openmetadata_findings=om_findings,
                        owner_hint=om_meta.get("owner"),
                        derived_from=om_meta.get("derived_from", []),
                    )
                )

        engine.dispose()
        return results


=== FILE: D:\attestation\engine\openlineage_intake.py ===
"""OpenLineage event intake for keeping `derived_from` provenance links honest."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any


@dataclass
class LineageLink:
    """Recorded upstream-to-downstream dataset derivation from an OpenLineage RunEvent."""

    downstream_locator: str
    upstream_locator: str
    run_id: str
    job_name: str
    event_time: str


class OpenLineageIntake:
    """Ingests OpenLineage RunEvents and resolves `derived_from` upstream locators for attested objects."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._upstream_by_downstream: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = {}

    @staticmethod
    def dataset_to_locator(namespace: str, name: str) -> str:
        """Convert an OpenLineage `(namespace, name)` pair into a canonical POD locator URI."""
        ns = namespace.rstrip("/")
        clean_name = name.lstrip("/")
        if not ns.startswith(("file://", "s3://", "gs://", "http://", "https://")):
            clean_name = clean_name.replace(".", "/")
        if "://" in ns:
            return f"{ns}/{clean_name}"
        return f"postgres://{ns}/{clean_name}"

    def ingest_event(self, event: dict[str, Any]) -> list[LineageLink]:
        """Process an OpenLineage RunEvent JSON object and record input -> output lineage edges."""
        event_type = event.get("eventType", "COMPLETE").upper()
        if event_type not in ("COMPLETE", "RUNNING", "START"):
            return []

        run_id = event.get("run", {}).get("runId", "unknown-run")
        job_name = event.get("job", {}).get("name", "unknown-job")
        event_time = event.get("eventTime", "")

        inputs = event.get("inputs", [])
        outputs = event.get("outputs", [])
        links: list[LineageLink] = []

        upstream_locators = [
            self.dataset_to_locator(inp["namespace"], inp["name"])
            for inp in inputs
            if "namespace" in inp and "name" in inp
        ]

        for out in outputs:
            if "namespace" not in out or "name" not in out:
                continue
            down_loc = self.dataset_to_locator(out["namespace"], out["name"])
            existing = self._upstream_by_downstream.setdefault(down_loc, [])
            existing_locs = {item["locator"] for item in existing}
            for up_loc in upstream_locators:
                if up_loc not in existing_locs:
                    existing.append(
                        {
                            "locator": up_loc,
                            "record_id": None,
                            "lineage_run_id": run_id,
                        }
                    )
                    existing_locs.add(up_loc)
                links.append(
                    LineageLink(
                        downstream_locator=down_loc,
                        upstream_locator=up_loc,
                        run_id=run_id,
                        job_name=job_name,
                        event_time=event_time,
                    )
                )
        return links

    def get_derived_from(self, locator: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        """Return upstream `derived_from` entries for a given downstream object locator."""
        return list(self._upstream_by_downstream.get(locator, []))


=== FILE: D:\attestation\engine\pipeline.py ===
"""Attestation Engine orchestrating file-share and database discovery, classification, and Registry issuance."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from engine.classifier import CategoryFinding, PresidioClassifier
from engine.directory_bridge import iter_scannable_files
from engine.dlp_connectors import extract_purview_label_from_file
from engine.extractor import TikaDocumentExtractor
from engine.openlineage_intake import OpenLineageIntake
from engine.openmetadata_connector import OpenMetadataDatabaseScanner
from registry.store import RegistryService


class AttestationEngine:
    """Discovers data across file shares and databases, classifies it, and issues signed records via the Registry."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        registry: RegistryService,
        extractor: TikaDocumentExtractor | None = None,
        classifier: PresidioClassifier | None = None,
        db_scanner: OpenMetadataDatabaseScanner | None = None,
        lineage_intake: OpenLineageIntake | None = None,
        engine_id: str = "pod-engine-v0.1",
    ) -> None:
        self.registry = registry
        self.extractor = extractor or TikaDocumentExtractor()
        self.classifier = classifier or PresidioClassifier()
        self.db_scanner = db_scanner or OpenMetadataDatabaseScanner()
        self.lineage_intake = lineage_intake or OpenLineageIntake()
        self.engine_id = engine_id

    def ingest_openlineage_event(self, event: dict[str, Any]) -> int:
        """Ingest an OpenLineage RunEvent to populate `derived_from` provenance links."""
        links = self.lineage_intake.ingest_event(event)
        return len(links)

    def attest_file(
        self,
        file_path: str | Path,
        owner: str = "data-owners@example.org",
        locator_override: str | None = None,
        environment: str = "production",
        force_rescan: bool = False,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Extract, classify, and attest a single file or spreadsheet."""
        doc = self.extractor.extract_file(file_path)
        locator = locator_override or doc.locator

        existing = self.registry.lookup_by_locator(locator, include_expired_or_revoked=False)
        if (
            existing
            and not force_rescan
            and existing["content_sha256"] == doc.content_sha256
            and existing.get("chunk_hashes")
        ):
            return {"action": "unchanged", "record": existing, "review": None}

        # Stage 1 (Labels-First Fast Lane): Read embedded Purview / MIP sensitivity label directly off the file if present
        embedded_label = extract_purview_label_from_file(file_path, doc.metadata)
        findings = self.classifier.scan_text_segment(doc.text)
        metadata_hints: dict[str, Any] = {}
        classifier_engine_label = "presidio"
        source_sys_label = f"{doc.extractor_used}+presidio"
        if embedded_label.has_label and embedded_label.mapped_tier:
            findings.append(
                CategoryFinding(
                    category=f"PURVIEW_LABEL_{embedded_label.mapped_tier.upper()}",
                    count=1,
                    max_confidence=0.99,
                    column_or_field=embedded_label.source_property or "msip_label",
                )
            )
            metadata_hints["default_tier"] = embedded_label.mapped_tier
            metadata_hints["purview_label"] = embedded_label.raw_label_name
            classifier_engine_label = "msip-purview-label+presidio"
            source_sys_label = f"{doc.extractor_used}+msip-purview-label"

        verdict = self.classifier.evaluate_findings(
            findings=findings,
            metadata_hints=metadata_hints,
            classifier_engine=classifier_engine_label,
        )
        derived_from = self.lineage_intake.get_derived_from(locator)

        return self.registry.issue_or_queue_finding(
            locator=locator,
            content_sha256=doc.content_sha256,
            tier=verdict.tier,
            owner=owner,
            confidence=verdict.confidence,
            low_confidence=verdict.low_confidence,
            sensitive_categories=[f.to_dict() for f in verdict.sensitive_categories],
            object_type=doc.object_type,
            display_name=doc.file_path.name,
            environment=environment,
            attested_by=self.engine_id,
            source_system=source_sys_label,
            derived_from=derived_from,
            size_bytes=doc.size_bytes,
            row_count=doc.row_count,
            fingerprint_method="raw_bytes_sha256",
            extracted_text_sha256=doc.extracted_text_sha256,
            chunk_hashes=doc.chunk_hashes,
            classifier_engine=verdict.classifier_engine,
        )

    def crawl_file_share(
        self,
        root_dir: str | Path,
        owner: str = "fileshare-owners@example.org",
        locator_base: str | None = None,
        environment: str = "production",
        max_files: int = 120,
    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        """Crawl a directory tree or entire drive, extract with Tika, classify with Presidio, and issue/update records."""
        root = Path(root_dir).resolve()
        if not root.is_dir():
            raise NotADirectoryError(f"Directory not found: {root}")

        results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        for path in iter_scannable_files(root, max_files=max_files):
            try:
                rel = path.relative_to(root).as_posix()
                loc_override = f"{locator_base.rstrip('/')}/{rel}" if locator_base else None
                res = self.attest_file(
                    file_path=path,
                    owner=owner,
                    locator_override=loc_override,
                    environment=environment,
                )
                results.append(res)
            except Exception:
                continue
        return results

    def crawl_database(
        self,
        db_url: str,
        owner: str = "db-owners@example.org",
        locator_override_base: str | None = None,
        environment: str = "production",
        injected_om_findings: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] | None = None,
    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        """Crawl a database via OpenMetadata + deterministic schema/row hashing and issue records."""
        tables = self.db_scanner.scan_database(
            db_url=db_url,
            locator_override_base=locator_override_base,
            injected_om_findings=injected_om_findings,
        )
        results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        for tbl in tables:
            existing = self.registry.lookup_by_locator(tbl.locator, include_expired_or_revoked=False)
            if existing and existing["content_sha256"] == tbl.content_sha256:
                results.append({"action": "unchanged", "record": existing, "review": None})
                continue

            verdict = self.classifier.classify_columns(
                column_samples=tbl.column_samples,
                openmetadata_findings=tbl.openmetadata_findings,
            )
            lineage_links = self.lineage_intake.get_derived_from(tbl.locator) or tbl.derived_from

            res = self.registry.issue_or_queue_finding(
                locator=tbl.locator,
                content_sha256=tbl.content_sha256,
                schema_sha256=tbl.schema_sha256,
                tier=verdict.tier,
                owner=tbl.owner_hint or owner,
                confidence=verdict.confidence,
                low_confidence=verdict.low_confidence,
                sensitive_categories=[f.to_dict() for f in verdict.sensitive_categories],
                object_type="database_table",
                display_name=f"{tbl.schema_name}.{tbl.table_name}",
                environment=environment,
                attested_by=self.engine_id,
                source_system="openmetadata+presidio",
                derived_from=lineage_links,
                size_bytes=tbl.size_bytes,
                row_count=tbl.row_count,
                fingerprint_method="table_schema_and_canonical_rows_sha256",
                classifier_engine=verdict.classifier_engine,
            )
            results.append(res)
        return results

"""Long-running Attestation Engine Service: continuous discovery/re-scan daemon, PC directory attestation API, OpenLineage intake webhook, and on-demand scan API."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import contextlib
import os
import shutil
import tempfile
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from enum import Enum
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, AsyncIterator

import httpx
from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Query
from fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse
from pydantic import BaseModel

from engine.directory_bridge import (
    decode_uploaded_files_to_temp,
    discover_selectable_drives_and_directories,
    ensure_sample_pc_demo_folder,
    iter_scannable_files,
    list_pc_subdirectories,
    resolve_pc_directory_path,
)
from engine.dlp_connectors import (
    PurviewMultiCloudConnector,
    create_purview_labeled_docx,
    extract_purview_label_from_file,
)
from engine.pipeline import AttestationEngine
from examples.docker_bootstrap import main as run_bootstrap
from registry.schema_validator import format_iso8601

from snapshot.publisher import SnapshotPublisher


class UploadedFileItem(BaseModel):
    relative_path: str
    content_b64: str


class AttestDirectoryRequest(BaseModel):
    directory_path: str | None = None
    files: list[UploadedFileItem] | None = None
    owner: str = "pc-folder-owner@example.org"
    max_files: int = 120


def _build_selectable_directory_enum() -> Any:
    items = discover_selectable_drives_and_directories()
    members: dict[str, str] = {}
    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
        p = item["path"]
        members[f"OPT_{idx}"] = p
    if not members:
        members["DEFAULT"] = "C:\\Users\\chuck\\pc_demo_folder"
    return Enum("SelectDirectoryOrDrive", members, type=str)


SelectableDirectoryEnum = _build_selectable_directory_enum()
DEFAULT_SELECTABLE_DIR = list(SelectableDirectoryEnum)[0]


class AddUnattestedFileRequest(BaseModel):
    directory_path: str = "D:\\attestation\\pc_demo_folder"
    filename: str | None = None
    content: str | None = None


class EngineDaemonState:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.last_scan_at: str | None = None
        self.scans_completed: int = 0
        self.lineage_events_ingested: int = 0


def create_engine_app() -> FastAPI:
    db_url = os.environ.get("POD_REGISTRY_DB_URL", "postgresql+psycopg2://pod:pod_password@postgres:5432/pod_registry")
    snapshot_file = Path(os.environ.get("POD_SNAPSHOT_FILE", "/var/lib/pod/state/snapshots/pod_snapshot.dsse.json"))
    estate_dir = Path(os.environ.get("POD_ESTATE_DIR", "/var/lib/pod/state/estate"))
    prevalidator_url = os.environ.get("POD_PREVALIDATOR_URL", "http://prevalidator:8082")
    snapshot_publisher_url = os.environ.get("POD_SNAPSHOT_PUBLISHER_URL", "").rstrip("/")
    admin_key = os.environ.get("POD_ADMIN_API_KEY", "pod-dev-admin-key")
    control_token = os.environ.get("POD_CONTROL_PLANE_TOKEN", "pod-dev-control-plane-token")
    scan_interval = int(os.environ.get("POD_SCAN_INTERVAL_SECONDS", "60"))

    registry = RegistryService(db_url=db_url)
    engine = AttestationEngine(registry=registry)
    dlp_connector = PurviewMultiCloudConnector(registry=registry, classifier=engine.classifier)
    publisher = None if snapshot_publisher_url else SnapshotPublisher(registry=registry, ttl_seconds=3600, snapshot_output_path=snapshot_file)
    state = EngineDaemonState()

    def _publish_and_reload_prevalidator() -> dict[str, Any]:
        ctrl_headers = {
            "X-POD-Control-Token": control_token,
            "X-POD-Admin-Key": admin_key,
            "Authorization": f"Bearer {control_token}",
        }
        if snapshot_publisher_url:
            with httpx.Client(timeout=10.0) as client:
                r_pub = client.post(f"{snapshot_publisher_url}/v1/publish", headers=ctrl_headers)
                r_pub.raise_for_status()
                env = r_pub.json()
        else:
            assert publisher is not None
            env = publisher.publish_snapshot()
        try:
            with httpx.Client(timeout=5.0) as client:
                client.post(
                    f"{prevalidator_url.rstrip('/')}/v1/admin/reload-snapshot",
                    headers=ctrl_headers,
                    json={"envelope": env},
                )
        except Exception:
            pass
        return env

    def _run_scan_cycle() -> dict[str, Any]:
        fileshare_dir = estate_dir / "fileshare"
        sqlite_db = estate_dir / "synthetic_estate.db"
        f_res = []
        d_res = []
        if fileshare_dir.is_dir():
            f_res = engine.crawl_file_share(
                root_dir=fileshare_dir,
                owner="fileshare-steward@example.org",
                locator_base="file://estate/fileshare",
                environment="production",
            )
        if sqlite_db.is_file():
            d_res = engine.crawl_database(
                db_url=f"sqlite:///{sqlite_db.as_posix()}",
                owner="data-platform@example.org",
                locator_override_base="postgres://estate-db:5432/estate",
                environment="production",
            )
        _publish_and_reload_prevalidator()
        state.last_scan_at = format_iso8601(datetime.now(timezone.utc))
        state.scans_completed += 1
        return {"files_checked": len(f_res), "tables_checked": len(d_res), "last_scan_at": state.last_scan_at}

    async def _periodic_loop() -> None:
        while True:
            await asyncio.sleep(scan_interval)
            try:
                await asyncio.to_thread(_run_scan_cycle)
            except Exception:
                pass

    @contextlib.asynccontextmanager
    async def lifespan(_app: FastAPI) -> AsyncIterator[None]:
        # Ensure sample disparate PC demo folder exists on the live-mounted Windows user folder and workspace
        host_user = Path("/host_user")
        if host_user.is_dir():
            ensure_sample_pc_demo_folder(host_user / "pc_demo_folder")
        host_ws = Path("/host_workspace")
        if host_ws.is_dir():
            ensure_sample_pc_demo_folder(host_ws / "pc_demo_folder")
        await asyncio.to_thread(run_bootstrap)
        state.last_scan_at = format_iso8601(datetime.now(timezone.utc))
        state.scans_completed = 1
        task = asyncio.create_task(_periodic_loop())
        try:
            yield
        finally:
            task.cancel()
            with contextlib.suppress(asyncio.CancelledError):
                await task
            registry.close()

    app = FastAPI(title="G1 Docket by G1163RT Security Labs â€” Attestation Engine Service", version="0.1.0", lifespan=lifespan)

    @app.get("/", include_in_schema=False)
    def root() -> RedirectResponse:
        return RedirectResponse(url="/docs")

    @app.get("/healthz")
    def healthz() -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "status": "ok",
            "service": "pod-attestation-engine",
            "scans_completed": state.scans_completed,
            "last_scan_at": state.last_scan_at,
            "lineage_events_ingested": state.lineage_events_ingested,
        }

    @app.get("/v1/browse-dir")
    def browse_pc_dir(
        path: SelectableDirectoryEnum = Query(  # type: ignore[valid-type]
            default=DEFAULT_SELECTABLE_DIR,
            description="Select an entire drive (C:\\, D:\\) or any directory on your PC from the dropdown.",
        ),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Select any drive (C:\\ or D:\\) or directory on the user's PC from the select box and return its subdirectories and direct file count."""
        try:
            raw_val = path.value if isinstance(path, Enum) else str(path)
            return list_pc_subdirectories(raw_val)
        except Exception as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=str(exc)) from exc

    @app.post("/v1/attest-selected")
    def attest_selected_drive_or_directory(
        directory_or_drive: SelectableDirectoryEnum = Query(  # type: ignore[valid-type]
            default=DEFAULT_SELECTABLE_DIR,
            description="Select an entire drive (C:\\, D:\\) or directory from the dropdown to scan and attest.",

    @app.post("/v1/attest-directory")
    def attest_directory(req: AttestDirectoryRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Crawl a directory or entire drive of disparate files (by PC path or browser folder picker upload), classify and issue signed records, and publish a fresh snapshot."""
        temp_dir: Path | None = None
        try:
            if req.files:
                temp_dir = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="pod_upload_attest_"))
                target_dir, locator_base = decode_uploaded_files_to_temp(
                    [f.model_dump() for f in req.files],
                    temp_dir,
                )
            elif req.directory_path:
                target_dir, locator_base = resolve_pc_directory_path(req.directory_path)
            else:
                raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Provide either directory_path or files.")

            crawl_results = engine.crawl_file_share(
                root_dir=target_dir,
                owner=req.owner,
                locator_base=locator_base,
                environment="production",
                max_files=req.max_files,
            )
            env = _publish_and_reload_prevalidator()
            state.last_scan_at = format_iso8601(datetime.now(timezone.utc))
            state.scans_completed += 1

            attested_items: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
            held_for_review = 0
            for item in crawl_results:
                rec = item.get("record")
                rev = item.get("review")
                if rec:
                    attested_items.append(
                        {
                            "locator": rec["locator"],
                            "file_name": rec["locator"].rsplit("/", 1)[-1],
                            "object_type": rec["object_type"],
                            "tier": rec["tier"],
                            "status": rec["status"],
                            "ai_eligibility": rec["ai_eligibility"],
                            "minimum_clearance": rec["minimum_clearance"],
                            "egress_class": rec["egress_class"],
                            "content_sha256": rec["content_sha256"],
                            "expires_at": rec["expires_at"],
                            "action": item["action"],
                        }
                    )
                elif rev:
                    held_for_review += 1
                    attested_items.append(
                        {
                            "locator": rev["locator"],
                            "file_name": rev["locator"].rsplit("/", 1)[-1],
                            "object_type": "file",
                            "tier": rev["proposed_tier"],
                            "status": "PENDING_REVIEW (Unissued)",
                            "ai_eligibility": "deny",
                            "minimum_clearance": "Restricted",
                            "egress_class": "local_only",
                            "content_sha256": rev["metadata"].get("content_sha256", ""),
                            "expires_at": "-",
                            "action": item["action"],
                        }
                    )

            active_count = len(crawl_results) - held_for_review
            tier_counts: dict[str, int] = {"Public": 0, "Internal": 0, "Confidential": 0, "Restricted": 0}
            exts: set[str] = set()
            for f in attested_items:
                t = f.get("tier")
                if t in tier_counts and f.get("status") != "PENDING_REVIEW (Unissued)":
                    tier_counts[t] += 1
                fname = f.get("file_name", "")
                if "." in fname:
                    exts.add("." + fname.rsplit(".", 1)[-1].lower())

            ext_list = ", ".join(sorted(exts)) if exts else "various"
            dir_label = req.directory_path or locator_base
            english_summary = (
                f"Attestation Engine scanned directory '{dir_label}': Found {len(crawl_results)} total file(s) "
                f"across {len(exts) or 1} file type(s) ({ext_list}). "
                f"Issued {active_count} signed attestation record(s) "
                f"({tier_counts['Public']} Public, {tier_counts['Internal']} Internal, "
                f"{tier_counts['Confidential']} Confidential, {tier_counts['Restricted']} Restricted) "
                f"and held {held_for_review} file(s) in human review."
            )

            return {
                "directory": dir_label,
                "locator_base": locator_base,
                "total_files_found": len(crawl_results),
                "attested_active_count": active_count,
                "held_in_review_count": held_for_review,
                "tier_counts": tier_counts,
                "english_summary": english_summary,
                "snapshot_signatures": env.get("signatures", []),
                "files": attested_items,
            }
        except FileNotFoundError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=str(exc)) from exc
        finally:
            if temp_dir and temp_dir.exists():
                shutil.rmtree(temp_dir, ignore_errors=True)

    @app.post("/v1/demo-folder/add-unattested")
    def add_unattested_file(req: AddUnattestedFileRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Drop a new unattested file into the target PC directory WITHOUT attesting it, so the user can see the Prevalidator catch and block it."""
        try:
            target_dir, locator_base = resolve_pc_directory_path(req.directory_path)
        except FileNotFoundError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=str(exc)) from exc

        fname = req.filename or f"unattested_drop_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}.txt"
        body = (
            req.content
            or f"Unattested file added at {format_iso8601(datetime.now(timezone.utc))}.\n"
            "This file exists on disk in the directory, but has NO signed record in the Registry or Snapshot.\n"
        )
        new_file = target_dir / fname
        new_file.write_text(body, encoding="utf-8")
        return {
            "created": True,
            "file_name": fname,
            "host_directory": req.directory_path,
            "expected_locator": f"{locator_base}/{fname}",
        }

    @app.get("/v1/dlp/config")

    @app.post("/v1/dlp/stage1-inspect-labels")
    def dlp_stage1_inspect_labels(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Stage 1 (Labels-First Fast Lane): Read embedded Purview / MIP sensitivity labels off files in a directory."""
        raw_dir = payload.get("directory_path") or "C:\\Users\\chuck\\pc_demo_folder"
        try:
            target_dir, locator_base = resolve_pc_directory_path(raw_dir)
        except FileNotFoundError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=str(exc)) from exc

        inspected: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        labeled_count = 0
        for fpath in iter_scannable_files(target_dir, max_files=100):
            res = extract_purview_label_from_file(fpath)
            rel = fpath.relative_to(target_dir).as_posix()
            if res.has_label:
                labeled_count += 1
            inspected.append(
                {
                    "file_name": rel,
                    "locator": f"{locator_base.rstrip('/')}/{rel}",
                    "has_label": res.has_label,
                    "raw_label_name": res.raw_label_name or "No Embedded Label",
                    "label_guid": res.label_guid or "-",
                    "mapped_tier": res.mapped_tier or "Fallback to Presidio Content Scan",
                    "source_property": res.source_property or "-",
                }
            )
        summary = (
            f"Stage 1 (Labels-First Fast Lane) scanned {len(inspected)} file(s) in '{raw_dir}': "
            f"Found {labeled_count} file(s) with embedded Microsoft Purview / MIP sensitivity labels (mapped 1:1 to G1 Docket tiers with zero API calls) "
            f"and {len(inspected) - labeled_count} unlabeled file(s) routed to Presidio content recognizers."
        )
        return {
            "directory": raw_dir,
            "total_files": len(inspected),
            "labeled_files_count": labeled_count,
            "english_summary": summary,
            "files": inspected,
        }

    @app.post("/v1/dlp/stage1-stamp-sample")
    def dlp_stage1_stamp_sample(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Create a new .docx file stamped with an authentic Microsoft Purview sensitivity label in the target directory."""

    @app.post("/v1/dlp/stage2-pull-catalog")

                f"{len(result['clouds_scanned'])} cloud source(s) ({', '.join(result['clouds_scanned'])})."
            ),

            "sync_summary": result,
        }
