"""Host directory path translator, interactive PC directory browser, and disparate file generator."""

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import hashlib
import os
import re
import zipfile
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import openpyxl

SKIP_DIR_NAMES: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
    {
        "$recycle.bin",
        "$sysreset",
        "$windows.~bt",
        "$windows.~ws",
        "system volume information",
        "windows",
        "program files",
        "program files (x86)",
        "programdata",
        "perflogs",
        "recovery",
        "onedrivetemp",
        "intel",
        "esd",
        "appdata",
        "application data",
        "local settings",
        "cookies",
        "nethood",
        "printhood",
        "recent",
        "sendto",
        "start menu",
        "templates",
        "saved games",
        "searches",
        "documents and settings",
        "node_modules",
        ".venv",
        "venv",
        "__pycache__",
        ".git",
        ".pytest_cache",
        "anaconda3",
        "anaconda3test",
        ".conda",
        ".anaconda",
        ".cache",
        ".local",
        ".npm",
        ".docker",
        ".kube",
        ".lmstudio",
        "go",
        "python314",
        "pod_attestation.egg-info",
    }
)

SKIP_FILE_EXTENSIONS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
    {
        ".dll",
        ".exe",
        ".sys",
        ".msi",
        ".cab",
        ".zip",
        ".tar",
        ".gz",
        ".7z",
        ".whl",
        ".pyc",
        ".pyd",
        ".so",
        ".iso",
        ".vhd",
        ".vhdx",
        ".blf",
        ".regtrans-ms",
        ".tmp",
    }
)

MAX_SCANNABLE_FILE_BYTES = 8 * 1024 * 1024  # 8 MB cap per file during directory/drive scans


def iter_scannable_files(root: Path, max_files: int = 120) -> list[Path]:
    """Safely walk a directory or an entire drive (`C:\\`, `D:\\`), skipping OS/system/virtualenv folders and locked files."""
    collected: list[Path] = []
    if not root.is_dir():
        return collected

    for dirpath, dirnames, filenames in os.walk(root, topdown=True, onerror=lambda _e: None):
        # Prune skipped, hidden, and system directories in-place
        kept_dirs: list[str] = []
        for d in sorted(dirnames, key=str.lower):
            if d.startswith(".") or d.startswith("$"):
                continue
            if d.lower() in SKIP_DIR_NAMES:
                continue
            kept_dirs.append(d)
        dirnames[:] = kept_dirs

        for fname in sorted(filenames, key=str.lower):
            if fname.startswith(".") or fname.startswith("$") or fname.startswith("~"):
                continue
            lower_f = fname.lower()
            if lower_f.startswith("ntuser.") or lower_f in ("hiberfil.sys", "pagefile.sys", "swapfile.sys", "dumpstack.log"):
                continue
            ext = Path(fname).suffix.lower()
            if ext in SKIP_FILE_EXTENSIONS:
                continue
            fpath = Path(dirpath) / fname
            try:
                if not fpath.is_file() or fpath.is_symlink():
                    continue
                size = fpath.stat().st_size
                if size > MAX_SCANNABLE_FILE_BYTES:
                    continue
            except OSError:
                continue
            collected.append(fpath)
            if len(collected) >= max_files:
                return collected

    return collected


def discover_selectable_drives_and_directories() -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    """Return all selectable drives (`C:\\`, `D:\\`) and user/project directories on the PC for `<select>` dropdowns."""
    items: list[dict[str, str]] = []
    seen_paths: set[str] = set()

    def _add(path_str: str, label: str) -> None:
        key = path_str.lower().rstrip("\\")
        if key not in seen_paths:
            seen_paths.add(key)
            items.append({"path": path_str, "label": label})

    # 1. Featured Demo & User Directories
    _add(
        "C:\\Users\\chuck\\pc_demo_folder",
        "ðŸ“ C:\\Users\\chuck\\pc_demo_folder â€” Sample Disparate Files (.md, .json, .txt, .docx, .xlsx, .csv)",
    )
    _add(
        "D:\\attestation\\pc_demo_folder",
        "ðŸ“ D:\\attestation\\pc_demo_folder â€” Sample Disparate Files",
    )

    # 2. Entire Drives
    _add("C:\\", "ðŸ–´ C:\\ â€” Entire C: Drive (Recursive Data Scan)")
    _add("D:\\", "ðŸ–´ D:\\ â€” Entire D: Drive (Recursive Data Scan)")

    # 3. Standard User Profile Directories
    user_mount = Path("/host_user") if Path("/host_user").is_dir() else Path("C:/Users/chuck")
    if user_mount.is_dir():
        _add("C:\\Users\\chuck", "ðŸ“ C:\\Users\\chuck (User Home Root)")
        for std_sub in ("Desktop", "Documents", "Downloads", "OneDrive"):
            if (user_mount / std_sub).is_dir():
                _add(f"C:\\Users\\chuck\\{std_sub}", f"ðŸ“ C:\\Users\\chuck\\{std_sub}")

    # 4. Discover Project Directories in D:\attestation and D:\
    d_ws = Path("/host_workspace") if Path("/host_workspace").is_dir() else Path("D:/attestation")
    if d_ws.is_dir():
        _add("D:\\attestation", "ðŸ“ D:\\attestation (POD Framework Workspace)")
        try:
            for child in sorted(d_ws.iterdir(), key=lambda p: p.name.lower()):
                if child.is_dir() and not child.name.startswith(".") and child.name.lower() not in SKIP_DIR_NAMES:
                    _add(f"D:\\attestation\\{child.name}", f"ðŸ“ D:\\attestation\\{child.name}")
        except OSError:
            pass

    d_root = Path("/host_d") if Path("/host_d").is_dir() else Path("D:/")
    if d_root.is_dir():
        try:
            for child in sorted(d_root.iterdir(), key=lambda p: p.name.lower()):
                if child.is_dir() and not child.name.startswith(".") and child.name.lower() not in SKIP_DIR_NAMES:
                    _add(f"D:\\{child.name}", f"ðŸ“ D:\\{child.name}")
        except OSError:
            pass

    # 5. Discover Top-Level Project Directories on C:\
    c_root = Path("/host_c") if Path("/host_c").is_dir() else Path("C:/")
    if c_root.is_dir():
        try:
            for child in sorted(c_root.iterdir(), key=lambda p: p.name.lower()):
                try:
                    if (
                        child.is_dir()
                        and not child.name.startswith(".")
                        and not child.name.startswith("$")
                        and child.name.lower() not in SKIP_DIR_NAMES
                        and child.name.lower() != "users"
                    ):
                        _add(f"C:\\{child.name}", f"ðŸ“ C:\\{child.name}")
                except OSError:
                    continue
        except OSError:
            pass

    # 6. Discover User Project Directories in C:\Users\chuck
    if user_mount.is_dir():
        try:
            for child in sorted(user_mount.iterdir(), key=lambda p: p.name.lower()):
                try:
                    if (
                        child.is_dir()
                        and not child.name.startswith(".")
                        and not child.name.startswith("$")
                        and child.name.lower() not in SKIP_DIR_NAMES
                    ):
                        _add(f"C:\\Users\\chuck\\{child.name}", f"ðŸ“ C:\\Users\\chuck\\{child.name}")
                except OSError:
                    continue
        except OSError:
            pass

    return items


def resolve_pc_directory_path(raw_path: str) -> tuple[Path, str]:
    """Translate any Windows host path (`D:\\...`, `C:\\...`) to the mounted path inside the container
    (or direct path on host), returning `(resolved_path, canonical_locator_prefix)`.
    """
    cleaned = raw_path.strip().strip('"').strip("'")
    norm_slashes = cleaned.replace("\\", "/")

    # Compute a deterministic canonical locator base from the full normalized Windows path
    slug = re.sub(r"[^a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+", "_", norm_slashes.strip("/")).strip("_") or "root"
    locator_base = f"file://pc-dir/{slug}"

    lower_norm = norm_slashes.lower().rstrip("/")

    # Alias pc_demo_folder on either C:\Users\chuck\pc_demo_folder or D:\attestation\pc_demo_folder
    # to the live-mounted /host_user/pc_demo_folder inside Docker so Windows Explorer and Docker share the exact same files
    if lower_norm in ("d:/attestation/pc_demo_folder", "c:/users/chuck/pc_demo_folder"):
        locator_base = "file://pc-dir/pc_demo_folder"
        live_user_demo = Path("/host_user/pc_demo_folder")
        if live_user_demo.exists():
            return live_user_demo, locator_base

    # 1. Direct path exists
    direct = Path(cleaned)
    if direct.exists():
        return direct, locator_base

    # 2. Translate D:/attestation/... -> /host_workspace/...
    if lower_norm.startswith("d:/attestation"):
        sub = norm_slashes[len("d:/attestation") :].lstrip("/")
        candidate = Path("/host_workspace") / sub if sub else Path("/host_workspace")
        if candidate.exists():
            return candidate, locator_base

    # 3. Translate any D:/... -> /host_d/...
    if lower_norm.startswith("d:"):
        sub = norm_slashes[2:].lstrip("/")
        candidate = Path("/host_d") / sub if sub else Path("/host_d")
        if candidate.exists():
            return candidate, locator_base

    # 4. Translate C:/Users/chuck/... -> /host_user/...
    if lower_norm.startswith("c:/users/chuck"):
        sub = norm_slashes[len("c:/users/chuck") :].lstrip("/")
        candidate = Path("/host_user") / sub if sub else Path("/host_user")
        if candidate.exists():
            return candidate, locator_base

    # 5. Translate any C:/... -> /host_c/...
    if lower_norm.startswith("c:"):
        sub = norm_slashes[2:].lstrip("/")
        candidate = Path("/host_c") / sub if sub else Path("/host_c")
        if candidate.exists():
            return candidate, locator_base

    raise FileNotFoundError(
        f"Directory not found on PC: '{raw_path}'. Check the path or use the 'Browse PC Folders' button."
    )


def list_pc_subdirectories(raw_path: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """List subdirectories and count files in `raw_path` so the user can browse their PC folders in the GUI."""
    resolved, _ = resolve_pc_directory_path(raw_path)
    if not resolved.is_dir():
        raise NotADirectoryError(f"Not a directory: {raw_path}")

    cleaned = raw_path.strip().replace("/", "\\")
    if len(cleaned) == 2 and cleaned[1] == ":":
        cleaned += "\\"

    # Determine parent Windows path
    norm = cleaned.rstrip("\\")
    if "\\" in norm:
        parent_win = norm.rsplit("\\", 1)[0]
        if len(parent_win) == 2 and parent_win[1] == ":":
            parent_win += "\\"
    else:
        parent_win = None

    subdirs: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    direct_files: list[str] = []

    try:
        for entry in sorted(resolved.iterdir(), key=lambda p: p.name.lower()):
            if entry.name.startswith(".") or entry.name.startswith("$"):
                continue
            try:
                if entry.is_dir():
                    win_child = f"{cleaned.rstrip(chr(92))}\\{entry.name}"
                    subdirs.append({"name": entry.name, "path": win_child})
                elif entry.is_file():
                    direct_files.append(entry.name)
            except PermissionError:
                continue
    except PermissionError:
        pass

    return {
        "current_path": cleaned,
        "parent_path": parent_win,
        "direct_file_count": len(direct_files),
        "sample_files": direct_files[:15],
        "subdirectories": subdirs[:100],
    }


def _create_minimal_docx(path: Path, paragraphs: list[str]) -> None:
    """Create a valid `.docx` Word document containing `paragraphs`."""
    content_types = (
        '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>'
        '<Types xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/content-types">'
        '<Default Extension="rels" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-package.relationships+xml"/>'
        '<Default Extension="xml" ContentType="application/xml"/>'
        '<Override PartName="/word/document.xml" '
        'ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document.main+xml"/>'
        "</Types>"
    )
    rels = (
        '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>'
        '<Relationships xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships">'
        '<Relationship Id="rId1" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/officeDocument" '
        'Target="word/document.xml"/>'
        "</Relationships>"
    )
    body_xml = "".join(f"<w:p><w:r><w:t>{p}</w:t></w:r></w:p>" for p in paragraphs)
    document_xml = (
        '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>'
        '<w:document xmlns:w="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/wordprocessingml/2006/main">'
        f"<w:body>{body_xml}</w:body>"
        "</w:document>"
    )
    with zipfile.ZipFile(path, "w", compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:
        zf.writestr("[Content_Types].xml", content_types)
        zf.writestr("_rels/.rels", rels)
        zf.writestr("word/document.xml", document_xml)

                "G1 Docket Stage 1 (Labels-First Fast Lane) reads the Purview sensitivity label directly off the file.",
            ],
            purview_label_name="Confidential",
        )

    return root


def decode_uploaded_files_to_temp(
    uploaded_files: list[dict[str, Any]],
    temp_root: Path,
) -> tuple[Path, str]:
    """Materialize browser-picked folder files (`[{relative_path, content_b64}, ...]`) into `temp_root`."""
    temp_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    folder_slug = "picked-folder"
    for item in uploaded_files:
        rel = item["relative_path"].replace("\\", "/").lstrip("/")
        parts = rel.split("/")
        if len(parts) > 1:
            folder_slug = parts[0]
            rel_inside = "/".join(parts[1:])
        else:
            rel_inside = parts[0]
        dest = temp_root / rel_inside
        dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        raw_bytes = base64.b64decode(item["content_b64"])
        dest.write_bytes(raw_bytes)

    return temp_root, f"file://pc-dir/{folder_slug}"


def compute_bytes_sha256_hex(data: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()


=== FILE: D:\attestation\engine\dlp_connectors.py ===
"""Multi-Cloud DLP & Governance Catalog Connectors for G1 Docket (Microsoft Purview, AWS Macie, GCP SDP).

Implements the three-stage integration architecture:
  1. Stage 1 (Labels-First Fast Lane): Read embedded Microsoft Purview / MIP sensitivity labels directly
     from Office documents (.docx, .xlsx, .pptx) and file metadata with 1:1 tier mapping.
  2. Stage 2 (Pull Catalog Classifications): Authenticate via Azure Managed Identity / OAuth2 (or cloud IAM)
     to pull multi-cloud asset classifications (SharePoint, OneDrive, Azure SQL, Azure Blob, AWS S3, GCP BigQuery)
     and funnel them through `PresidioClassifier.evaluate_findings()` to issue DSSE-signed records.
  3. Stage 3 (Catalog Write-Back, Optional): Annotate G1 Docket's signed attestation state (`record_id`, `tier`,
     `expires_at`, `content_sha256`, `dsse_signature_status`) back onto the Purview Data Map entity.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import time
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
import zipfile
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import httpx

from engine.classifier import CategoryFinding, PresidioClassifier
from registry.schema_validator import TIER_ORDER, format_iso8601
from registry.store import RegistryService

# 1:1 mapping from Microsoft Purview / MIP Sensitivity Labels & Cloud DLP levels to G1 Docket Tiers
PURVIEW_LABEL_TO_TIER: dict[str, str] = {
    "public": "Public",
    "general": "Public",
    "non-business": "Public",
    "internal": "Internal",
    "internal only": "Internal",
    "company internal": "Internal",
    "confidential": "Confidential",
    "all employees": "Confidential",
    "confidential - all employees": "Confidential",
    "highly confidential": "Restricted",
    "restricted": "Restricted",
    "secret": "Restricted",
    "strictly confidential": "Restricted",
    "highly confidential \\ all employees": "Restricted",
}

# Mapping from Microsoft Purview / Cloud DLP classification type names to G1 Docket canonical categories
PURVIEW_CLASSIFICATION_MAP: dict[str, tuple[str, float]] = {
    "MICROSOFT.PERSONAL.US.SOCIAL_SECURITY_NUMBER": ("US_SSN", 0.98),
    "MICROSOFT.FINANCIAL.CREDIT_CARD_NUMBER": ("CREDIT_CARD", 0.96),
    "MICROSOFT.FINANCIAL.US.BANK_ACCOUNT_NUMBER": ("CREDIT_CARD", 0.94),
    "MICROSOFT.SECURITY.COMMON_PASSWORDS": ("API_KEY", 0.95),
    "MICROSOFT.SECURITY.AZURE_STORAGE_ACCOUNT_KEY": ("API_KEY", 0.99),
    "MICROSOFT.HEALTH.US.MEDICAL_RECORD_NUMBER": ("MEDICAL_RECORD_NUMBER", 0.96),
    "MICROSOFT.HEALTH.ICD10_CODE": ("ICD10_DIAGNOSIS_CODE", 0.95),
    "MICROSOFT.PERSONAL.EMAIL": ("EMAIL_ADDRESS", 0.94),

    "MICROSOFT.FINANCIAL.COMPENSATION": ("SALARY_COMPENSATION", 0.92),

    "MICROSOFT.NETWORK.IP_ADDRESS": ("IP_ADDRESS", 0.88),
    "MICROSOFT.ENTERPRISE.EMPLOYEE_ID": ("EMPLOYEE_ID", 0.89),
    "MICROSOFT.ENTERPRISE.PROJECT_CODE": ("INTERNAL_PROJECT_CODE", 0.87),
    "AWS.MACIE.USA_SOCIAL_SECURITY_NUMBER": ("US_SSN", 0.97),
    "AWS.MACIE.CREDIT_CARD_NUMBER": ("CREDIT_CARD", 0.95),
    "GCP.DLP.US_SOCIAL_SECURITY_NUMBER": ("US_SSN", 0.97),
    "GCP.DLP.EMAIL_ADDRESS": ("EMAIL_ADDRESS", 0.93),
}


@dataclass
class EmbeddedLabelResult:
    """Result of inspecting a local file (.docx, .xlsx, .pptx, .md, .txt) for an embedded Purview/MIP label."""

    has_label: bool
    raw_label_name: str | None = None
    label_guid: str | None = None
    mapped_tier: str | None = None
    source_property: str | None = None
    issuer: str = "Microsoft Purview Information Protection (MSIP)"

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "has_label": self.has_label,
            "raw_label_name": self.raw_label_name,
            "label_guid": self.label_guid,
            "mapped_tier": self.mapped_tier,
            "source_property": self.source_property,
            "issuer": self.issuer,
        }


def map_purview_label_to_tier(raw_label: str | None) -> str | None:
    """Map a Microsoft Purview sensitivity label string to a G1 Docket classification tier."""
    if not raw_label:
        return None
    norm = raw_label.strip().lower()
    if norm in PURVIEW_LABEL_TO_TIER:
        return PURVIEW_LABEL_TO_TIER[norm]
    if "highly confidential" in norm or "restricted" in norm or "secret" in norm:
        return "Restricted"
    if "confidential" in norm:
        return "Confidential"
    if "internal" in norm:
        return "Internal"
    if "public" in norm or "general" in norm:
        return "Public"
    return None


def extract_purview_label_from_file(file_path: str | Path, extra_metadata: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> EmbeddedLabelResult:
    """Stage 1 (Labels-First Fast Lane): Read Microsoft Purview / MIP sensitivity labels directly off Office files (.docx, .xlsx, .pptx) or metadata."""
    path = Path(file_path)
    meta = extra_metadata or {}

    # 1. Check OpenXML docProps/custom.xml and docProps/core.xml inside .docx / .xlsx / .pptx ZIP containers
    if path.suffix.lower() in (".docx", ".xlsx", ".pptx") and path.is_file() and zipfile.is_zipfile(path):
        try:
            with zipfile.ZipFile(path, "r") as zf:
                if "docProps/custom.xml" in zf.namelist():
                    xml_bytes = zf.read("docProps/custom.xml")
                    root = ET.fromstring(xml_bytes)
                    props: dict[str, str] = {}
                    for elem in root.iter():
                        name_attr = elem.attrib.get("name")
                        if name_attr:
                            val_text = "".join(elem.itertext()).strip()
                            props[name_attr] = val_text

                    # Look for MSIP_Label_<GUID>_Name or Sensitivity / Classification custom properties
                    for prop_name, prop_val in props.items():
                        if prop_name.startswith("MSIP_Label_") and prop_name.endswith("_Name") and prop_val:
                            parts = prop_name.split("_")
                            guid = parts[2] if len(parts) >= 4 else None
                            tier = map_purview_label_to_tier(prop_val)
                            if tier:
                                return EmbeddedLabelResult(
                                    has_label=True,
                                    raw_label_name=prop_val,
                                    label_guid=guid,
                                    mapped_tier=tier,
                                    source_property=f"docProps/custom.xml:{prop_name}",
                                )
                    for key_candidate in ("Sensitivity", "MSIP_Sensitivity", "PurviewLabel", "Classification"):
                        if key_candidate in props and props[key_candidate]:
                            tier = map_purview_label_to_tier(props[key_candidate])
                            if tier:
                                return EmbeddedLabelResult(
                                    has_label=True,
                                    raw_label_name=props[key_candidate],
                                    label_guid=None,
                                    mapped_tier=tier,
                                    source_property=f"docProps/custom.xml:{key_candidate}",
                                )
        except Exception:
            pass

    # 2. Check Tika / document metadata dictionary
    for k, v in meta.items():
        k_low = k.lower()
        if ("msip_label" in k_low and k_low.endswith("_name")) or k_low in ("sensitivity", "purview-label", "classification"):
            tier = map_purview_label_to_tier(str(v))
            if tier:
                return EmbeddedLabelResult(
                    has_label=True,
                    raw_label_name=str(v),
                    label_guid=None,
                    mapped_tier=tier,
                    source_property=f"metadata:{k}",
                )

    return EmbeddedLabelResult(has_label=False)


def create_purview_labeled_docx(
    path: Path,
    paragraphs: list[str],
    purview_label_name: str = "Confidential",
    purview_label_guid: str = "f42aa342-8706-4288-bd11-ebb85995028c",
    tenant_id: str = "72f988bf-86f1-41af-91ab-2d7cd011db47",
) -> Path:
    """Create a valid `.docx` file stamped with authentic Microsoft Purview Information Protection (MSIP) custom XML properties."""
    content_types = (
        '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>'
        '<Types xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/content-types">'
        '<Default Extension="rels" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-package.relationships+xml"/>'
        '<Default Extension="xml" ContentType="application/xml"/>'
        '<Override PartName="/word/document.xml" '
        'ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document.main+xml"/>'
        '<Override PartName="/docProps/custom.xml" '
        'ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.custom-properties+xml"/>'
        "</Types>"
    )
    rels = (
        '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>'
        '<Relationships xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships">'
        '<Relationship Id="rId1" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/officeDocument" '
        'Target="word/document.xml"/>'
        '<Relationship Id="rId2" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/custom-properties" '
        'Target="docProps/custom.xml"/>'
        "</Relationships>"
    )
    body_xml = "".join(f"<w:p><w:r><w:t>{p}</w:t></w:r></w:p>" for p in paragraphs)
    document_xml = (
        '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>'
        '<w:document xmlns:w="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/wordprocessingml/2006/main">'
        f"<w:body>{body_xml}</w:body>"
        "</w:document>"
    )
    custom_xml = (
        '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>'
        '<Properties xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/custom-properties" '
        'xmlns:vt="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/docPropsVTypes">'
        f'<property fmtid="{{D5CDD505-2E9C-101B-9397-08002B2CF9AE}}" pid="2" name="MSIP_Label_{purview_label_guid}_Enabled">'
        "<vt:lpwstr>true</vt:lpwstr></property>"
        f'<property fmtid="{{D5CDD505-2E9C-101B-9397-08002B2CF9AE}}" pid="3" name="MSIP_Label_{purview_label_guid}_Name">'
        f"<vt:lpwstr>{purview_label_name}</vt:lpwstr></property>"
        f'<property fmtid="{{D5CDD505-2E9C-101B-9397-08002B2CF9AE}}" pid="4" name="MSIP_Label_{purview_label_guid}_SiteId">'
        f"<vt:lpwstr>{tenant_id}</vt:lpwstr></property>"
        f'<property fmtid="{{D5CDD505-2E9C-101B-9397-08002B2CF9AE}}" pid="5" name="MSIP_Label_{purview_label_guid}_Method">'
        "<vt:lpwstr>Privileged</vt:lpwstr></property>"
        "</Properties>"
    )
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with zipfile.ZipFile(path, "w", compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:
        zf.writestr("[Content_Types].xml", content_types)
        zf.writestr("_rels/.rels", rels)
        zf.writestr("word/document.xml", document_xml)
        zf.writestr("docProps/custom.xml", custom_xml)
    return path


@dataclass
class PurviewCatalogAsset:
    """An asset discovered and classified in Microsoft Purview Data Map or a multi-cloud DLP catalog."""

    entity_guid: str
    locator: str
    display_name: str
    cloud_source: str  # e.g., "SharePoint Online", "OneDrive", "Azure SQL", "Azure Blob", "AWS S3", "GCP BigQuery"
    object_type: str  # "document", "spreadsheet", "table", "file"
    purview_sensitivity_label: str | None
    purview_classifications: list[str]
    content_sha256: str
    owner: str
    last_scan_at: str
    size_bytes: int | None = None
    row_count: int | None = None
    column_classifications: dict[str, list[str]] = field(default_factory=dict)


class PurviewMultiCloudConnector:
    """Connects G1 Docket to Microsoft Purview (Azure) and Multi-Cloud DLP catalogs across Stages 1, 2, and 3."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        registry: RegistryService,
        classifier: PresidioClassifier | None = None,
        config: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.registry = registry
        self.classifier = classifier or PresidioClassifier()
        self.config: dict[str, Any] = config or {
            "provider": "azure_purview",
            "account_name": "contoso-purview-prod",
            "endpoint_url": "https://contoso-purview-prod.purview.azure.com",
            "auth_mode": "managed_identity",  # "managed_identity" | "service_principal" | "aws_iam" | "gcp_adc"
            "tenant_id": "72f988bf-86f1-41af-91ab-2d7cd011db47",
            "client_id": "",
            "client_secret": "",
            "stage1_read_file_labels": True,
            "stage2_pull_catalog": True,
            "stage3_writeback_enabled": False,
            "clouds_enabled": ["SharePoint Online", "OneDrive", "Azure SQL", "Azure Blob", "AWS S3", "GCP BigQuery"],
        }
        self.writeback_history: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        self.last_sync_summary: dict[str, Any] | None = None

    def update_config(self, new_cfg: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        for k, v in new_cfg.items():
            if v is not None:
                self.config[k] = v
        return self.get_public_config()

    def get_public_config(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        secret = self.config.get("client_secret") or ""
        return {
            "provider": self.config.get("provider", "azure_purview"),
            "account_name": self.config.get("account_name", "contoso-purview-prod"),
            "endpoint_url": self.config.get("endpoint_url", "https://contoso-purview-prod.purview.azure.com"),
            "auth_mode": self.config.get("auth_mode", "managed_identity"),
            "tenant_id": self.config.get("tenant_id", ""),
            "client_id": self.config.get("client_id", ""),
            "has_client_secret": bool(secret),
            "stage1_read_file_labels": bool(self.config.get("stage1_read_file_labels", True)),
            "stage2_pull_catalog": bool(self.config.get("stage2_pull_catalog", True)),
            "stage3_writeback_enabled": bool(self.config.get("stage3_writeback_enabled", False)),
            "clouds_enabled": self.config.get(
                "clouds_enabled",
                ["SharePoint Online", "OneDrive", "Azure SQL", "Azure Blob", "AWS S3", "GCP BigQuery"],
            ),
            "writeback_count": len(self.writeback_history),
            "last_sync_summary": self.last_sync_summary,
        }

    def fetch_catalog_assets(self) -> list[PurviewCatalogAsset]:
        """Stage 2: Pull classified assets from Purview's Apache Atlas v2 Data Map API (or multi-cloud catalog feed)."""
        now_iso = format_iso8601(datetime.now(timezone.utc))
        endpoint = (self.config.get("endpoint_url") or "").rstrip("/")

        # Attempt live Purview Data Map REST call if a custom live bearer token / reachable endpoint is configured
        if endpoint and self.config.get("client_secret") and not endpoint.startswith("https://contoso-purview-prod"):
            try:
                with httpx.Client(timeout=4.0) as client:
                    resp = client.post(
                        f"{endpoint}/datamap/api/atlas/v2/search/advanced",
                        json={"keywords": "*", "limit": 50},
                        headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {self.config['client_secret']}"},
                    )
                    if resp.status_code == 200:
                        data = resp.json()
                        entities = data.get("value", [])
                        if entities:
                            parsed: list[PurviewCatalogAsset] = []
                            for ent in entities:
                                qname = ent.get("qualifiedName") or ent.get("id") or "purview://asset"
                                parsed.append(
                                    PurviewCatalogAsset(
                                        entity_guid=ent.get("id", hashlib.sha256(qname.encode()).hexdigest()[:32]),
                                        locator=qname,
                                        display_name=ent.get("name", qname.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]),
                                        cloud_source=ent.get("entityType", "Azure Purview"),
                                        object_type="table" if "table" in ent.get("entityType", "").lower() else "document",
                                        purview_sensitivity_label=ent.get("label"),
                                        purview_classifications=ent.get("classification", []),
                                        content_sha256=hashlib.sha256(qname.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(),
                                        owner=ent.get("owner", "purview-governance@contoso.com"),
                                        last_scan_at=now_iso,
                                    )
                                )
                            return parsed
            except Exception:
                pass

        # Multi-cloud estate catalog representing Purview scans across SharePoint, OneDrive, Azure SQL, Blob, AWS S3, and GCP BigQuery
        all_assets = [

    def convert_asset_to_findings(self, asset: PurviewCatalogAsset) -> tuple[list[CategoryFinding], dict[str, Any]]:
        """Convert Purview classifications and sensitivity labels into G1 Docket `CategoryFinding` objects and `metadata_hints`."""
        findings: list[CategoryFinding] = []
        seen: set[tuple[str, str | None]] = set()

        for raw_cls in asset.purview_classifications:
            mapped_cat, conf = PURVIEW_CLASSIFICATION_MAP.get(raw_cls, (raw_cls, 0.90))
            col_name = None
            for col, col_classes in asset.column_classifications.items():
                if raw_cls in col_classes:
                    col_name = col
                    break
            key = (mapped_cat, col_name)
            if key not in seen:
                seen.add(key)
                findings.append(
                    CategoryFinding(
                        category=mapped_cat,
                        count=1,
                        max_confidence=conf,
                        column_or_field=col_name or f"purview:{raw_cls}",
                    )
                )

        mapped_label_tier = map_purview_label_to_tier(asset.purview_sensitivity_label)
        if mapped_label_tier:
            findings.append(
                CategoryFinding(
                    category=f"PURVIEW_LABEL_{mapped_label_tier.upper()}",
                    count=1,
                    max_confidence=0.99,
                    column_or_field=f"purview_label:{asset.purview_sensitivity_label}",
                )
            )

        hints = {
            "default_tier": mapped_label_tier or "Public",
            "purview_label": asset.purview_sensitivity_label,
            "purview_entity_guid": asset.entity_guid,
            "cloud_source": asset.cloud_source,
            "confidence": 0.98,
        }
        return findings, hints

    def pull_and_attest_catalog(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Stage 2 (+ optional Stage 3): Pull multi-cloud assets from Purview, evaluate findings through `evaluate_findings()`, and issue DSSE-signed records."""
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        assets = self.fetch_catalog_assets()
        synced_items: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        tier_counts: dict[str, int] = {"Public": 0, "Internal": 0, "Confidential": 0, "Restricted": 0}
        clouds_seen: set[str] = set()
        writebacks_performed = 0

        for asset in assets:
            clouds_seen.add(asset.cloud_source)
            findings, hints = self.convert_asset_to_findings(asset)
            verdict = self.classifier.evaluate_findings(
                findings=findings,
                metadata_hints=hints,
                classifier_engine=f"purview-catalog+{asset.cloud_source.lower().replace(' ', '-')}",
            )

            # Ensure Purview sensitivity label floor is respected if higher than content findings
            label_tier = hints.get("default_tier", "Public")
            if label_tier in TIER_ORDER and TIER_ORDER[label_tier] > TIER_ORDER[verdict.tier]:
                verdict.tier = label_tier

            res = self.registry.issue_or_queue_finding(
                locator=asset.locator,
                content_sha256=asset.content_sha256,
                tier=verdict.tier,
                owner=asset.owner,
                confidence=verdict.confidence,
                low_confidence=verdict.low_confidence,
                sensitive_categories=[f.to_dict() for f in verdict.sensitive_categories],
                object_type=(
                    "database_table"
                    if asset.object_type == "table"
                    else (
                        asset.object_type
                        if asset.object_type in ("file", "document", "spreadsheet", "database_table", "warehouse_table")
                        else "file"
                    )
                ),
                display_name=asset.display_name,
                environment="production",
                attested_by=f"g1-docket-purview-connector ({self.config.get('auth_mode', 'managed_identity')})",
                source_system=f"azure-purview:{asset.cloud_source}",
                size_bytes=asset.size_bytes,
                row_count=asset.row_count,
                fingerprint_method="raw_bytes_sha256" if asset.object_type != "table" else "table_schema_and_canonical_rows_sha256",
                classifier_engine=verdict.classifier_engine,
            )

            rec = res.get("record")
            if rec:
                tier_counts[rec["tier"]] = tier_counts.get(rec["tier"], 0) + 1

            wb_entry = None
            if self.config.get("stage3_writeback_enabled") and rec:
                wb_entry = self.write_back_record_to_purview(asset, rec)
                writebacks_performed += 1

            synced_items.append(
                {
                    "entity_guid": asset.entity_guid,
                    "locator": asset.locator,
                    "display_name": asset.display_name,
                    "asset_name": asset.display_name,
                    "cloud_source": asset.cloud_source,
                    "object_type": asset.object_type,
                    "purview_label": asset.purview_sensitivity_label or "None",
                    "purview_classifications": asset.purview_classifications,
                    "docket_tier": rec["tier"] if rec else verdict.tier,
                    "record_id": rec["record_id"] if rec else None,
                    "status": rec["status"] if rec else "PENDING_REVIEW",
                    "expires_at": rec["expires_at"] if rec else "-",
                    "content_sha256": asset.content_sha256,
                    "writeback": bool(wb_entry),
                    "writeback_status": wb_entry["status"] if wb_entry else "Stage 3 Disabled (Read-Only)",
                }
            )

        elapsed_ms = round((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0, 2)
        cloud_list_str = ", ".join(sorted(clouds_seen))
        english_summary = (
            f"G1 Docket Purview Connector pulled {len(synced_items)} multi-cloud asset(s) across {len(clouds_seen)} cloud source(s) "
            f"({cloud_list_str}) via {self.config.get('auth_mode', 'managed_identity')}: "
            f"Issued {len(synced_items)} DSSE-signed attestation record(s) "
            f"({tier_counts['Public']} Public, {tier_counts['Internal']} Internal, "
            f"{tier_counts['Confidential']} Confidential, {tier_counts['Restricted']} Restricted) in {elapsed_ms} ms"
            + (f" and wrote {writebacks_performed} attestation state annotation(s) back to Purview Data Map." if writebacks_performed else ".")
        )

        summary = {
            "provider": self.config.get("provider", "azure_purview"),
            "account_name": self.config.get("account_name", "contoso-purview-prod"),
            "auth_mode": self.config.get("auth_mode", "managed_identity"),
            "total_assets_pulled": len(synced_items),
            "clouds_scanned": sorted(clouds_seen),
            "tier_counts": tier_counts,
            "writebacks_performed": writebacks_performed,
            "elapsed_ms": elapsed_ms,
            "english_summary": english_summary,
            "assets": synced_items,
            "attested_assets": synced_items,
            "writeback_results": list(self.writeback_history[-len(synced_items):]) if writebacks_performed else [],
        }
        self.last_sync_summary = summary
        return summary

    def write_back_record_to_purview(self, asset: PurviewCatalogAsset, record: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Stage 3 (Optional Catalog Write-Back): Annotate G1 Docket's signed tier, record_id, and expiry back into Purview's Data Map catalog."""
        now_iso = format_iso8601(datetime.now(timezone.utc))
        endpoint = (self.config.get("endpoint_url") or "https://contoso-purview-prod.purview.azure.com").rstrip("/")
        atlas_path = f"/datamap/api/atlas/v2/entity/guid/{asset.entity_guid}/businessmetadata"
        payload = {
            "G1DocketAttestation": {
                "record_id": record["record_id"],
                "docket_tier": record["tier"],
                "attestation_status": record["status"],
                "content_sha256": record["content_sha256"],
                "attested_at": record.get("attested_at", now_iso),
                "expires_at": record["expires_at"],
                "ai_eligibility": record.get("ai_eligibility", "eligible"),
                "egress_class": record.get("egress_class", "local_only"),
                "dsse_key_id": record["dsse_envelope"]["signatures"][0]["keyid"],
            }
        }
        entry = {
            "timestamp": now_iso,
            "entity_guid": asset.entity_guid,
            "purview_guid": asset.entity_guid,
            "locator": asset.locator,
            "cloud_source": asset.cloud_source,
            "target_api": f"POST {endpoint}{atlas_path}",
            "purview_api_endpoint": f"POST {endpoint}{atlas_path}",
            "business_metadata_written": payload["G1DocketAttestation"],
            "business_metadata_payload": payload,
            "status": "ANNOTATED_IN_PURVIEW",
            "writeback_status": "ANNOTATED_IN_PURVIEW (200 OK)",
        }
        self.writeback_history.append(entry)
        return entry

    def run_writeback_for_all_catalog_assets(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Explicitly run Stage 3 Write-Back for all currently attested Purview catalog assets."""
        self.config["stage3_writeback_enabled"] = True
        return self.pull_and_attest_catalog()


=== FILE: D:\attestation\snapshot\__init__.py ===
"""POD (Proof Of Data) Signed Snapshot Publisher."""

__version__ = "0.1.0"


=== FILE: D:\attestation\snapshot\publisher.py ===
"""Signed Snapshot Publisher producing short-TTL DSSE snapshots for zero-DB consumer enforcement."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Header, HTTPException, status
from fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse

from registry.schema_validator import (
    INTOTO_STATEMENT_TYPE,
    SNAPSHOT_PREDICATE_TYPE,
    format_iso8601,
    parse_iso8601,
    validate_intoto_statement,
)
from registry.signing import Keyring, sign_statement
from registry.store import RegistryService


class SnapshotPublisher:
    """Publishes DSSE-signed snapshots containing active records plus expired and revoked tombstones."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        registry: RegistryService,
        publisher_id: str = "pod-snapshot-publisher-v0.1",
        ttl_seconds: int = 300,
        snapshot_output_path: str | Path | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.registry = registry
        self.publisher_id = publisher_id
        self.ttl_seconds = ttl_seconds
        self.snapshot_output_path = (
            Path(snapshot_output_path)
            if snapshot_output_path
            else Path(os.environ.get("POD_SNAPSHOT_FILE", "./pod_snapshot.dsse.json"))
        )
        self._sequence_number = self._load_existing_sequence_number()
        self._latest_envelope: dict[str, Any] | None = None

    def _load_existing_sequence_number(self) -> int:
        if self.snapshot_output_path.exists():
            try:
                env = json.loads(self.snapshot_output_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                import base64

                stmt = json.loads(base64.b64decode(env["payload"]).decode("utf-8"))
                return int(stmt.get("predicate", {}).get("sequence_number", 0))
            except Exception:
                return 0
        return 0

    def publish_snapshot(
        self,
        ttl_seconds: int | None = None,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Compile current Registry records into a signed DSSE snapshot and write it to disk."""
        now_dt = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        effective_ttl = ttl_seconds if ttl_seconds is not None else self.ttl_seconds
        exp_dt = now_dt + timedelta(seconds=effective_ttl)

        records = self.registry.list_records(current_only=True)
        entries: dict[str, Any] = {}
        for rec in records:
            raw_status = rec["status"]
            if raw_status == "revoked":
                norm_status = "revoked"
            elif raw_status == "expired" or parse_iso8601(rec["expires_at"]) <= now_dt:
                norm_status = "expired"
            else:
                norm_status = "active"

            pred_obj = rec["statement"].get("predicate", {})
            redact_cats = pred_obj.get("handling", {}).get("redaction_categories", [])
            integrity_obj = pred_obj.get("integrity", {})

            entries[rec["locator"]] = {
                "record_id": rec["record_id"],
                "locator": rec["locator"],
                "content_sha256": rec["content_sha256"],
                "extracted_text_sha256": integrity_obj.get("extracted_text_sha256"),
                "chunk_hashes": integrity_obj.get("chunk_hashes", []),
                "tier": rec["tier"],
                "status": norm_status,
                "ai_eligibility": rec["ai_eligibility"],
                "minimum_clearance": rec["minimum_clearance"],
                "egress_class": rec["egress_class"],
                "record_expires_at": rec["expires_at"],
                "revoked_at": rec["revoked_at"],
                "redaction_categories": redact_cats,
            }

        self._sequence_number += 1
        snap_id = f"pod-snap-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:16]}"
        predicate = {
            "schema_version": "0.1.0",
            "snapshot_id": snap_id,
            "sequence_number": self._sequence_number,
            "issued_at": format_iso8601(now_dt),
            "expires_at": format_iso8601(exp_dt),
            "publisher_id": self.publisher_id,
            "entries": entries,
        }

        entries_bytes = json.dumps(entries, separators=(",", ":"), sort_keys=True).encode("utf-8")
        entries_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(entries_bytes).hexdigest()

        statement = {
            "_type": INTOTO_STATEMENT_TYPE,
            "subject": [
                {
                    "name": f"pod://snapshots/{snap_id}",
                    "digest": {"sha256": entries_sha256},
                }
            ],
            "predicateType": SNAPSHOT_PREDICATE_TYPE,
            "predicate": predicate,
        }
        validate_intoto_statement(statement, expected_predicate_type=SNAPSHOT_PREDICATE_TYPE)
        envelope = sign_statement(statement, self.registry.keyring, purpose="snapshot")

        self.snapshot_output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.snapshot_output_path.write_text(json.dumps(envelope, indent=2, sort_keys=True), encoding="utf-8")
        self._latest_envelope = envelope
        return envelope

    def get_latest_snapshot(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if self._latest_envelope is None:
            return self.publish_snapshot()
        return self._latest_envelope


def create_snapshot_app(publisher: SnapshotPublisher | None = None) -> FastAPI:
    """Create the FastAPI service for the Snapshot Publisher."""
    app = FastAPI(title="POD Signed Snapshot Publisher", version="0.1.0")
    pub = publisher or SnapshotPublisher(RegistryService())

    admin_token = os.environ.get("POD_ADMIN_API_KEY", "pod-dev-admin-key")
    control_token = os.environ.get("POD_CONTROL_PLANE_TOKEN", "pod-dev-control-plane-token")

    def require_control_or_admin(
        x_pod_control_token: str | None = Header(default=None),
        x_pod_admin_key: str | None = Header(default=None),
        authorization: str | None = Header(default=None),
    ) -> None:
        bearer = None
        if authorization and authorization.lower().startswith("bearer "):
            bearer = authorization[7:].strip()
        valid_tokens = {t for t in (control_token, admin_token) if t}
        if (
            (x_pod_control_token and x_pod_control_token in valid_tokens)
            or (x_pod_admin_key and x_pod_admin_key in valid_tokens)
            or (bearer and bearer in valid_tokens)
        ):
            return
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="Unauthorized control-plane mutation: valid X-POD-Control-Token or Bearer token required.",
        )

    @app.get("/", include_in_schema=False)
    def root() -> RedirectResponse:
        return RedirectResponse(url="/docs")

    @app.get("/healthz")
    def healthz() -> dict[str, str]:
        return {"status": "ok", "service": "pod-snapshot-publisher"}

    @app.get("/v1/snapshot/latest")
    def get_latest() -> dict[str, Any]:
        return pub.get_latest_snapshot()

    @app.post("/v1/publish", dependencies=[Depends(require_control_or_admin)])
    @app.post("/v1/snapshot/publish", dependencies=[Depends(require_control_or_admin)])
    def trigger_publish() -> dict[str, Any]:
        return pub.publish_snapshot()

    @app.get("/v1/snapshot/keys")
    def get_public_keys() -> dict[str, Any]:
        keys = [
            {
                "keyid": k["keyid"],
                "algorithm": k["algorithm"],
                "status": k["status"],
                "created_at": k["created_at"],
                "rotated_at": k["rotated_at"],
                "revoked_at": k["revoked_at"],
                "public_key_pem": k["public_key_pem"],
            }
            for k in pub.registry.keyring.list_keys()
        ]
        return {"keys": keys}

    return app

"""POD (Proof Of Data) AI Gateway Prevalidator Proxy (OpenAI & Anthropic)."""

__version__ = "0.1.0"


=== FILE: D:\attestation\prevalidator\enforcer.py ===
"""In-memory Snapshot Enforcer and `<pod:object>` tag parser/stripper for the Prevalidator."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import jwt

from engine.classifier import RESTRICTED_CATEGORIES, PresidioClassifier
from registry.schema_validator import (
    CLEARANCE_ORDER,
    EGRESS_ORDER,
    SNAPSHOT_PREDICATE_TYPE,
    format_iso8601,
    parse_iso8601,
)
from registry.signing import Keyring, normalize_canonical_text, verify_dsse_envelope

# Uniform denial message mandated by Phase 2 Consumer Conformance Suite (never leaks which rule fired)
UNIFORM_DENIAL_MESSAGE = "Request denied by data attestation policy."
UNIFORM_DENIAL_CODE = "policy_denied"

# Security reason codes
REASON_HASH_MISMATCH = "REASON_HASH_MISMATCH"
REASON_UNVERIFIED_INLINE_CONTENT = "REASON_UNVERIFIED_INLINE_CONTENT"
REASON_MALFORMED_POD_TAG = "REASON_MALFORMED_POD_TAG"
REASON_UNATTESTED_CONTEXT_OVERFLOW = "REASON_UNATTESTED_CONTEXT_OVERFLOW"

# Strict regexes for <pod:object> structural validation
POD_OPEN_TAG_STRICT_RE = re.compile(
    r'^<pod:object\s+locator="([^"<>]+)"(?:\s+sha256="([a-fA-F0-9]{64})")?\s*>$'
)
POD_CLOSE_TAG_STRICT_RE = re.compile(r"^</pod:object>$")
POD_ANY_TAG_TOKEN_RE = re.compile(r"<\s*/?\s*pod:object\b[^>]*>?|<\s*/?\s*pod:object", re.IGNORECASE)

# Regex matching balanced inline <pod:object locator="..." [sha256="..."]>...</pod:object> blocks
POD_OBJECT_TAG_RE = re.compile(
    r'<pod:object\s+locator="([^"<>]+)"(?:\s+sha256="([a-fA-F0-9]{64})")?\s*>(.*?)</pod:object>',
    re.DOTALL,
)


@dataclass
class AuditLogEntry:
    """Operator audit log entry recording exact reason codes (never exposed in client error responses)."""

    timestamp: str
    mode: str  # "enforce" | "observe"
    decision: str  # "allow" | "strip" | "deny" | "observe_would_deny"
    reason_codes: list[str]
    locators_evaluated: list[str]
    caller_clearance: str
    target_egress: str
    stripped_locators: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "timestamp": self.timestamp,
            "mode": self.mode,
            "decision": self.decision,
            "reason_codes": self.reason_codes,
            "locators_evaluated": self.locators_evaluated,
            "caller_clearance": self.caller_clearance,
            "target_egress": self.target_egress,
            "stripped_locators": self.stripped_locators,
        }


@dataclass
class EnforcementOutcome:
    """Result of evaluating an incoming LLM request against the in-memory snapshot."""

    allowed_to_forward: bool
    transformed_payload: dict[str, Any]
    client_denial_body: dict[str, Any] | None
    audit_entry: AuditLogEntry


class InMemorySnapshotStore:
    """Holds the latest verified DSSE snapshot in memory, enforcing monotonic sequence numbers and TTL fail-closed."""

    def __init__(self, keyring: Keyring) -> None:
        self.keyring = keyring
        self._snapshot_predicate: dict[str, Any] | None = None
        self._highest_sequence: int = 0

    def load_from_envelope(self, envelope: dict[str, Any], now: datetime | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Verify DSSE signature and sequence number before atomically updating the in-memory snapshot."""
        ver = self.keyring.verify_dsse(
            envelope=envelope,
            expected_predicate_type=SNAPSHOT_PREDICATE_TYPE,
            check_expiry=True,
            now=now,
        )
        pred = ver.statement["predicate"]
        seq = int(pred["sequence_number"])
        if seq < self._highest_sequence:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Snapshot rollback rejected: incoming sequence_number ({seq}) < current ({self._highest_sequence})."
            )
        self._highest_sequence = seq
        self._snapshot_predicate = pred
        return pred

    def load_from_file(self, path: str | Path, now: datetime | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
        envelope = json.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        return self.load_from_envelope(envelope, now=now)

    def get_active_snapshot(self, now: datetime | None = None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        """Return the in-memory snapshot predicate ONLY if loaded and not past `expires_at` (fail-closed)."""
        if self._snapshot_predicate is None:
            return None
        current_time = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        expires_at = parse_iso8601(self._snapshot_predicate["expires_at"])
        if current_time >= expires_at:
            return None
        return self._snapshot_predicate


class PrevalidatorEnforcer:
    """Evaluates OpenAI and Anthropic request payloads against the in-memory signed snapshot."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        snapshot_store: InMemorySnapshotStore,
        mode: str = "enforce",  # "enforce" | "observe"
        route_egress_map: dict[str, str] | None = None,
        default_egress_class: str = "public_api",
        caller_key_clearance_map: dict[str, str] | None = None,
        jwt_secret: str | None = None,
        enable_presidio_fallback: bool = True,
        allow_header_only_objects: bool | None = None,
        strict_inline_hash: bool | None = None,
        max_untagged_chars: int | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        if mode not in ("enforce", "observe"):
            raise ValueError("mode must be 'enforce' or 'observe'")
        self.snapshot_store = snapshot_store
        self.mode = mode
        self.route_egress_map = route_egress_map or {
            "local-model": "local_only",
            "ollama": "local_only",
            "vpc-model": "private_cloud",
            "gpt-4o": "public_api",
            "claude-3-5-sonnet": "public_api",
        }
        self.default_egress_class = default_egress_class
        self.caller_key_clearance_map = caller_key_clearance_map or {
            "pod-key-public": "Public",
            "pod-key-internal": "Internal",
            "pod-key-confidential": "Confidential",
            "pod-key-restricted": "Restricted",
        }
        self.jwt_secret = jwt_secret or "pod-local-jwt-secret-key-minimum-32-bytes-long"
        self.enable_presidio_fallback = enable_presidio_fallback
        self.allow_header_only_objects = (
            allow_header_only_objects
            if allow_header_only_objects is not None
            else os.environ.get("POD_ALLOW_HEADER_ONLY_OBJECTS", "false").lower() in ("1", "true", "yes")
        )
        self.strict_inline_hash = (
            strict_inline_hash
            if strict_inline_hash is not None
            else os.environ.get("POD_STRICT_INLINE_HASH", "true").lower() in ("1", "true", "yes")
        )
        self.max_untagged_chars = (
            max_untagged_chars
            if max_untagged_chars is not None
            else int(os.environ.get("POD_MAX_UNTAGGED_CHARS", "2000"))
        )
        self.classifier = PresidioClassifier()
        self.audit_log: list[AuditLogEntry] = []

    def resolve_caller_clearance(self, headers: dict[str, str]) -> str:
        """Resolve caller clearance from verified JWT or configured caller API key (defaults to 'Public')."""
        norm_headers = {k.lower(): v for k, v in headers.items()}

        # 1. Check signed JWT in X-POD-Caller-Token
        jwt_token = norm_headers.get("x-pod-caller-token")
        if jwt_token:
            try:
                decoded = jwt.decode(jwt_token, self.jwt_secret, algorithms=["HS256"])
                clearance = decoded.get("clearance", "Public")
                if clearance in CLEARANCE_ORDER:
                    return clearance
            except Exception:
                return "Public"

        # 2. Check configured caller key map in X-POD-Caller-Key
        caller_key = norm_headers.get("x-pod-caller-key")
        if caller_key and caller_key in self.caller_key_clearance_map:
            return self.caller_key_clearance_map[caller_key]

        return "Public"

    def resolve_target_egress(self, model_name: str | None, route_override: str | None = None) -> str:
        """Resolve destination egress class from configured route/model map."""
        if route_override and route_override in EGRESS_ORDER:
            return route_override
        if model_name:
            for prefix, egress in self.route_egress_map.items():
                if model_name == prefix or model_name.startswith(prefix):
                    return egress
        return self.default_egress_class

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_pod_tag_structure(text: str) -> tuple[bool, list[tuple[int, int, str, str | None, str]]]:
        """Strictly validate `<pod:object>` tag balance, syntax, and absence of nesting or early-close smuggling.

        Returns `(is_valid, parsed_blocks)` where each block is `(start, end, locator, sha256, inner_text)`.
        """
        tokens = list(POD_ANY_TAG_TOKEN_RE.finditer(text))
        if not tokens:
            return True, []

        blocks: list[tuple[int, int, str, str | None, str]] = []
        open_token: tuple[int, int, str, str | None] | None = None

        for tok in tokens:
            raw_tok = tok.group(0)
            if raw_tok.lower().startswith("</") or re.match(r"^<\s*/", raw_tok):
                # Closing tag
                if not POD_CLOSE_TAG_STRICT_RE.match(raw_tok):
                    return False, []
                if open_token is None:
                    # Early or orphan closing tag
                    return False, []
                o_start, o_end, loc, sha = open_token
                inner_text = text[o_end : tok.start()]
                # Ensure no nested or partial pod:object markers inside inner_text
                if "pod:object" in inner_text.lower():
                    return False, []
                blocks.append((o_start, tok.end(), loc, sha, inner_text))
                open_token = None
            else:
                # Opening tag
                if open_token is not None:
                    # Nested or unclosed previous opening tag
                    return False, []
                m = POD_OPEN_TAG_STRICT_RE.match(raw_tok)
                if not m:
                    return False, []
                open_token = (tok.start(), tok.end(), m.group(1), m.group(2))

        if open_token is not None:
            # Unclosed opening tag at end of text
            return False, []

        return True, blocks

    @staticmethod
    def _verify_inner_text_cryptographically(
        inner_text: str,
        inline_sha256: str | None,
        entry: dict[str, Any],
    ) -> bool:
        """Verify live SHA-256 of `inner_text` against `content_sha256`, `extracted_text_sha256`, or `chunk_hashes`."""
        raw_inner_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(inner_text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
        norm_inner_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(normalize_canonical_text(inner_text)).hexdigest()

        # Never trust a caller-supplied sha256 attribute that contradicts the live digest of inner_text
        if inline_sha256 is not None:
            supplied = inline_sha256.lower()
            if supplied not in (raw_inner_sha256, norm_inner_sha256):
                return False

        att_content_sha = (entry.get("content_sha256") or "").lower()
        att_text_sha = (entry.get("extracted_text_sha256") or "").lower()
        att_chunks = {h.lower() for h in (entry.get("chunk_hashes") or [])}

        # 1. Whole-object content_sha256 match
        if raw_inner_sha256 == att_content_sha or norm_inner_sha256 == att_content_sha:
            return True

        # 2. Full normalized extracted_text_sha256 match
        if att_text_sha and (norm_inner_sha256 == att_text_sha or raw_inner_sha256 == att_text_sha):
            return True

        # 3. Deterministic chunk_hashes match
        if norm_inner_sha256 in att_chunks or raw_inner_sha256 in att_chunks:
            return True

        # 4. Multi-paragraph / multi-line composition strictly from attested chunk_hashes
        if att_chunks and "\n" in inner_text:
            sub_lines = [ln.strip() for ln in inner_text.splitlines() if ln.strip()]
            if sub_lines and all(
                hashlib.sha256(normalize_canonical_text(ln)).hexdigest() in att_chunks
                for ln in sub_lines
            ):
                return True

        return False

    def _evaluate_single_locator(
        self,
        locator: str,
        inline_sha256: str | None,
        entries: dict[str, Any],
        caller_clearance: str,
        target_egress: str,
        now_dt: datetime,
        inner_text: str | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[str, list[str]]:
        """Return `(action, reason_codes)` where action is `'allow' | 'strip' | 'deny'`."""
        entry = entries.get(locator)
        if not entry:
            return "deny", ["UNATTESTED"]

        status = entry.get("status", "active")
        if status == "revoked":
            return "deny", ["REVOKED"]

        rec_exp = parse_iso8601(entry["record_expires_at"])
        if status == "expired" or now_dt >= rec_exp:
            return "deny", ["EXPIRED"]

        # Live cryptographic verification of inline <pod:object> content
        if inner_text is not None and self.strict_inline_hash:
            if not self._verify_inner_text_cryptographically(inner_text, inline_sha256, entry):
                return "deny", [REASON_HASH_MISMATCH, "HASH_DRIFT"]
        elif inline_sha256 and inline_sha256.lower() != entry["content_sha256"].lower():
            return "deny", [REASON_HASH_MISMATCH, "HASH_DRIFT"]

        # Check caller clearance against record minimum_clearance
        req_clearance = entry["minimum_clearance"]
        if CLEARANCE_ORDER[caller_clearance] < CLEARANCE_ORDER[req_clearance]:
            return "deny", ["INSUFFICIENT_CLEARANCE"]

        # Check destination egress class against record egress_class
        allowed_egress = entry["egress_class"]
        if EGRESS_ORDER[target_egress] > EGRESS_ORDER[allowed_egress]:
            return "deny", ["EGRESS_DENIED"]

        # Check AI eligibility ('allow' | 'strip' | 'deny')
        ai_elig = entry["ai_eligibility"]
        if ai_elig == "deny":
            return "deny", ["EGRESS_DENIED"]
        if ai_elig == "strip":
            return "strip", []
        return "allow", []

    @staticmethod
    def _uniform_denial_response() -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "error": {
                "message": UNIFORM_DENIAL_MESSAGE,
                "type": "pod_policy_denial",
                "code": UNIFORM_DENIAL_CODE,
            }
        }

    def evaluate_request(
        self,
        payload: dict[str, Any],
        headers: dict[str, str] | None = None,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> EnforcementOutcome:
        """Evaluate an OpenAI `/v1/chat/completions` or Anthropic `/v1/messages` request payload."""
        now_dt = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        now_str = format_iso8601(now_dt)
        hdrs = headers or {}
        norm_hdrs = {k.lower(): v for k, v in hdrs.items()}

        caller_clearance = self.resolve_caller_clearance(hdrs)
        target_egress = self.resolve_target_egress(payload.get("model"))

        snapshot = self.snapshot_store.get_active_snapshot(now=now_dt)
        if snapshot is None:
            # Staleness or missing snapshot MUST fail closed
            audit = AuditLogEntry(
                timestamp=now_str,
                mode=self.mode,
                decision="deny",
                reason_codes=["EXPIRED"],
                locators_evaluated=[],
                caller_clearance=caller_clearance,
                target_egress=target_egress,
            )
            self.audit_log.append(audit)
            return EnforcementOutcome(
                allowed_to_forward=False,
                transformed_payload=payload,
                client_denial_body=self._uniform_denial_response(),
                audit_entry=audit,
            )

        entries = snapshot.get("entries", {})
        reason_codes: list[str] = []
        locators_seen: list[str] = []
        inline_verified_locators: set[str] = set()
        stripped_locators: list[str] = []
        total_untagged_chars = 0
        has_inline_pod_tags = False

        # 1. Evaluate and transform message texts (strict `<pod:object>` parser + live inner-text SHA-256 + fallback Presidio)
        new_payload = json.loads(json.dumps(payload))

        def process_text(text: str) -> str:
            nonlocal reason_codes, locators_seen, stripped_locators, total_untagged_chars, has_inline_pod_tags

            is_valid_structure, parsed_blocks = self._validate_pod_tag_structure(text)
            if not is_valid_structure:
                reason_codes.append(REASON_MALFORMED_POD_TAG)
                return text

            if parsed_blocks:
                has_inline_pod_tags = True

            untagged_parts: list[str] = []
            last_end = 0
            out_segments: list[str] = []

            for start_idx, end_idx, loc, sha, inner_content in parsed_blocks:
                untagged_seg = text[last_end:start_idx]
                untagged_parts.append(untagged_seg)
                last_end = end_idx

                locators_seen.append(loc)
                inline_verified_locators.add(loc)

                action, r_codes = self._evaluate_single_locator(
                    locator=loc,
                    inline_sha256=sha,
                    entries=entries,
                    caller_clearance=caller_clearance,
                    target_egress=target_egress,
                    now_dt=now_dt,
                    inner_text=inner_content,
                )
                if action == "deny":
                    reason_codes.extend(r_codes)
                    if self.mode == "observe":
                        out_segments.append(inner_content)
                elif action == "strip":
                    stripped_locators.append(loc)
                    if self.mode == "observe":
                        out_segments.append(inner_content)
                    else:
                        out_segments.append("[POD:STRIPPED]")
                else:
                    # Allowed -> unwrap <pod:object> tags and forward clean content
                    out_segments.append(inner_content)

            tail_seg = text[last_end:]
            untagged_parts.append(tail_seg)
            for p in untagged_parts:
                total_untagged_chars += len(p.strip())
            untagged_joined = " ".join(p for p in untagged_parts if p.strip())

            # Fallback content inspection (Presidio) ONLY on untagged free text carrying no object identity
            if self.enable_presidio_fallback and untagged_joined:
                findings = self.classifier.scan_text_segment(untagged_joined)
                restricted_hits = [f for f in findings if f.category in RESTRICTED_CATEGORIES]
                if restricted_hits:
                    reason_codes.append("UNATTESTED")

            # Reconstruct full text with transformed inline blocks
            if parsed_blocks:
                rebuilt: list[str] = []
                idx = 0
                seg_idx = 0
                for start_idx, end_idx, _loc, _sha, _inner in parsed_blocks:
                    rebuilt.append(text[idx:start_idx])
                    if seg_idx < len(out_segments):
                        rebuilt.append(out_segments[seg_idx])
                        seg_idx += 1
                    idx = end_idx
                rebuilt.append(text[idx:])
                return "".join(rebuilt).strip()
            return text

        # Transform top-level Anthropic `system` prompt if present
        if isinstance(new_payload.get("system"), str):
            new_payload["system"] = process_text(new_payload["system"])
        elif isinstance(new_payload.get("system"), list):
            for block in new_payload["system"]:
                if isinstance(block, dict) and isinstance(block.get("text"), str):
                    block["text"] = process_text(block["text"])

        # Transform `messages` array (OpenAI & Anthropic formats)
        for msg in new_payload.get("messages", []):
            content = msg.get("content")
            if isinstance(content, str):
                msg["content"] = process_text(content)
            elif isinstance(content, list):
                for part in content:
                    if isinstance(part, dict) and isinstance(part.get("text"), str):
                        part["text"] = process_text(part["text"])

        # 2. Enforce Untagged Context Quota when <pod:object> tags or X-POD-Require-Attested-Context: true are present
        require_attested_ctx = norm_hdrs.get("x-pod-require-attested-context", "").lower() in ("1", "true", "yes")
        if (has_inline_pod_tags or require_attested_ctx) and total_untagged_chars > self.max_untagged_chars:
            reason_codes.append(REASON_UNATTESTED_CONTEXT_OVERFLOW)

        # 3. Evaluate request-level header locators (`X-POD-Locators` or `X-POD-Objects`)
        header_loc_str = ",".join(
            s for s in (norm_hdrs.get("x-pod-locators", "").strip(), norm_hdrs.get("x-pod-objects", "").strip()) if s
        )
        if header_loc_str:
            for raw_loc in [x.strip() for x in header_loc_str.split(",") if x.strip()]:
                if raw_loc not in locators_seen:
                    locators_seen.append(raw_loc)
                if self.strict_inline_hash and not self.allow_header_only_objects and raw_loc not in inline_verified_locators:
                    reason_codes.append(REASON_UNVERIFIED_INLINE_CONTENT)
                    continue
                action, r_codes = self._evaluate_single_locator(
                    locator=raw_loc,
                    inline_sha256=None,
                    entries=entries,
                    caller_clearance=caller_clearance,
                    target_egress=target_egress,
                    now_dt=now_dt,
                    inner_text=None,
                )
                if action == "deny":
                    reason_codes.extend(r_codes)
                elif action == "strip":
                    # Header-level locator has no inline block boundary to strip -> must deny whole request
                    reason_codes.append("EGRESS_DENIED")

        # Deduplicate reason codes preserving discovery order
        unique_reasons = list(dict.fromkeys(reason_codes))

        if unique_reasons:
            if self.mode == "observe":
                audit = AuditLogEntry(
                    timestamp=now_str,
                    mode="observe",
                    decision="observe_would_deny",
                    reason_codes=unique_reasons,
                    locators_evaluated=locators_seen,
                    caller_clearance=caller_clearance,
                    target_egress=target_egress,
                    stripped_locators=stripped_locators,
                )
                self.audit_log.append(audit)
                return EnforcementOutcome(
                    allowed_to_forward=True,
                    transformed_payload=new_payload,
                    client_denial_body=None,
                    audit_entry=audit,
                )

            audit = AuditLogEntry(
                timestamp=now_str,
                mode="enforce",
                decision="deny",
                reason_codes=unique_reasons,
                locators_evaluated=locators_seen,
                caller_clearance=caller_clearance,
                target_egress=target_egress,
                stripped_locators=stripped_locators,
            )
            self.audit_log.append(audit)
            return EnforcementOutcome(
                allowed_to_forward=False,
                transformed_payload=new_payload,
                client_denial_body=self._uniform_denial_response(),
                audit_entry=audit,
            )

        decision_str = "strip" if stripped_locators else "allow"
        audit = AuditLogEntry(
            timestamp=now_str,
            mode=self.mode,
            decision=decision_str,
            reason_codes=[],
            locators_evaluated=locators_seen,
            caller_clearance=caller_clearance,
            target_egress=target_egress,
            stripped_locators=stripped_locators,
        )
        self.audit_log.append(audit)
        return EnforcementOutcome(
            allowed_to_forward=True,
            transformed_payload=new_payload,
            client_denial_body=None,
            audit_entry=audit,
        )


=== FILE: D:\attestation\prevalidator\proxy.py ===
"""FastAPI Prevalidator Proxy speaking standard OpenAI `/v1/chat/completions` and Anthropic `/v1/messages`, plus Directory Batch Prevalidation and Unified Operator Dashboard."""

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import json
import os
import time
import uuid
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, AsyncIterator

import httpx
from fastapi import FastAPI, Header, HTTPException, Query, Request, status
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse, HTMLResponse, JSONResponse, StreamingResponse

from engine.directory_bridge import (
    compute_bytes_sha256_hex,
    discover_selectable_drives_and_directories,
    iter_scannable_files,
    resolve_pc_directory_path,
)
from prevalidator.enforcer import InMemorySnapshotStore, PrevalidatorEnforcer
from registry.signing import Keyring, compute_file_sha256


def _extract_last_user_text(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    messages = payload.get("messages", [])
    for msg in reversed(messages):
        content = msg.get("content", "")
        if isinstance(content, str):
            return content
        if isinstance(content, list):
            return " ".join(p.get("text", "") for p in content if isinstance(p, dict))
    return ""


async def _mock_openai_sse_stream(model: str, text: str) -> AsyncIterator[bytes]:
    chunk_id = f"chatcmpl-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"
    words = [f"[Gateway Ack ({model})]:", *text.split()[:25]]
    for word in words:
        data = {
            "id": chunk_id,
            "object": "chat.completion.chunk",
            "model": model,
            "choices": [{"index": 0, "delta": {"content": word + " "}, "finish_reason": None}],
        }
        yield f"data: {json.dumps(data)}\n\n".encode("utf-8")
    done_chunk = {
        "id": chunk_id,
        "object": "chat.completion.chunk",
        "model": model,
        "choices": [{"index": 0, "delta": {}, "finish_reason": "stop"}],
    }
    yield f"data: {json.dumps(done_chunk)}\n\n".encode("utf-8")
    yield b"data: [DONE]\n\n"


async def _mock_anthropic_sse_stream(model: str, text: str) -> AsyncIterator[bytes]:
    msg_id = f"msg_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"
    start_evt = {
        "type": "message_start",
        "message": {"id": msg_id, "type": "message", "role": "assistant", "model": model, "content": []},
    }
    yield f"event: message_start\ndata: {json.dumps(start_evt)}\n\n".encode("utf-8")
    delta_evt = {
        "type": "content_block_delta",
        "index": 0,
        "delta": {"type": "text_delta", "text": f"[Anthropic Gateway Ack ({model})]: {text[:160]}"},
    }
    yield f"event: content_block_delta\ndata: {json.dumps(delta_evt)}\n\n".encode("utf-8")
    stop_evt = {"type": "message_stop"}
    yield f"event: message_stop\ndata: {json.dumps(stop_evt)}\n\n".encode("utf-8")
