=== FILE: D:\attestation\examples\phase0_worked_example.py ===
"""Phase 0 Worked Example: A single synthetic file attested end-to-end by hand â€” hashed, classified, signed â€” plus verification and tamper rejection."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

from engine.classifier import PresidioClassifier
from registry.signing import (
    Keyring,
    SignatureVerificationError,
    build_attestation_statement,
    compute_file_sha256,
    sign_statement,
    verify_dsse_envelope,
)


def run_phase0_worked_example(work_dir: Path | None = None) -> dict[str, object]:
    base_dir = work_dir or Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="pod_phase0_"))
    base_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # 1. Create synthetic file
    synthetic_file = base_dir / "synthetic_clinical_summary.txt"
    synthetic_file.write_text(
        "Synthetic Clinical Study Brief\n"
        "Cohort Contact: researcher.jane@example.org\n"
        "Internal Project Tag: PROJ-GENOME9\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    # 2. Compute SHA-256 fingerprint
    file_sha256 = compute_file_sha256(synthetic_file)

    # 3. Classify content (types and counts only, never values)
    classifier = PresidioClassifier()
    verdict = classifier.classify_text(synthetic_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

    # 4. Generate local operator keys (Ed25519) & build in-toto Statement v1
    keyring = Keyring(base_dir / "keys")
    key_meta = keyring.generate_key(algorithm="Ed25519", operator_id="phase0-operator")

    locator = "file:///synthetic/synthetic_clinical_summary.txt"
    statement = build_attestation_statement(
        locator=locator,
        content_sha256=file_sha256,
        tier=verdict.tier,
        owner="clinical-data-steward@example.org",
        object_type="document",
        display_name=synthetic_file.name,
        environment="synthetic",
        confidence=verdict.confidence,
        sensitive_categories=[c.to_dict() for c in verdict.sensitive_categories],
        size_bytes=synthetic_file.stat().st_size,
    )

    # 5. Sign into a DSSE envelope
    envelope = sign_statement(statement, keyring)
    envelope_path = base_dir / "synthetic_clinical_summary.dsse.json"
    envelope_path.write_text(json.dumps(envelope, indent=2, sort_keys=True), encoding="utf-8")

    # 6. Verify valid record + untampered file
    verification = verify_dsse_envelope(
        envelope=envelope,
        keyring=keyring,
        expected_content_sha256=compute_file_sha256(synthetic_file),
    )

    # 7. Verify tampered file copy FAILS verification
    tampered_file = base_dir / "synthetic_clinical_summary.tampered.txt"
    tampered_file.write_text(
        synthetic_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8") + "\nInjected unauthorized line\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    tampered_file_rejected = False
    try:
        verify_dsse_envelope(
            envelope=envelope,
            keyring=keyring,
            expected_content_sha256=compute_file_sha256(tampered_file),
        )
    except SignatureVerificationError:
        tampered_file_rejected = True

    # 8. Verify tampered DSSE payload FAILS verification
    tampered_envelope = json.loads(json.dumps(envelope))
    tampered_envelope["payload"] = tampered_envelope["payload"][:-4] + "AAAA"
    tampered_envelope_rejected = False
    try:
        verify_dsse_envelope(envelope=tampered_envelope, keyring=keyring)
    except SignatureVerificationError:
        tampered_envelope_rejected = True

    return {
        "locator": locator,
        "file_sha256": file_sha256,
        "tier": verdict.tier,
        "sensitive_categories": [c.to_dict() for c in verdict.sensitive_categories],
        "keyid": key_meta["keyid"],
        "verified": verification.verified,
        "tampered_file_rejected": tampered_file_rejected,
        "tampered_envelope_rejected": tampered_envelope_rejected,
        "envelope_path": str(envelope_path),
    }


if __name__ == "__main__":
    result = run_phase0_worked_example(Path("./examples/out_phase0"))
    print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))


=== FILE: D:\attestation\examples\synthetic_estate.py ===
"""Golden Synthetic Estate Generator: creates documents, spreadsheets (.xlsx/.csv), and database tables with known ground-truth classifications."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import openpyxl
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, text


def build_synthetic_estate(estate_dir: str | Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Create a deterministic synthetic sample estate on disk for testing and evaluation."""
    root = Path(estate_dir).resolve()
    fileshare = root / "fileshare"
    fileshare.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # 1. Public document (no PII/secrets)
    pub_doc = fileshare / "product_overview_public.md"
    pub_doc.write_text(
        "# Open Source AI Architecture Overview\n\n"
        "This whitepaper describes standard request routing and public documentation practices.\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    # 2. Internal document (internal project codes & internal IP addresses)
    int_doc = fileshare / "engineering_runbook_internal.txt"
    int_doc.write_text(
        "INTERNAL-ONLY Engineering Runbook\n"
        "Project Code: PROJ-ATLAS42\n"
        "Cluster Bastion Subnet: 10.24.18.92\n"
        "Assigned Engineer ID: EMP-40912\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    # 3. Confidential Excel spreadsheet (.xlsx) with employee emails & salary compensation
    conf_xlsx = fileshare / "dept_compensation_confidential.xlsx"
    wb = openpyxl.Workbook()
    ws = wb.active
    ws.title = "Compensation_Q4"
    ws.append(["employee_id", "work_email", "compensation_note", "office_phone"])
    ws.append(["EMP-10021", "diana.prince@example.org", "salary: $145,000", "415-555-0192"])
    ws.append(["EMP-10022", "clark.kent@example.org", "salary: $138,500", "415-555-0144"])
    wb.save(conf_xlsx)
    wb.close()

    # 4. Restricted clinical/PII document (US SSN, Medical Record Number, ICD-10, Genomic variant)
    rest_doc = fileshare / "clinical_intake_restricted.txt"
    rest_doc.write_text(
        "Synthetic Patient Intake Record (DO NOT EXPORT)\n"
        "Patient SSN: 412-58-9021\n"
        "Medical Record: MRN-8839201\n"
        "Primary Diagnosis: ICD10-E11.9\n"
        "Genomic Marker: chr17:43094464:G>A (rs80357906)\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    # 5. Low-confidence ambiguous reference file (triggers PENDING_REVIEW hold)
    low_conf_doc = fileshare / "unverified_notes_ambiguous.txt"
    low_conf_doc.write_text(
        "Legacy Archive Notes\n"
        "Reference marker: AMBIGUOUS_REF_9042\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    # 6. Relational database with 3 tables: public_catalog, customer_contacts, patient_biomarkers
    db_path = root / "synthetic_estate.db"
    if db_path.exists():
        db_path.unlink()
    db_url = f"sqlite:///{db_path.as_posix()}"
    engine = create_engine(db_url)
    with engine.begin() as conn:
        conn.execute(
            text(
                "CREATE TABLE public_catalog ("
                "  sku_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,"
                "  title TEXT NOT NULL,"
                "  category TEXT NOT NULL"
                ")"
            )
        )
        conn.execute(
            text(
                "INSERT INTO public_catalog (sku_id, title, category) VALUES "
                "('SKU-001', 'Standard Gateway Adapter', 'Software'),"
                "('SKU-002', 'Open Schema Validator', 'Tooling')"
            )
        )

        conn.execute(
            text(
                "CREATE TABLE customer_contacts ("
                "  customer_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,"
                "  email TEXT NOT NULL,"
                "  phone TEXT NOT NULL"
                ")"
            )
        )
        conn.execute(
            text(
                "INSERT INTO customer_contacts (customer_id, email, phone) VALUES "
                "('CUST-101', 'buyer.one@example.com', '212-555-0181'),"
                "('CUST-102', 'buyer.two@example.com', '212-555-0182')"
            )
        )

        conn.execute(
            text(
                "CREATE TABLE patient_biomarkers ("
                "  subject_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,"
                "  ssn TEXT NOT NULL,"
                "  mrn TEXT NOT NULL,"
                "  variant_id TEXT NOT NULL"
                ")"
            )
        )
        conn.execute(
            text(
                "INSERT INTO patient_biomarkers (subject_id, ssn, mrn, variant_id) VALUES "
                "('SUBJ-01', '521-44-8192', 'MRN-7710293', 'rs121913529')"
            )
        )
    engine.dispose()

    return {
        "root_dir": str(root),
        "fileshare_dir": str(fileshare),
        "db_url": db_url,
        "files": {
            "public": str(pub_doc),
            "internal": str(int_doc),
            "confidential_xlsx": str(conf_xlsx),
            "restricted": str(rest_doc),
            "low_confidence": str(low_conf_doc),
        },
        "expected_tiers": {
            "file://estate/fileshare/product_overview_public.md": "Public",
            "file://estate/fileshare/engineering_runbook_internal.txt": "Internal",
            "file://estate/fileshare/dept_compensation_confidential.xlsx": "Confidential",
            "file://estate/fileshare/clinical_intake_restricted.txt": "Restricted",
            "postgres://estate-db:5432/estate/public/public_catalog": "Public",
            "postgres://estate-db:5432/estate/public/customer_contacts": "Confidential",
            "postgres://estate-db:5432/estate/public/patient_biomarkers": "Restricted",
        },
    }


=== FILE: D:\attestation\examples\run_demo.py ===
"""Reproducible End-to-End Demo exercising Phases 0, 1, 2, and 3 over the Synthetic Sample Estate."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import tempfile
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from engine.pipeline import AttestationEngine
from examples.phase0_worked_example import run_phase0_worked_example
from examples.synthetic_estate import build_synthetic_estate
from prevalidator.enforcer import InMemorySnapshotStore, PrevalidatorEnforcer
from prevalidator.proxy import create_prevalidator_app
from registry.store import RegistryService
from snapshot.publisher import SnapshotPublisher


def run_end_to_end_demo(work_dir: Path | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    base_dir = work_dir or Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="pod_e2e_demo_"))
    base_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # --- PHASE 0: Worked Single-File Example & Tamper Verification ---
    phase0_summary = run_phase0_worked_example(base_dir / "phase0")

    # --- PHASE 1: Synthetic Estate Crawl (File Share + Database) & Full Lifecycle ---
    estate = build_synthetic_estate(base_dir / "estate")
    db_file = base_dir / "pod_registry.db"
    if db_file.exists():
        db_file.unlink()
    registry_db_url = f"sqlite:///{db_file.as_posix()}"
    key_dir = base_dir / "keys"
    registry = RegistryService(db_url=registry_db_url, key_dir=key_dir)
    engine = AttestationEngine(registry=registry)

    file_results = engine.crawl_file_share(
        root_dir=estate["fileshare_dir"],
        owner="fileshare-steward@example.org",
        locator_base="file://estate/fileshare",
        environment="synthetic",
    )
    db_results = engine.crawl_database(
        db_url=estate["db_url"],
        owner="data-platform@example.org",
        locator_override_base="postgres://estate-db:5432/estate",
        environment="synthetic",
    )

    # Verify low-confidence file was queued in PENDING_REVIEW with no active record
    pending_reviews = registry.list_review_queue(status="PENDING_REVIEW")
    low_conf_reviews = [r for r in pending_reviews if r["reason"] == "low_confidence"]
    assert len(low_conf_reviews) == 1

    # Exercise lifecycle transitions:
    # 1) Raise tier of public catalog table to Internal (free and immediate)
    raised = registry.raise_tier("postgres://estate-db:5432/estate/public/public_catalog", "Internal")
    assert raised["tier"] == "Internal"

    # 2) Lower tier back to Public with dual approval (2 distinct reviewers)
    lowered = registry.lower_tier(
        "postgres://estate-db:5432/estate/public/public_catalog",
        "Public",
        approvals=[
            {"approver_id": "alice-sec@example.org", "rationale": "Verified SKU catalog contains only public SKUs."},
            {"approver_id": "bob-dpo@example.org", "rationale": "Confirmed public release approval."},
        ],
    )
    assert lowered["tier"] == "Public"
    assert lowered["status"] == "tier_lowered"

    # 3) Renew public document record with matching SHA-256
    pub_rec = registry.lookup_by_locator("file://estate/fileshare/product_overview_public.md")
    assert pub_rec is not None
    renewed = registry.renew_record(
        locator="file://estate/fileshare/product_overview_public.md",
        observed_content_sha256=pub_rec["content_sha256"],
    )
    assert renewed["status"] == "renewed"

    # --- PHASE 2: Publish Signed Snapshot ---
    snap_path = base_dir / "pod_snapshot.dsse.json"
    publisher = SnapshotPublisher(registry=registry, ttl_seconds=600, snapshot_output_path=snap_path)
    snapshot_envelope = publisher.publish_snapshot()

    # --- PHASE 3: Prevalidator Proxy (OpenAI & Anthropic, Observe & Enforce, Latency Benchmark) ---
    snap_store = InMemorySnapshotStore(registry.keyring)
    snap_store.load_from_envelope(snapshot_envelope)
    enforcer = PrevalidatorEnforcer(snapshot_store=snap_store, mode="enforce")
    app = create_prevalidator_app(enforcer=enforcer, upstream_gateway_url="mock://local")
    client = TestClient(app)

    # Warm up TestClient and measure pure in-memory snapshot-lookup enforcement latency
    client.get("/healthz")
    sample_req = {
        "model": "gpt-4o",
        "messages": [
            {
                "role": "user",
                "content": (
                    "Summarize this document: "
                    '<pod:object locator="file://estate/fileshare/product_overview_public.md">'
                    "Open Source AI Architecture Overview"
                    "</pod:object>"
                ),
            }
        ],
    }
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    enforcer.evaluate_request(sample_req, headers={"X-POD-Caller-Key": "pod-key-public"})
    allow_latency_ms = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0

    # Case A: Allowed Public file reference on OpenAI `/v1/chat/completions`
    resp_allow = client.post(
        "/v1/chat/completions",
        headers={"X-POD-Caller-Key": "pod-key-public"},
        json=sample_req,
    )
    assert resp_allow.status_code == 200

    # Case B: Stripped Confidential spreadsheet reference on Anthropic `/v1/messages` (local model + Confidential clearance)
    resp_strip = client.post(
        "/v1/messages",
        headers={"X-POD-Caller-Key": "pod-key-confidential"},
        json={
            "model": "local-model",
            "messages": [
                {
                    "role": "user",
                    "content": (
                        "Provide general HR policy guidance. "
                        '<pod:object locator="file://estate/fileshare/dept_compensation_confidential.xlsx">'
                        "diana.prince@example.org | salary: $145,000"
                        "</pod:object>"
                    ),
                }
            ],
        },
    )
    assert resp_strip.status_code == 200
    strip_text = resp_strip.json()["content"][0]["text"]
    assert "diana.prince@example.org" not in strip_text
    assert "[POD:STRIPPED]" in strip_text

    # Case C: Refused Restricted clinical record on OpenAI `/v1/chat/completions`
    resp_refuse_restricted = client.post(
        "/v1/chat/completions",
        headers={"X-POD-Caller-Key": "pod-key-restricted"},
        json={
            "model": "gpt-4o",
            "messages": [
                {
                    "role": "user",
                    "content": (
                        '<pod:object locator="file://estate/fileshare/clinical_intake_restricted.txt">'
                        "Patient SSN: 412-58-9021"
                        "</pod:object>"
                    ),
                }
            ],
        },
    )
    assert resp_refuse_restricted.status_code == 403

    # Case D: Refused Unattested / Low-confidence held file
    resp_refuse_unattested = client.post(
        "/v1/chat/completions",
        headers={"X-POD-Caller-Key": "pod-key-public"},
        json={
            "model": "gpt-4o",
            "messages": [
                {
                    "role": "user",
                    "content": (
                        '<pod:object locator="file://estate/fileshare/unverified_notes_ambiguous.txt">'
                        "AMBIGUOUS_REF_9042"
                        "</pod:object>"
                    ),
                }
            ],
        },
    )
    assert resp_refuse_unattested.status_code == 403
    # Verify uniform denial response across different refusal reasons
    assert resp_refuse_restricted.json() == resp_refuse_unattested.json()

    registry.close()

    return {
        "phase0": phase0_summary,
        "phase1": {
            "files_processed": len(file_results),
            "tables_processed": len(db_results),
            "pending_reviews_count": len(pending_reviews),
            "lifecycle_exercised": ["attest", "raise_tier", "lower_tier_dual_approval", "renew"],
        },
        "phase2": {
            "snapshot_path": str(snap_path),
            "snapshot_signatures": snapshot_envelope["signatures"],
        },
        "phase3": {
            "openai_allow_status": resp_allow.status_code,
            "anthropic_strip_status": resp_strip.status_code,
            "restricted_refusal_status": resp_refuse_restricted.status_code,
            "unattested_refusal_status": resp_refuse_unattested.status_code,
            "uniform_denial_body": resp_refuse_restricted.json(),
            "in_memory_enforcement_latency_ms": round(allow_latency_ms, 3),
        },
    }


if __name__ == "__main__":
    summary = run_end_to_end_demo(Path("./examples/out_demo"))
    print(json.dumps(summary, indent=2))

=== FILE: D:\attestation\examples\docker_bootstrap.py ===
"""Docker Compose Bootstrap: seeds the synthetic estate into Postgres and shared volumes, attests all objects, publishes the signed snapshot, and reloads the Prevalidator."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import time
from pathlib import Path

import httpx
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, text

from engine.pipeline import AttestationEngine
from examples.synthetic_estate import build_synthetic_estate
from registry.store import RegistryService
from snapshot.publisher import SnapshotPublisher


def main() -> int:
    db_url = os.environ.get("POD_REGISTRY_DB_URL", "postgresql+psycopg2://pod:pod_password@postgres:5432/pod_registry")
    if db_url.startswith("postgresql://"):
        db_url = "postgresql+psycopg2://" + db_url[len("postgresql://") :]
    snapshot_file = Path(os.environ.get("POD_SNAPSHOT_FILE", "/var/lib/pod/state/snapshots/pod_snapshot.dsse.json"))
    estate_dir = Path(os.environ.get("POD_ESTATE_DIR", "/var/lib/pod/state/estate"))
    prevalidator_url = os.environ.get("POD_PREVALIDATOR_URL", "http://prevalidator:8082")
    snapshot_publisher_url = os.environ.get("POD_SNAPSHOT_PUBLISHER_URL", "").rstrip("/")
    admin_key = os.environ.get("POD_ADMIN_API_KEY", "pod-dev-admin-key")
    control_token = os.environ.get("POD_CONTROL_PLANE_TOKEN", "pod-dev-control-plane-token")

    # 1. Build synthetic fileshare on volume
    estate = build_synthetic_estate(estate_dir)

    # 2. Seed a dedicated `estate_data` schema inside Postgres so `crawl_database` scans real Postgres tables
    pg_engine = create_engine(db_url)
    with pg_engine.begin() as conn:
        conn.execute(text("CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS estate_data"))
        conn.execute(text("DROP TABLE IF EXISTS estate_data.public_catalog"))
        conn.execute(text("DROP TABLE IF EXISTS estate_data.customer_contacts"))
        conn.execute(text("DROP TABLE IF EXISTS estate_data.patient_biomarkers"))

        conn.execute(
            text(
                "CREATE TABLE estate_data.public_catalog ("
                "  sku_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,"
                "  title TEXT NOT NULL,"
                "  category TEXT NOT NULL"
                ")"
            )
        )
        conn.execute(
            text(
                "INSERT INTO estate_data.public_catalog (sku_id, title, category) VALUES "
                "('SKU-001', 'Standard Gateway Adapter', 'Software'),"
                "('SKU-002', 'Open Schema Validator', 'Tooling')"
            )
        )

        conn.execute(
            text(
                "CREATE TABLE estate_data.customer_contacts ("
                "  customer_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,"
                "  email TEXT NOT NULL,"
                "  phone TEXT NOT NULL"
                ")"
            )
        )
        conn.execute(
            text(
                "INSERT INTO estate_data.customer_contacts (customer_id, email, phone) VALUES "
                "('CUST-101', 'buyer.one@example.com', '212-555-0181'),"
                "('CUST-102', 'buyer.two@example.com', '212-555-0182')"
            )
        )

        conn.execute(
            text(
                "CREATE TABLE estate_data.patient_biomarkers ("
                "  subject_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,"
                "  ssn TEXT NOT NULL,"
                "  mrn TEXT NOT NULL,"
                "  variant_id TEXT NOT NULL"
                ")"
            )
        )
        conn.execute(
            text(
                "INSERT INTO estate_data.patient_biomarkers (subject_id, ssn, mrn, variant_id) VALUES "
                "('SUBJ-01', '521-44-8192', 'MRN-7710293', 'rs121913529')"
            )
        )
    pg_engine.dispose()

    # 3. Run AttestationEngine over fileshare and sqlite/postgres estate
    registry = RegistryService(db_url=db_url)
    engine = AttestationEngine(registry=registry)

    file_results = engine.crawl_file_share(
        root_dir=estate["fileshare_dir"],
        owner="fileshare-steward@example.org",
        locator_base="file://estate/fileshare",
        environment="production",
    )
    db_results = engine.crawl_database(
        db_url=estate["db_url"],
        owner="data-platform@example.org",
        locator_override_base="postgres://estate-db:5432/estate",
        environment="production",
    )

    # 4. Publish signed DSSE snapshot via Snapshot Publisher (or local publisher in single-process mode)
    ctrl_headers = {
        "X-POD-Control-Token": control_token,
        "X-POD-Admin-Key": admin_key,
        "Authorization": f"Bearer {control_token}",
    }
    if snapshot_publisher_url:
        envelope = None
        for _ in range(15):
            try:
                with httpx.Client(timeout=5.0) as client:
                    r_pub = client.post(f"{snapshot_publisher_url}/v1/publish", headers=ctrl_headers)
                    if r_pub.status_code == 200:
                        envelope = r_pub.json()
                        break
            except Exception:
                pass
            time.sleep(1.0)
        if envelope is None:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Failed to trigger snapshot publish at {snapshot_publisher_url}/v1/publish")
    else:
        publisher = SnapshotPublisher(registry=registry, ttl_seconds=3600, snapshot_output_path=snapshot_file)
        envelope = publisher.publish_snapshot()
    registry.close()

    # 5. Notify Prevalidator to reload snapshot in memory
    reloaded = False
    for _ in range(15):
        try:
            with httpx.Client(timeout=3.0) as client:
                r = client.post(
                    f"{prevalidator_url.rstrip('/')}/v1/admin/reload-snapshot",
                    headers=ctrl_headers,
                    json={"envelope": envelope},
                )
                if r.status_code == 200:
                    reloaded = True
                    break
        except Exception:
            time.sleep(1.0)

    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "bootstrap": "completed",
                "files_attested": len(file_results),
                "tables_attested": len(db_results),
                "snapshot_file": str(snapshot_file),
                "prevalidator_reloaded": reloaded,
            },
            indent=2,
        )
    )
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


=== FILE: D:\attestation\examples\local_gateway.py ===
"""Local Open-Source AI Gateway service (OpenAI `/v1/chat/completions` & Anthropic `/v1/messages`) for the Docker `demo` profile."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import uuid
from typing import Any, AsyncIterator

from fastapi import FastAPI, Request
from fastapi.responses import StreamingResponse


def _extract_last_text(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    for msg in reversed(payload.get("messages", [])):
        content = msg.get("content", "")
        if isinstance(content, str):
            return content
        if isinstance(content, list):
            return " ".join(p.get("text", "") for p in content if isinstance(p, dict))
    return ""


async def _openai_stream(model: str, text: str) -> AsyncIterator[bytes]:
    cid = f"chatcmpl-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"
    for token in [f"[Gateway ({model})]:", *text.split()[:30]]:
        chunk = {
            "id": cid,
            "object": "chat.completion.chunk",
            "model": model,
            "choices": [{"index": 0, "delta": {"content": token + " "}, "finish_reason": None}],
        }
        yield f"data: {json.dumps(chunk)}\n\n".encode("utf-8")
    done = {
        "id": cid,
        "object": "chat.completion.chunk",
        "model": model,
        "choices": [{"index": 0, "delta": {}, "finish_reason": "stop"}],
    }
    yield f"data: {json.dumps(done)}\n\n".encode("utf-8")
    yield b"data: [DONE]\n\n"


async def _anthropic_stream(model: str, text: str) -> AsyncIterator[bytes]:
    mid = f"msg_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"
    start = {
        "type": "message_start",
        "message": {"id": mid, "type": "message", "role": "assistant", "model": model, "content": []},
    }
    yield f"event: message_start\ndata: {json.dumps(start)}\n\n".encode("utf-8")
    delta = {
        "type": "content_block_delta",
        "index": 0,
        "delta": {"type": "text_delta", "text": f"[Anthropic Gateway ({model})]: {text[:200]}"},
    }
    yield f"event: content_block_delta\ndata: {json.dumps(delta)}\n\n".encode("utf-8")
    yield b'event: message_stop\ndata: {"type": "message_stop"}\n\n'


def create_gateway_app() -> FastAPI:
    app = FastAPI(title="POD Demo Local AI Gateway (OpenAI & Anthropic)", version="0.1.0")

    @app.get("/healthz")
    def healthz() -> dict[str, str]:
        return {"status": "ok", "service": "pod-demo-ai-gateway"}

    @app.get("/v1/models")
    def list_models() -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "object": "list",
            "data": [
                {"id": "local-model", "object": "model", "owned_by": "g1-docket-local"},
                {"id": "vpc-model", "object": "model", "owned_by": "g1-docket-vpc"},
                {"id": "gpt-4o", "object": "model", "owned_by": "openai"},
                {"id": "claude-3-5-sonnet", "object": "model", "owned_by": "anthropic"},
            ],
        }

    @app.post("/v1/chat/completions")
    async def chat_completions(request: Request) -> Any:
        payload = await request.json()
        model = payload.get("model", "gpt-4o")
        text = _extract_last_text(payload)
        if payload.get("stream"):
            return StreamingResponse(_openai_stream(model, text), media_type="text/event-stream")
        return {
            "id": f"chatcmpl-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}",
            "object": "chat.completion",
            "model": model,
            "choices": [
                {
                    "index": 0,
                    "message": {"role": "assistant", "content": f"[Gateway ({model})]: {text}"},
                    "finish_reason": "stop",
                }
            ],
        }

    @app.post("/v1/messages")
    async def messages(request: Request) -> Any:
        payload = await request.json()
        model = payload.get("model", "claude-3-5-sonnet")
        text = _extract_last_text(payload)
        if payload.get("stream"):
            return StreamingResponse(_anthropic_stream(model, text), media_type="text/event-stream")
        return {
            "id": f"msg_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}",
            "type": "message",
            "role": "assistant",
            "model": model,
            "content": [{"type": "text", "text": f"[Anthropic Gateway ({model})]: {text}"}],
            "stop_reason": "end_turn",
        }

    return app


=== FILE: D:\attestation\examples\verify_docker_stack.py ===
"""Host-side live verification script against the running Docker Compose `demo` stack."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import time

import httpx


def main() -> int:
    registry_url = "http://localhost:18080"
    snapshot_url = "http://localhost:18081"
    prevalidator_url = "http://localhost:18082"
    engine_url = "http://localhost:18083"
    gateway_url = "http://localhost:14000"
    admin_headers = {"X-POD-Admin-Key": "pod-dev-admin-key"}

    with httpx.Client(timeout=10.0) as client:
        # Wait up to 20s for engine bootstrap to populate Registry and reload Prevalidator
        for _ in range(20):
            try:
                r_prev = client.get(f"{prevalidator_url}/healthz")
                if r_prev.status_code == 200 and r_prev.json().get("status") == "ok":
                    break
            except Exception:
                pass
            time.sleep(1.0)

        reg_records = client.get(f"{registry_url}/v1/records", headers=admin_headers).json()["records"]
        reg_reviews = client.get(f"{registry_url}/v1/reviews", headers=admin_headers).json()["reviews"]
        snap_latest = client.get(f"{snapshot_url}/v1/snapshot/latest").json()
        prev_health = client.get(f"{prevalidator_url}/healthz").json()
        eng_health = client.get(f"{engine_url}/healthz").json()
        gw_health = client.get(f"{gateway_url}/healthz").json()

        # 1. OpenAI `/v1/chat/completions` ALLOW on Public attested document
        r_allow = client.post(
            f"{prevalidator_url}/v1/chat/completions",
            headers={"X-POD-Caller-Key": "pod-key-public"},
            json={
                "model": "gpt-4o",
                "messages": [
                    {
                        "role": "user",
                        "content": (
                            "Summarize: "
                            '<pod:object locator="file://estate/fileshare/product_overview_public.md">'
                            "Open Source AI Architecture Overview"
                            "</pod:object>"
                        ),
                    }
                ],
            },
        )

        # 2. Anthropic `/v1/messages` STRIP on Confidential Excel spreadsheet
        r_strip = client.post(
            f"{prevalidator_url}/v1/messages",
            headers={"X-POD-Caller-Key": "pod-key-confidential"},
            json={
                "model": "local-model",
                "messages": [
                    {
                        "role": "user",
                        "content": (
                            "Answer HR policy question. "
                            '<pod:object locator="file://estate/fileshare/dept_compensation_confidential.xlsx">'
                            "diana.prince@example.org | salary: $145,000"
                            "</pod:object>"
                        ),
                    }
                ],
            },
        )

        # 3. OpenAI `/v1/chat/completions` REFUSE (403) on Restricted clinical record
        r_deny_restricted = client.post(
            f"{prevalidator_url}/v1/chat/completions",
            headers={"X-POD-Caller-Key": "pod-key-restricted"},
            json={
                "model": "gpt-4o",
                "messages": [
                    {
                        "role": "user",
                        "content": (
                            '<pod:object locator="file://estate/fileshare/clinical_intake_restricted.txt">'
                            "Patient SSN: 412-58-9021"
                            "</pod:object>"
                        ),
                    }
                ],
            },
        )

        # 4. OpenAI `/v1/chat/completions` REFUSE (403) on Unattested object
        r_deny_unattested = client.post(
            f"{prevalidator_url}/v1/chat/completions",
            headers={"X-POD-Caller-Key": "pod-key-public"},
            json={
                "model": "gpt-4o",
                "messages": [
                    {
                        "role": "user",
                        "content": '<pod:object locator="file://estate/fileshare/unknown_secret.txt">Secret</pod:object>',
                    }
                ],
            },
        )

        audit_log = client.get(f"{prevalidator_url}/v1/admin/audit-log", headers=admin_headers).json()["entries"]

        # 5. Test PC Directory Attestation + Prevalidator Directory Scan + Unattested File Drop workflow
        r_att_dir = client.post(
            f"{prevalidator_url}/v1/admin/attest-directory",
            headers=admin_headers,
            json={"directory_path": "D:\\attestation\\pc_demo_folder"},
        )
        assert r_att_dir.status_code == 200, r_att_dir.text
        att_dir_data = r_att_dir.json()

        r_pv_before = client.post(
            f"{prevalidator_url}/v1/prevalidate-directory",
            json={
                "directory_path": "D:\\attestation\\pc_demo_folder",
                "caller_key": "pod-key-confidential",
                "model": "local-model",
            },
        )
        assert r_pv_before.status_code == 200, r_pv_before.text
        pv_before = r_pv_before.json()

        # Drop an unattested file into D:\attestation\pc_demo_folder and re-run Prevalidator Directory Scan
        r_drop = client.post(
            f"{prevalidator_url}/v1/admin/add-unattested-file",
            headers=admin_headers,
            json={
                "directory_path": "D:\\attestation\\pc_demo_folder",
                "filename": "07_rogue_unattested_note.txt",
            },
        )
        assert r_drop.status_code == 200, r_drop.text

        r_pv_after = client.post(
            f"{prevalidator_url}/v1/prevalidate-directory",
            json={
                "directory_path": "D:\\attestation\\pc_demo_folder",
                "caller_key": "pod-key-confidential",
                "model": "local-model",
            },
        )
        assert r_pv_after.status_code == 200, r_pv_after.text
        pv_after = r_pv_after.json()

    summary = {
        "services": {
            "engine": {"url": engine_url, "health": eng_health},
            "registry": {"url": registry_url, "active_records": len(reg_records), "pending_reviews": len(reg_reviews)},
            "snapshot_publisher": {"url": snapshot_url, "signatures": snap_latest.get("signatures")},
            "prevalidator": {"url": prevalidator_url, "health": prev_health},
            "ai_gateway": {"url": gateway_url, "health": gw_health},
        },
        "pc_directory_workflow": {
            "directory": "D:\\attestation\\pc_demo_folder",
            "attested_files": att_dir_data["attested_active_count"],
            "scan_before_unattested_drop": {
                "total": pv_before["total_files"],
                "passed": pv_before["passed_count"],
                "failed": pv_before["failed_count"],
                "unattested": pv_before["unattested_count"],
            },
            "scan_after_unattested_drop": {
                "total": pv_after["total_files"],
                "passed": pv_after["passed_count"],
                "failed": pv_after["failed_count"],
                "unattested": pv_after["unattested_count"],
            },
        },
        "live_requests": {
            "openai_allow": {
                "status_code": r_allow.status_code,
                "gateway_reply": r_allow.json()["choices"][0]["message"]["content"],
            },
            "anthropic_strip": {
                "status_code": r_strip.status_code,
                "gateway_reply": r_strip.json()["content"][0]["text"],
            },
            "restricted_refusal": {
                "status_code": r_deny_restricted.status_code,
                "body": r_deny_restricted.json(),
            },
            "unattested_refusal": {
                "status_code": r_deny_unattested.status_code,
                "body": r_deny_unattested.json(),
            },
        },
        "audit_log_count": len(audit_log),
    }
    print(json.dumps(summary, indent=2))
    assert r_allow.status_code == 200
    assert r_strip.status_code == 200
    assert "[POD:STRIPPED]" in r_strip.json()["content"][0]["text"]
    assert r_deny_restricted.status_code == 403
    assert r_deny_unattested.status_code == 403
    assert r_deny_restricted.json() == r_deny_unattested.json()

    # Verify cryptographic privilege separation: prevalidator cannot access any *.priv.pem file
    import subprocess

    priv_check = subprocess.run(
        ["docker", "compose", "exec", "-T", "prevalidator", "sh", "-c", "find /var/lib/pod -name '*.priv.pem' 2>/dev/null"],
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=False,
    )
    assert priv_check.stdout.strip() == "", f"Private key exposed inside prevalidator: {priv_check.stdout}"
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


=== FILE: D:\attestation\tests\test_phase0_signing_and_schema.py ===
"""Phase 0 Tests: Schema invariants, 7 mandatory fields, DSSE signing, key rotation, and tamper detection."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from examples.phase0_worked_example import run_phase0_worked_example
from registry.schema_validator import SchemaValidationError, validate_attestation_predicate
from registry.signing import (
    Keyring,
    SignatureVerificationError,
    build_attestation_statement,
    compute_bytes_sha256,
    sign_statement,
    verify_dsse_envelope,
)


def test_phase0_worked_example_end_to_end(tmp_path: Path):
    summary = run_phase0_worked_example(tmp_path)
    assert summary["verified"] is True
    assert summary["tampered_file_rejected"] is True
    assert summary["tampered_envelope_rejected"] is True
    assert summary["tier"] == "Confidential"


def test_schema_rejects_signature_inside_predicate():
    now = datetime(2026, 10, 29, 12, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    stmt = build_attestation_statement(
        locator="file:///test/doc.txt",
        content_sha256=compute_bytes_sha256(b"hello"),
        tier="Public",
        owner="owner@example.org",
        issued_at=now,
    )
    pred = dict(stmt["predicate"])
    pred["signature"] = "illegal-embedded-signature"
    with pytest.raises(SchemaValidationError, match="outer DSSE envelope"):
        validate_attestation_predicate(pred)


def test_schema_enforces_tier_max_ttl():
    now = datetime(2026, 10, 29, 12, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    # Restricted max TTL is 30 days; attempting 45 days must fail
    with pytest.raises(SchemaValidationError, match="maximum TTL of 30 days"):
        build_attestation_statement(
            locator="file:///test/restricted.txt",
            content_sha256=compute_bytes_sha256(b"secret"),
            tier="Restricted",
            owner="sec@example.org",
            ttl_days=45,
            issued_at=now,
        )


def test_schema_rejects_raw_values_in_sensitive_categories():
    now = datetime(2026, 10, 29, 12, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    with pytest.raises(SchemaValidationError):
        build_attestation_statement(
            locator="file:///test/leak.txt",
            content_sha256=compute_bytes_sha256(b"ssn"),
            tier="Restricted",
            owner="sec@example.org",
            sensitive_categories=[{"category": "US_SSN", "count": 1, "value": "123-45-6789"}],
            issued_at=now,
        )


def test_key_rotation_and_revocation_windows(tmp_path: Path):
    keyring = Keyring(tmp_path / "keys")
    k1 = keyring.generate_key(algorithm="Ed25519")
    t1 = datetime(2026, 10, 29, 10, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)

    stmt1 = build_attestation_statement(
        locator="file:///test/k1.txt",
        content_sha256=compute_bytes_sha256(b"data-1"),
        tier="Public",
        owner="ops@example.org",
        issued_at=t1,
    )
    env1 = sign_statement(stmt1, keyring)

    # Rotate to ECDSA P-256 key
    k2 = keyring.rotate_key(algorithm="ECDSA_P256_SHA256")
    assert k2["keyid"] != k1["keyid"]

    # Record signed by k1 BEFORE rotation still verifies
    ver1 = verify_dsse_envelope(env1, keyring, now=t1 + timedelta(hours=1))
    assert ver1.verified is True
    assert ver1.keyid == k1["keyid"]

    # New record signed by active k2 verifies
    stmt2 = build_attestation_statement(
        locator="file:///test/k2.txt",
        content_sha256=compute_bytes_sha256(b"data-2"),
        tier="Public",
        owner="ops@example.org",
        issued_at=t1 + timedelta(hours=1),
    )
    env2 = sign_statement(stmt2, keyring)
    ver2 = verify_dsse_envelope(env2, keyring, now=t1 + timedelta(hours=2))
    assert ver2.verified is True
    assert ver2.keyid == k2["keyid"]

    # Revoking k1 immediately invalidates env1
    keyring.revoke_key(k1["keyid"])
    with pytest.raises(SignatureVerificationError, match="revoked"):
        verify_dsse_envelope(env1, keyring, now=t1 + timedelta(hours=2))


=== FILE: D:\attestation\tests\test_phase1_engine_and_lifecycle.py ===
"""Phase 1 Tests: Golden corpus classification regression, Registry lifecycle, dual approval, OpenLineage, and CLI."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from engine.pipeline import AttestationEngine
from examples.synthetic_estate import build_synthetic_estate
from registry.cli import main as cli_main
from registry.signing import compute_file_sha256
from registry.store import (
    DualApprovalRequiredError,
    FingerprintChangedError,
    RegistryService,
)


def test_golden_corpus_classification_and_lifecycle(tmp_path: Path):
    estate = build_synthetic_estate(tmp_path / "estate")
    db_url = f"sqlite:///{(tmp_path / 'registry.db').as_posix()}"
    registry = RegistryService(db_url=db_url, key_dir=tmp_path / "keys")
    engine = AttestationEngine(registry=registry)

    engine.crawl_file_share(
        root_dir=estate["fileshare_dir"],
        owner="fileshare-owner@example.org",
        locator_base="file://estate/fileshare",
    )
    engine.crawl_database(
        db_url=estate["db_url"],
        owner="db-owner@example.org",
        locator_override_base="postgres://estate-db:5432/estate",
    )

    # Check every expected ground-truth classification tier across files, .xlsx spreadsheets, and DB tables
    for loc, expected_tier in estate["expected_tiers"].items():
        rec = registry.lookup_by_locator(loc)
        assert rec is not None, f"Missing record for {loc}"
        assert rec["tier"] == expected_tier, f"Locator {loc} expected {expected_tier}, got {rec['tier']}"

    # Low-confidence file must NOT have an active record until reviewed
    ambig_loc = "file://estate/fileshare/unverified_notes_ambiguous.txt"
    assert registry.lookup_by_locator(ambig_loc) is None
    reviews = registry.list_review_queue(status="PENDING_REVIEW")
    low_conf = [r for r in reviews if r["locator"] == ambig_loc]
    assert len(low_conf) == 1

    # Approve the low-confidence item in human review -> now issues an active record
    approved = registry.approve_pending_review(
        review_id=low_conf[0]["review_id"],
        approver_id="steward-1@example.org",
        confirmed_tier="Internal",
    )
    assert approved["record"]["tier"] == "Internal"
    assert registry.lookup_by_locator(ambig_loc) is not None

    # Asymmetric tier lowering: single approver or duplicate approver MUST fail
    rest_loc = "file://estate/fileshare/clinical_intake_restricted.txt"
    with pytest.raises(DualApprovalRequiredError):
        registry.lower_tier(
            locator=rest_loc,
            new_tier="Confidential",
            approvals=[{"approver_id": "reviewer-a@example.org", "rationale": "Single approval attempt"}],
        )
    with pytest.raises(DualApprovalRequiredError):
        registry.lower_tier(
            locator=rest_loc,
            new_tier="Confidential",
            approvals=[
                {"approver_id": "reviewer-a@example.org", "rationale": "First"},
                {"approver_id": "reviewer-a@example.org", "rationale": "Same person twice"},
            ],
        )

    # Two distinct approvers succeed
    lowered = registry.lower_tier(
        locator=rest_loc,
        new_tier="Confidential",
        approvals=[
            {"approver_id": "reviewer-a@example.org", "rationale": "De-identified cohort verified"},
            {"approver_id": "reviewer-b@example.org", "rationale": "Second independent sign-off"},
        ],
    )
    assert lowered["tier"] == "Confidential"
    assert lowered["status"] == "tier_lowered"

    # Fingerprint change blocks blind renewal (must re-classify, not re-stamp)
    pub_loc = "file://estate/fileshare/product_overview_public.md"
    with pytest.raises(FingerprintChangedError):
        registry.renew_record(locator=pub_loc, observed_content_sha256="0" * 64)

    registry.close()


def test_openlineage_derived_from_tier_inheritance(tmp_path: Path):
    db_url = f"sqlite:///{(tmp_path / 'lineage_reg.db').as_posix()}"
    registry = RegistryService(db_url=db_url, key_dir=tmp_path / "keys")
    engine = AttestationEngine(registry=registry)

    # Create upstream Restricted file and downstream summary file with no obvious PII
    up_file = tmp_path / "upstream_restricted.txt"
    up_file.write_text("Patient SSN: 443-22-1920\n", encoding="utf-8")
    down_file = tmp_path / "downstream_rollup.txt"
    down_file.write_text("Cohort aggregate summary metric: 42\n", encoding="utf-8")

    up_loc = "file://lineage/upstream_restricted.txt"
    down_loc = "file://lineage/downstream_rollup.txt"

    engine.attest_file(up_file, locator_override=up_loc)
    engine.ingest_openlineage_event(
        {
            "eventType": "COMPLETE",
            "eventTime": "2026-10-29T12:00:00Z",
            "run": {"runId": "run-001"},
            "job": {"namespace": "etl", "name": "cohort_rollup"},
            "inputs": [{"namespace": "file://lineage", "name": "upstream_restricted.txt"}],
            "outputs": [{"namespace": "file://lineage", "name": "downstream_rollup.txt"}],
        }
    )

    down_res = engine.attest_file(down_file, locator_override=down_loc)
    # Because downstream_rollup derives from a Restricted object, it inherits the Restricted floor
    assert down_res["record"]["tier"] == "Restricted"
    derived_links = down_res["record"]["statement"]["predicate"]["provenance"]["derived_from"]
    assert len(derived_links) == 1
    assert derived_links[0]["locator"] == up_loc
    registry.close()


def test_cli_lifecycle_end_to_end(tmp_path: Path):
    db_url = f"sqlite:///{(tmp_path / 'cli.db').as_posix()}"
    key_dir = str(tmp_path / "keys")
    doc_path = tmp_path / "sample.txt"
    doc_path.write_text("Public technical note.", encoding="utf-8")
    env_out = tmp_path / "sample.dsse.json"
    snap_out = tmp_path / "snap.dsse.json"
    loc = "file:///cli/sample.txt"

    assert cli_main(["--db-url", db_url, "--key-dir", key_dir, "keygen"]) == 0
    assert (
        cli_main(
            [
                "--db-url",
                db_url,
                "--key-dir",
                key_dir,
                "attest",
                str(doc_path),
                "--locator",
                loc,
                "--output",
                str(env_out),
            ]
        )
        == 0
    )
    assert cli_main(["--db-url", db_url, "--key-dir", key_dir, "lookup", loc]) == 0
    assert cli_main(["--db-url", db_url, "--key-dir", key_dir, "verify", str(env_out), "--file", str(doc_path)]) == 0
    assert cli_main(["--db-url", db_url, "--key-dir", key_dir, "raise-tier", loc, "Confidential"]) == 0
    assert (
        cli_main(
            [
                "--db-url",
                db_url,
                "--key-dir",
                key_dir,
                "lower-tier",
                loc,
                "Internal",
                "--approver",
                "alice@example.org",
                "--approver",
                "bob@example.org",
            ]
        )
        == 0
    )
    assert (
        cli_main(
            [
                "--db-url",
                db_url,
                "--key-dir",
                key_dir,
                "renew",
                loc,
                "--sha256",
                compute_file_sha256(doc_path),
            ]
        )
        == 0
    )
    assert cli_main(["--db-url", db_url, "--key-dir", key_dir, "list-expiring", "--within-days", "365"]) == 0
    assert cli_main(["--db-url", db_url, "--key-dir", key_dir, "publish-snapshot", "--output", str(snap_out)]) == 0
    assert cli_main(["--db-url", db_url, "--key-dir", key_dir, "revoke", loc, "--reason", "Test complete"]) == 0

"""Phase 3 Tests: Prevalidator Proxy (OpenAI & Anthropic, streaming, Observe mode, Presidio fallback, Registry API auth)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import jwt
import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from engine.pipeline import AttestationEngine
from examples.run_demo import run_end_to_end_demo
from prevalidator.enforcer import (
    REASON_HASH_MISMATCH,
    REASON_MALFORMED_POD_TAG,
    REASON_UNATTESTED_CONTEXT_OVERFLOW,
    REASON_UNVERIFIED_INLINE_CONTENT,
    InMemorySnapshotStore,
    PrevalidatorEnforcer,
)
from prevalidator.proxy import create_prevalidator_app
from registry.api import create_registry_app
from registry.signing import Keyring, SignatureVerificationError, compute_bytes_sha256
from registry.store import RegistryService
from snapshot.publisher import SnapshotPublisher, create_snapshot_app


def test_end_to_end_demo_runner(tmp_path: Path):
    summary = run_end_to_end_demo(tmp_path)
    assert summary["phase0"]["verified"] is True
    assert summary["phase3"]["openai_allow_status"] == 200
    assert summary["phase3"]["anthropic_strip_status"] == 200
    assert summary["phase3"]["restricted_refusal_status"] == 403
    assert summary["phase3"]["unattested_refusal_status"] == 403


def test_prevalidator_observe_mode_and_streaming_and_fallback(tmp_path: Path):
    db_url = f"sqlite:///{(tmp_path / 'prev.db').as_posix()}"
    registry = RegistryService(db_url=db_url, key_dir=tmp_path / "keys")

    pub_sha = compute_bytes_sha256(b"Clean public text")
    registry.issue_or_queue_finding(
        locator="file:///docs/public.txt",
        content_sha256=pub_sha,
        tier="Public",
        owner="docs@example.org",
    )

    int_sha = compute_bytes_sha256(b"Internal architecture note")
    registry.issue_or_queue_finding(
        locator="file:///docs/internal.txt",
        content_sha256=int_sha,
        tier="Internal",
        owner="eng@example.org",
    )

    pub = SnapshotPublisher(registry=registry, snapshot_output_path=tmp_path / "snap.dsse.json")
    env = pub.publish_snapshot()

    store = InMemorySnapshotStore(registry.keyring)
    store.load_from_envelope(env)
    enforcer = PrevalidatorEnforcer(
        snapshot_store=store,
        mode="observe",
        jwt_secret="test-jwt-secret-key-at-least-32-bytes-long",
    )
    app = create_prevalidator_app(enforcer=enforcer, upstream_gateway_url="mock://local")
    client = TestClient(app)

    # 1. In OBSERVE mode, an unattested locator is forwarded AND logged with reason_code="UNATTESTED"
    r_obs = client.post(
        "/v1/chat/completions",
        json={
            "model": "gpt-4o",
            "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": '<pod:object locator="file:///docs/missing.txt">Unattested</pod:object>'}],
        },
    )
    assert r_obs.status_code == 200
    audit_resp = client.get("/v1/admin/audit-log", headers={"X-POD-Admin-Key": "pod-dev-admin-key"})
    assert audit_resp.status_code == 200
    last_entry = audit_resp.json()["entries"][-1]
    assert last_entry["decision"] == "observe_would_deny"
    assert "UNATTESTED" in last_entry["reason_codes"]

    # 2. Switch to ENFORCE mode via admin API
    mode_resp = client.post(
        "/v1/admin/mode",
        headers={"X-POD-Admin-Key": "pod-dev-admin-key"},
        json={"mode": "enforce"},
    )
    assert mode_resp.status_code == 200

    # 3. Fallback Presidio content inspection catches raw untagged SSN in free text
    r_fallback = client.post(
        "/v1/chat/completions",
        json={
            "model": "gpt-4o",
            "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": "Here is an untagged SSN in prompt: 412-58-9021"}],
        },
    )
    assert r_fallback.status_code == 403

    # 4. Signed JWT caller clearance + SSE streaming on both OpenAI and Anthropic endpoints
    token = jwt.encode(
        {"sub": "eng-user", "clearance": "Internal"},
        "test-jwt-secret-key-at-least-32-bytes-long",
        algorithm="HS256",
    )
    r_stream_oai = client.post(
        "/v1/chat/completions",
        headers={"X-POD-Caller-Token": token},
        json={
            "model": "vpc-model",
            "stream": True,
            "messages": [
                {
                    "role": "user",
                    "content": f'<pod:object locator="file:///docs/internal.txt" sha256="{int_sha}">Internal architecture note</pod:object>',
                }
            ],
        },
    )
    assert r_stream_oai.status_code == 200
    assert "data: [DONE]" in r_stream_oai.text

    r_stream_ant = client.post(
        "/v1/messages",
        headers={"X-POD-Caller-Token": token},
        json={
            "model": "vpc-model",
            "stream": True,
            "messages": [
                {
                    "role": "user",
                    "content": f'<pod:object locator="file:///docs/internal.txt" sha256="{int_sha}">Internal architecture note</pod:object>',
                }
            ],
        },
    )
    assert r_stream_ant.status_code == 200
    assert "event: message_stop" in r_stream_ant.text
    registry.close()

def test_adversarial_1_spoofed_tag_content_rejected(tmp_path: Path):
    """Test 1 (Spoofed Tag Content): Pass a valid <pod:object locator="..." sha256="<valid_public_hash>">TAMPERED CONFIDENTIAL TEXT</pod:object>. Assert 403 Forbidden with REASON_HASH_MISMATCH."""
    db_url = f"sqlite:///{(tmp_path / 'adv1.db').as_posix()}"
    registry = RegistryService(db_url=db_url, key_dir=tmp_path / "keys")

    valid_public_text = b"Approved Public Release Document v1.0"
    valid_public_hash = compute_bytes_sha256(valid_public_text)
    registry.issue_or_queue_finding(
        locator="file:///docs/public_release.txt",
        content_sha256=valid_public_hash,
        tier="Public",
        owner="comms@example.org",
    )

    pub = SnapshotPublisher(registry=registry, snapshot_output_path=tmp_path / "snap.dsse.json")
    env = pub.publish_snapshot()

    store = InMemorySnapshotStore(registry.keyring)
    store.load_from_envelope(env)
    enforcer = PrevalidatorEnforcer(snapshot_store=store, mode="enforce")
    app = create_prevalidator_app(enforcer=enforcer, upstream_gateway_url="mock://local")
    client = TestClient(app)

    resp = client.post(
        "/v1/chat/completions",
        headers={"X-POD-Caller-Key": "pod-key-public"},
        json={
            "model": "gpt-4o",
            "messages": [
                {
                    "role": "user",
                    "content": (
                        f'<pod:object locator="file:///docs/public_release.txt" sha256="{valid_public_hash}">'
                        "TAMPERED CONFIDENTIAL TEXT"
                        "</pod:object>"
                    ),
                }
            ],
        },
    )
    assert resp.status_code == 403
    assert resp.json()["error"]["code"] == "policy_denied"
    assert REASON_HASH_MISMATCH in enforcer.audit_log[-1].reason_codes

    # Also verify header-only X-POD-Objects without inline verification is rejected with REASON_UNVERIFIED_INLINE_CONTENT
    resp_hdr_only = client.post(
        "/v1/chat/completions",
        headers={"X-POD-Caller-Key": "pod-key-public", "X-POD-Objects": "file:///docs/public_release.txt"},
        json={
            "model": "gpt-4o",
            "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": "Unverified prompt claiming header object"}],
        },
    )
    assert resp_hdr_only.status_code == 403
    assert REASON_UNVERIFIED_INLINE_CONTENT in enforcer.audit_log[-1].reason_codes
    registry.close()

    """Test 2 (Valid Canonical Chunk Attestation): Attest a multi-paragraph document, then send a single legitimate extracted paragraph inside <pod:object locator="...">legitimate paragraph</pod:object> without sha256 attribute. Assert 200 OK verified via chunk_hashes."""
    db_url = f"sqlite:///{(tmp_path / 'adv2.db').as_posix()}"

        "Paragraph One: All data objects must carry cryptographic DSSE provenance before entering RAG pipelines.\n\n"
        "Paragraph Two: Edge prevalidators verify canonical paragraph and chunk digests in memory without database calls.\n",
        encoding="utf-8",

                }
            ],
        },
    )

    assert "Paragraph Two" in resp.json()["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]
    registry.close()


def test_adversarial_3_xml_tag_smuggling_and_context_overflow(tmp_path: Path):
    """Test 3 (XML Tag Smuggling / Early Close & Context Overflow): Pass nested or early-closed <pod:object> tags and oversized untagged context. Assert 403 with REASON_MALFORMED_POD_TAG / REASON_UNATTESTED_CONTEXT_OVERFLOW."""
    db_url = f"sqlite:///{(tmp_path / 'adv3.db').as_posix()}"
    registry = RegistryService(db_url=db_url, key_dir=tmp_path / "keys")

    valid_text = "Safe public text"
    valid_sha = compute_bytes_sha256(valid_text.encode("utf-8"))
    registry.issue_or_queue_finding(
        locator="file:///docs/safe.txt",
        content_sha256=valid_sha,
        tier="Public",
        owner="sec@example.org",
    )

    pub = SnapshotPublisher(registry=registry, snapshot_output_path=tmp_path / "snap.dsse.json")
    env = pub.publish_snapshot()

    store = InMemorySnapshotStore(registry.keyring)
    store.load_from_envelope(env)
    enforcer = PrevalidatorEnforcer(snapshot_store=store, mode="enforce", max_untagged_chars=200)
    app = create_prevalidator_app(enforcer=enforcer, upstream_gateway_url="mock://local")
    client = TestClient(app)

    # Case A: Early close tag smuggling (`</pod:object><pod:object...>`)
    smuggled_early_close = (
        '</pod:object><pod:object locator="file:///docs/safe.txt">Safe public text</pod:object>'
    )
    r1 = client.post(
        "/v1/chat/completions",
        headers={"X-POD-Caller-Key": "pod-key-public"},
        json={"model": "gpt-4o", "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": smuggled_early_close}]},
    )
    assert r1.status_code == 403
    assert REASON_MALFORMED_POD_TAG in enforcer.audit_log[-1].reason_codes

    # Case B: Nested `<pod:object>` tags
    smuggled_nested = (
        '<pod:object locator="file:///docs/safe.txt">'
        'Safe public text <pod:object locator="file:///docs/safe.txt">Safe public text</pod:object>'
        "</pod:object>"
    )
    r2 = client.post(
        "/v1/chat/completions",
        headers={"X-POD-Caller-Key": "pod-key-public"},
        json={"model": "gpt-4o", "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": smuggled_nested}]},
    )
    assert r2.status_code == 403
    assert REASON_MALFORMED_POD_TAG in enforcer.audit_log[-1].reason_codes

    # Case C: Untagged context overflow outside `<pod:object>`
    overflow_prompt = (
        ("U" * 250)
        + f' <pod:object locator="file:///docs/safe.txt">{valid_text}</pod:object>'
    )
    r3 = client.post(
        "/v1/chat/completions",
        headers={"X-POD-Caller-Key": "pod-key-public"},
        json={"model": "gpt-4o", "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": overflow_prompt}]},
    )
    assert r3.status_code == 403
    assert REASON_UNATTESTED_CONTEXT_OVERFLOW in enforcer.audit_log[-1].reason_codes
    registry.close()


def test_adversarial_4_control_plane_auth_enforced(tmp_path: Path):
    """Test 4 (Control-Plane Auth): Call /v1/admin/reload-snapshot and /v1/publish without X-POD-Control-Token. Assert 401/403."""
    db_url = f"sqlite:///{(tmp_path / 'adv4.db').as_posix()}"
    registry = RegistryService(db_url=db_url, key_dir=tmp_path / "keys")
    pub = SnapshotPublisher(registry=registry, snapshot_output_path=tmp_path / "snap.dsse.json")
    env = pub.publish_snapshot()

    store = InMemorySnapshotStore(registry.keyring)
    store.load_from_envelope(env)
    enforcer = PrevalidatorEnforcer(snapshot_store=store, mode="enforce")
    prev_app = create_prevalidator_app(enforcer=enforcer, upstream_gateway_url="mock://local")
    prev_client = TestClient(prev_app)

    # Unauthenticated reload-snapshot must be rejected with 401
    r_unauth = prev_client.post("/v1/admin/reload-snapshot", json={"envelope": env})
    assert r_unauth.status_code in (401, 403)

    # Authenticated with X-POD-Control-Token succeeds
    r_auth = prev_client.post(
        "/v1/admin/reload-snapshot",
        headers={"X-POD-Control-Token": "pod-dev-control-plane-token"},
        json={"envelope": env},
    )
    assert r_auth.status_code == 200

    # Snapshot publisher /v1/publish without token must be rejected with 401
    snap_app = create_snapshot_app(publisher=pub)
    snap_client = TestClient(snap_app)
    r_snap_unauth = snap_client.post("/v1/publish")
    assert r_snap_unauth.status_code in (401, 403)

    r_snap_auth = snap_client.post(
        "/v1/publish",
        headers={"Authorization": "Bearer pod-dev-control-plane-token"},
    )
    assert r_snap_auth.status_code == 200
    registry.close()


def test_adversarial_5_key_isolation_verification_only(tmp_path: Path):
    """Test 5 (Key Isolation): Assert Keyring in verification-only mode operates purely from POD_PUBLIC_KEY_DIR with zero *.priv.pem files present."""
    priv_dir = tmp_path / "private_keys"
    pub_dir = tmp_path / "public_keys"

    signer_keyring = Keyring(private_key_dir=priv_dir, public_key_dir=pub_dir)
    signer_keyring.generate_key(algorithm="Ed25519", key_purpose=["attestation", "snapshot"])

    # Verify private keys exist ONLY in priv_dir and NEVER in pub_dir
    assert len(list(priv_dir.glob("*.priv.pem"))) == 1
    assert len(list(pub_dir.glob("*.priv.pem"))) == 0
    assert len(list(pub_dir.glob("*.pub.pem"))) == 1
    assert (pub_dir / "keyring.json").exists()

    db_url = f"sqlite:///{(tmp_path / 'adv5.db').as_posix()}"
    registry = RegistryService(db_url=db_url, key_dir=pub_dir)
    registry.keyring = signer_keyring
    pub = SnapshotPublisher(registry=registry, snapshot_output_path=tmp_path / "snap.dsse.json")
    env = pub.publish_snapshot()

    # Verification-only keyring on pub_dir verifies the DSSE snapshot with zero *.priv.pem files
    verifier_keyring = Keyring(public_key_dir=pub_dir, verification_only=True)
    ver_res = verifier_keyring.verify_dsse(env)
    assert ver_res.verified is True

    # If an attacker or misconfiguration leaks a *.priv.pem into pub_dir, verification-only Keyring fails closed
    leaked_file = pub_dir / "leaked.priv.pem"
    leaked_file.write_text("LEAKED", encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(SignatureVerificationError, match="privilege separation violation"):
        verifier_keyring.verify_dsse(env)
    leaked_file.unlink()
    registry.close()


=== FILE: D:\attestation\docs\record-format.md ===
# POD Record Format Specification (v0.1)

## 1. Envelope & Statement Structure

Every POD attestation record is an [in-toto Attestation Framework v1.0 Statement](https://github.com/in-toto/attestation) serialized as canonical UTF-8 JSON and signed inside a [Dead Simple Signing Envelope (DSSE v1)](https://github.com/secure-systems-lab/dsse).

- **Outer Envelope Schema**: `schemas/dsse-envelope.schema.json` (`https://schemas.proofofdata.dev/dsse-envelope/v0.1`)
- **Payload Type**: `application/vnd.in-toto+json`
- **in-toto `_type`**: `https://in-toto.io/Statement/v1`
- **Predicate Type URI**: `https://schemas.proofofdata.dev/attestation/v0.1`
- **Predicate Schema**: `schemas/attestation-predicate.schema.json`

### Key Invariant: Signature Outside the Predicate
Cryptographic signatures MUST reside exclusively in the outer DSSE `signatures[]` array (computed over the `DSSEv1` Pre-Authentication Encoding of `payloadType` and `payload`). Any predicate containing a `signature` or `signatures` field is rejected by schema validation.

### DSSE Pre-Authentication Encoding (PAE)
```text
DSSEv1 <byte_length(payloadType)> <payloadType> <byte_length(payload_bytes)> <payload_bytes>
```

---

## 2. The Seven Field Groups & Seven Mandatory Fields

An object missing any of the **seven mandatory fields** does not receive a record, and without a valid record it is treated as nonexistent (`UNATTESTED`) by every consumer.

| Group | Mandatory Field | Additional Fields in Group |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **1. `identity`** | `identity.locator` | `record_id`, `object_type`, `display_name`, `environment` |
| **2. `ownership`** | `ownership.owner` | `steward`, `org_unit`, `jurisdiction` |
| **3. `provenance`** | `provenance.attested_by` | `source_system`, `scanner_version`, `discovered_at`, `derived_from` |
| **4. `integrity`** | `integrity.content_sha256` | `schema_sha256`, `size_bytes`, `row_count`, `fingerprint_method` |
| **5. `classification`** | `classification.tier` | `confidence`, `sensitive_categories` (`category`, `count`, `max_confidence`, `column_or_field`), `classifier_engine` |
| **6. `handling`** | `handling.policy` (`ai_eligibility`, `minimum_clearance`, `egress_class`) | `allow_rag_indexing`, `allow_model_training`, `redaction_categories` |
| **7. `lifecycle`** | `lifecycle.expires_at` | `status`, `issued_at`, `ttl_days`, `previous_record_id`, `revoked_at`, `revocation_reason`, `tier_lower_approvals` |

> **Privacy Invariant**: `classification.sensitive_categories` records entity types and occurrence counts only â€” **never raw values or samples**.

---

## 3. Classification Tiers, Minimum Clearance, and Maximum TTLs

Eligibility is rented, never owned. Every record carries an `expires_at` timestamp bounded by its classification tier:

| Tier | Max TTL | Default `minimum_clearance` | Default `egress_class` | Default `ai_eligibility` |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Restricted** | **30 days** | `Restricted` | `local_only` | `deny` |
| **Confidential** | **90 days** | `Confidential` | `local_only` | `strip` |
| **Internal** | **180 days** | `Internal` | `private_cloud` | `allow` |
| **Public** | **365 days** | `Public` | `public_api` | `allow` |

---

## 4. Load-Bearing Lifecycle Asymmetries

1. **Raising a tier is free and immediate**: Any authorized operator or upstream lineage change can elevate an object's tier immediately, issuing a new signed record (`status: "tier_raised"`).
2. **Lowering a tier requires dual approval**: Lowering a record's tier requires at least **two distinct `approver_id` principals** recorded in `lifecycle.tier_lower_approvals` (`status: "tier_lowered"`).
3. **Changed content fingerprint triggers re-classification, not a re-stamp**: Renewing a record (`status: "renewed"`) is permitted only when the observed `content_sha256` matches the attested `content_sha256`. If the hash changes, renewal fails with `FingerprintChangedError` and the object must be re-classified.
4. **Database table fingerprinting**: Database tables are attested at the `postgres://host:port/db/schema/table` level with column-level findings inside `classification.sensitive_categories` and a deterministic `content_sha256` computed over the canonical column schema DDL digest plus sorted canonical row digests.


=== FILE: D:\attestation\docs\consumer-contract.md ===
# POD Consumer Contract Specification (v0.1)

This specification defines how any enforcement consumer (including the POD Prevalidator or a third-party AI gateway plugin) verifies and enforces POD snapshots without reading the Attestation Engine's code or querying the Registry database in the request path.

---

## 1. Snapshot Format (`https://schemas.proofofdata.dev/snapshot/v0.1`)

Consumers ingest signed point-in-time snapshots validated against `schemas/snapshot-predicate.schema.json` and wrapped in a DSSE v1 envelope (`schemas/dsse-envelope.schema.json`).

### Required Snapshot Verification Steps
When loading a snapshot, a compliant consumer MUST:
1. Verify the outer DSSE signature over the `DSSEv1` PAE using the operator's local keyring (`keyring.json`).
2. Verify that the signing key is not `revoked`, and if `rotated`, that `predicate.issued_at` falls within the key's active window.
3. Verify `statement.predicateType == "https://schemas.proofofdata.dev/snapshot/v0.1"`.
4. Verify `predicate.sequence_number >= current_highest_sequence_number` (rejecting any rollback/replay of an older snapshot).
5. Verify `now < predicate.expires_at`.

### Active Entries & Tombstones
To allow consumers to distinguish `UNATTESTED`, `EXPIRED`, and `REVOKED` states using in-memory lookups alone, `predicate.entries` maps each canonical `locator` URI to:
- `status`: `"active"` | `"expired"` | `"revoked"`
- `content_sha256`: 64-character hex SHA-256 digest
- `tier`: `"Public"` | `"Internal"` | `"Confidential"` | `"Restricted"`
- `ai_eligibility`: `"allow"` | `"strip"` | `"deny"`
- `minimum_clearance`: `"Public"` | `"Internal"` | `"Confidential"` | `"Restricted"`
- `egress_class`: `"local_only"` | `"private_cloud"` | `"public_api"`
- `record_expires_at`: ISO-8601 UTC timestamp

---

## 2. Request Binding Protocol

Callers bind LLM context segments to attested objects in two ways:

1. **Inline `<pod:object>` Content Blocks** (supports `allow`, `strip`, and `deny`):
   ```xml
   <pod:object locator="file://estate/fileshare/product_overview_public.md" sha256="e3b0c442...">
   Document text or RAG chunk content...
   </pod:object>
   ```
   - On `allow`: the consumer strips the `<pod:object ...>` and `</pod:object>` wrapper tags and forwards the inner content cleanly.
   - On `strip`: the consumer replaces the entire `<pod:object>...</pod:object>` block with `[POD:STRIPPED]` and forwards the remainder of the request.
   - On `deny`: the consumer refuses the request.

2. **Request-Level Header (`X-POD-Locators`)**:
   - Comma-separated canonical locators referenced by the request. Because header-level references lack inline boundaries, both `strip` and `deny` policies result in request refusal.

---

## 3. Enforcement Rules & Reason Codes

For every referenced locator, the consumer evaluates rules in the following order:

1. **Snapshot Staleness**: If no valid snapshot is loaded or `now >= snapshot.expires_at`, **fail closed** (refuse request).
2. **`UNATTESTED`**: Locator is absent from `snapshot.entries`, or the inline `sha256` attribute does not match `entry.content_sha256`, or fallback Presidio inspection detects untagged `Restricted` entities.
3. **`REVOKED`**: `entry.status == "revoked"`.
4. **`EXPIRED`**: `entry.status == "expired"` or `now >= entry.record_expires_at`.
5. **`INSUFFICIENT_CLEARANCE`**: Caller's verified clearance is lower than `entry.minimum_clearance`.
6. **`EGRESS_DENIED`**: Target gateway/model route `egress_class` exceeds `entry.egress_class`, or `entry.ai_eligibility == "deny"`.

---

## 4. Uniform Denial Response Requirement

In **Enforce Mode**, whenever a request is refused for *any* reason above, the consumer MUST return HTTP `403 Forbidden` with the exact uniform JSON body below, never leaking which rule fired or the classification tier of the object:

```json
{
  "error": {
    "message": "Request denied by data attestation policy.",
    "type": "pod_policy_denial",
    "code": "policy_denied"
  }
}
```

Specific reason codes (`UNATTESTED`, `EXPIRED`, `REVOKED`, `INSUFFICIENT_CLEARANCE`, `EGRESS_DENIED`) are written exclusively to the operator's internal audit log.


=== FILE: D:\attestation\docs\security-review.md ===
# Phase 4 Security Review & Threat Model Audit

Because the Registry is a map of an operator's sensitive data holdings, its access controls, key handling, and enforcement boundaries enforce the following security invariants:

## 1. Registry Access Control
- Every Registry lookup and lifecycle mutation endpoint (`/v1/records/*`, `/v1/reviews/*`) requires administrative authentication via `X-POD-Admin-Key` or `Authorization: Bearer <token>`.
- Classification findings stored in the Registry and signed into predicates contain **only entity category names and integer counts** (`category`, `count`, `max_confidence`, `column_or_field`) â€” never raw PII, secrets, or cell values. Schema validation (`validate_attestation_predicate`) actively rejects any finding entry containing `value`, `raw`, or `sample` keys.

## 2. Cryptographic Key Handling & Rotation
- Operator signing keys live strictly in mounted host/volume directories (`/var/lib/pod/keys`), never baked into container images.
- The Prevalidator mounts the keyring and snapshot directories read-only (`:ro`) and never needs access to private signing keys in production (only public verification keys in `keyring.json`).
- Key rotation (`pod keygen --rotate`) transitions the prior active key to `rotated` with a `rotated_at` timestamp. Verification (`verify_dsse_envelope`) honors records signed by a rotated key only if `issued_at <= rotated_at`, preventing backdated forgery with retired keys.
- Key revocation (`keyring.revoke_key(keyid)`) immediately invalidates all records and snapshots signed by that key.

## 3. Fail-Closed Enforcement & Anti-Oracle Design
- **Zero-DB Request Path**: The Prevalidator never queries the Registry database during LLM request processing, eliminating database DoS amplification and keeping enforcement overhead sub-millisecond.
- **Staleness & Rollback Protection**: Every snapshot carries a short `expires_at` TTL and a monotonically increasing `sequence_number`. If the snapshot expires or a replay attack attempts to load an older `sequence_number`, the Prevalidator fails closed.
- **Anti-Oracle Uniform Denials**: All client-facing 403 refusal responses return an identical error payload (`policy_denied`) so an attacker probing locators cannot distinguish whether an object is `Restricted`, `Revoked`, `Expired`, or `Unattested`.
